# Module 5a: Invoices, Payments and Statements (staff side) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Staff can bill clients by EFT. Each customer has a Pre or Post billing switch. A manager presses "Create this month's invoices" (normally on the 20th) and gets one draft per active monthly client, with first-month part amounts and suggested late fees; the owner or manager reviews, edits, deletes and issues the drafts (one at a time or all at once). Once-off invoices are made on demand from an accepted once-off quote or typed lines. Issued invoices get a number (`INV-2026-0001`), a frozen recipient and bank details, a PDF, and an email with the PDF attached. Staff record EFT payments, allocate them to invoices (oldest first by default), void invoices and payments, see reported payments (empty until stage 5b), and open statements (with a per-complex breakdown for agencies) on screen and as a PDF. The owner edits the invoice settings (bank details, once-off due days, late fee).

**Architecture:** A new core folder `packages/core/src/invoices/` holds small pure files with their own unit tests (`labels.ts`, `dates.ts`, `money.ts`, `reference.ts`, `statement-math.ts`, `schemas.ts`), the shared database helpers (`store.ts`: the store type, locks, allocated sums, audit helpers), and the staff functions (`settings.ts`, `drafts.ts`, `run.ts`, `issue.ts`, `payments.ts`, `reports.ts`, `statements.ts`, `document.ts`, `emails.ts`, `activity.ts`), each taking `(store, actor, ...)` with `assertRole` first. Functions that need their own transactions per customer or per invoice (the run, issue-all) take a `TxRunner`. Anonymise and export reach the new tables through `invoices/anonymise.ts` (no role check, imported directly by `customers/privacy.ts`, never exported from a barrel). `sendEmail` gains attachments; the invoice email template lives next to the other templates in `portal/email/templates.ts`. `@ace/ui` gains the invoice and statement screen components and PDF layouts. The staff app gains `/invoices`, `/payments`, `/payments/reports`, `/statements`, `/settings/invoices`, an Invoices tab and a Billing timing switch on the customer page, and the PDF routes. One hand-written migration adds the column, the seven tables, Row Level Security and the revokes; it adds NO grants or policies for `ace_portal` (those belong to the 5b migration).

**Tech Stack:** Next.js 16.4 (App Router, server actions, `after`), React 19, TypeScript strict (`noUncheckedIndexedAccess`), Prisma 7 with `@prisma/adapter-pg`, Supabase Postgres, zod 4, vitest 5, Tailwind 4, `@react-pdf/renderer`, Resend HTTP API (plain `fetch`).

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-27-invoices-design.md` (read all of it; it is short and binding). Stage 5b (the client portal side: portal invoice pages, the "I have paid" button, the `ace_portal` grants) is OUT of scope and gets its own plan after 5a is merged.

## Global Constraints

- Not VAT registered; money is whole cents (no floats), shown with the existing money formatting (`formatZAR`); no em dashes in any text shown on screen or printed (use commas, colons or hyphens).
- Every staff-facing core function takes (store, actor, ...) with assertRole(owner/manager) first (owner only where the spec says: invoice settings). Crew never see invoices. Do not export role-less internals from the package barrels.
- Lock order everywhere: customers (smaller id first when several), then invoices (by id), then payments, then payment reports. Unlocked read first, then lock, then re-read and re-check; status-dependent writes conditional or re-checked after the lock.
- Audit lines hold field names, ids, counts and fixed words only: never names, addresses, references, notes, amounts typed by a client, email addresses.
- Emails: every sendEmail call is wrapped in try/catch and logs only a FIXED phrase (never the error object or an address); email failure never rolls back an issue; use Next `after()` in app actions after the commit.
- Database tests run only against the DEV database in always-rolled-back transactions (`npm run test:db`, 10 to 13 minutes; testTimeout 180s, rollback transaction timeout 150s). Issuing a QUOTE (makeIssued in quotes/test-helpers.ts) takes about 10 seconds and contends on the quote_counters row: invoice tests must NOT issue quotes; use the helpers in `invoices/test-helpers.ts` that create customers, properties and ACCEPTED quotes directly through Prisma. Invoice number allocation also uses a counter row: keep each test to at most three real issues, and negative tests that make Postgres refuse a statement each run in their own withRollback (a refused statement aborts the transaction). Known flake class: statement timeouts on counter rows in files that issue numbers; re-run the failing file alone and report both results.
- Formatting: never run the root `npm run format` (Windows line-ending noise). Format only touched files with `npx prettier --write <paths>`. Stage by explicit path only (unrelated modified files exist: README.md, apps/admin/src/components/form.tsx, apps/admin/src/app/(staff)/page.tsx, customers/[id]/*.tsx, services/*, apps/admin/src/lib/*, packages/core/scripts/test-db.mjs and others: before editing or staging any file shown as modified in `git status`, run `git diff --stat <path>` and verify that only your lines appear; an empty diff means the file differs only by line endings and is safe to edit). Commit messages end with `Co-Authored-By: Claude Sonnet 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`. Commit locally, never push. Implementers NEVER apply migrations (controller task with the owner's approval) and never run `npm run test:db` (the controller runs it after each core task).
- Windows: PowerShell needs npm.cmd, Git Bash fine; do not run `npm run build` while a dev server is running. noUncheckedIndexedAccess is on; React hooks lint rules apply (no ref writes during render, no synchronous setState in an effect body). Next.js 16.4 has breaking changes: read node_modules/next/dist/docs before writing Next code (server actions, redirect, forms, route handlers, searchParams as Promise, after()); apps/admin/AGENTS.md says the same.
- SERVER COMPONENTS MUST NEVER RECEIVE OR DEFINE EVENT HANDLERS (a past runtime crash that only rendering catches): interactivity goes in `"use client"` components that import only types and `@ace/core/client` from core; native `<details>` confirm steps need no JS. Staff ids from forms are validated with safeParse and redirect with fixed notice codes (never an error page, never typed text in URLs).
- Pages work at 375px with no sideways scroll; interactive controls at least 40px high. Test data is made up only (names like "ZZ E2E ...", emails @example.com).
- Lessons from earlier reviews to apply: re-check status after locks; tests must assert real behaviour (no vacuous assertions) and audit rows must be asserted to contain no typed text; every new column must be classified in the privacy-coverage test; keep functions without role checks out of barrels; use `select`/`omit` so nothing sensitive reaches pages or exports; PDF routes: no-store headers, safe filenames, 404 for unknown ids.

Notes that apply to every task:

- `npm run check` may end red on line-ending noise in an untouched file; that one is known. Run the individual commands each task lists.
- `.db.test.ts` files compile under `npm run typecheck` but run only in the controller's `npm run test:db`. A task's database tests must be written so they pass there; the controller reports failures back.
- Run core unit tests with `cd packages/core && npx vitest run <file> && cd ../..` (Git Bash) or the PowerShell equivalent with `npx.cmd`.
- The DB test files write to the shared dev `invoice_settings` singleton only inside rolled-back transactions (the `fillBankDetails` helper).

## Decisions taken in this plan (where the spec left room)

1. **Billing timing is its own switch**, like "Bills go to": `setBillingTiming(store, actor, customerId, "PRE" | "POST")`, shown as a small radio form on the customer's new Invoices tab. It is not added to `customerSchema` (so the customer form, the CSV import and their tests are untouched). Audit `customer.billing_timing_changed` with `{ billingTiming }` (an enum word, not personal).
2. **Bank details start empty.** The seeded `invoice_settings` row has empty bank fields, `onceOffDueDays` 14 and `lateFeeCents` 10000. Issuing is refused with a plain message until the owner has entered bank name, account holder, account number and branch code. A late fee of R0 switches the late-fee suggestion off.
3. **The frozen `bankSnapshot`** holds `{ bank, business, paymentReference }`: ACE's bank details, ACE's business header (legal and trading name from organisation settings; registration number, phone, email, website from the quote wording) and the payment reference (the bill-to customer's number, `C-0123`). A draft's preview uses the live values.
4. **Bill-to is resolved again at issue** from the customer's current Managed by and Bills go to settings, so a draft created before the switch changed is addressed correctly. The draft list shows the current resolution.
5. **Recipient** frozen at issue: `recipientName` = the bill-to customer's display name; `recipientAddress` = its billing address lines joined with ", " (null when none). For an agency invoice the complex's name is printed as "For: <complex>" (live, so anonymising the complex scrubs it).
6. **Due dates.** Monthly drafts get their due date from the run (Pre: last day of the run month; Post: the 25th of the run month). Once-off drafts keep `dueDate` empty until staff set one; at issue an empty due date becomes the issue date plus `onceOffDueDays`. Issuing with a due date that has already passed is refused ("The due date has passed. Change it before issuing.").
7. **Run eligibility and skip reasons.** Candidates are customers with at least one current monthly agreement (accepted quote, `agreementEndedAt` null, a priced monthly line or a chosen optional monthly line) on a property that is not archived. Skip reasons: `alreadyInvoiced`, `notStarted` (no agreement starts by the last day of the service month), `lead`, `paused`, `archived`, `anonymised`, `failed`. A missing start date falls back to the acceptance date. The run month must be between 12 months before and 1 month after the current South African month.
8. **Pro-rata** applies per agreement when its start date is in the service month and after the 1st: `round(amount × daysFromStart / daysInMonth)` (half a cent rounds up), with " (part month: 22 of 31 days from 10 Dec)" added to the line description. A Pre client whose service started before the service month is NOT charged for the earlier part month automatically; the draft list's "First month" warning (no earlier non-void monthly invoice for this customer) tells the owner to check, and a once-off invoice or an added line covers it.
9. **Line descriptions** from the run are `"<property label>: <quote line wording>"`, cut to 300 characters. Late-fee lines read `"Late payment fee: INV-2026-0001"` and carry `forInvoiceId`. Staff can add lines of kind Service, Adjustment (the only kind that may be negative) or Once-off work; existing lines keep their kind.
10. **Anonymise** (financial records kept): the customer's DRAFT invoices (as work customer or bill-to) are deleted; on its issued and void invoices `recipientName`, `recipientAddress` and `voidReason` are cleared (also where it is the bill-to), and every line description of invoices for this customer is replaced by a fixed word per kind ("Monthly service", "Optional extra", "Late payment fee", "Adjustment", "Once-off work") because run lines carry the property label and staff-typed lines may carry anything; payment `reference`, `note` and `voidReason` where it is the payer, and report `reference` on its invoices, are cleared. Numbers, dates, amounts, totals, allocations, the snapshot and the fingerprint stay.
11. **Export** adds the customer's invoices (as work customer and as bill-to) with lines and allocations, its payments with allocations, and the payment reports on its invoices. The bank snapshot is left out (it is ACE's details, not the customer's). Personal fields are included while they exist (it is the "what do you hold about me" export).
12. **Balances and statements use current allocations.** Allocations have no date of their own; a statement for an earlier period shows outstanding balances and credit as they stand now. A voided invoice is left out of statements altogether; a voided payment never counts.
13. **Emails.** Invoices issued together are grouped by bill-to customer: each email address of the bill-to customer's contacts who receive invoices gets one email with every PDF of that group attached. `emailStatus` becomes SENT when at least one address was sent to, otherwise FAILED (also when nobody receives invoices, audit reason `no_recipient`). Resend email is a button on an issued invoice. Attachments: at most 20 files, 15 MB in total, file names matching `^[A-Za-z0-9._-]{1,100}$`.
14. **Payments.** Received date between 2020-01-01 and today; amount R0.01 to R1 000 000; reference up to 100 characters, note up to 500. Allocations go only to ISSUED invoices billed to the payer, are at most the payment's unallocated amount and at most the invoice's balance; allocating again to the same invoice adds to the existing allocation. A voided payment keeps its allocation rows as history (they stop counting) and cannot be allocated or unallocated. The "involved customer" for locks is the payer (every allocated invoice is billed to it).
15. **Void.** Only ISSUED invoices can be voided (a draft is deleted instead), with a reason of 1 to 300 characters, and only when no allocation from a non-voided payment remains. A void reason is scrubbed on anonymise.
16. **Invoice and payment locks** use `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE` on the row (no `updatedAt` column is added to these tables); customers keep the existing `lockCustomer`.
17. **Audit entity types:** `invoice` (drafted, deleted_draft, issued, voided, emailed, email_failed, payment_allocated, payment_unallocated, report_confirmed, report_dismissed), `payment` (recorded, allocated, unallocated, voided), `invoice_settings` (updated, with changed field names only). Draft saves are not audited (like quote drafts).
18. **The DB test run** is made serial (`--no-file-parallelism`, Task 3), because every file that issues a number holds the counter row lock for the whole rolled-back test transaction; the controller measures the run time before and after and may set `ACE_DB_TEST_WORKERS=2` if serial is much slower.
19. **Once-off from a quote** is refused when a non-void invoice already came from that quote ("This quote has already been invoiced."), to prevent double billing; free-line once-off invoices are always possible.
20. **Payment reports** are shown on the invoice page and on `/payments/reports`. Confirm opens `/payments/new?report=<id>` pre-filled; recording that payment marks the report CONFIRMED in the same transaction. Dismiss marks it DISMISSED. 5a never creates reports (5b does); DB tests create them directly.

### Task 1: Database change: billing timing, invoice tables, payments and reports (not applied)

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/core/prisma/schema.prisma`
- Create: `packages/core/prisma/migrations/20261127120000_invoices/migration.sql`
- Modify: `packages/core/src/customers/privacy-coverage.test.ts` (classify every new column and table)
- Create: `packages/core/src/invoices/db-protection.db.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (Prisma, after `db:generate`): enum `BillingTiming` (`PRE | POST`); `Customer.billingTiming BillingTiming` (default `PRE`) with relations `Customer.invoices` (`"InvoiceCustomer"`), `Customer.billedInvoices` (`"InvoiceBillTo"`), `Customer.payments`; `Contact.paymentReports`; `Property.invoiceLines`; `Quote.invoices`; `QuoteLine.invoiceLines`; enums `InvoiceKind` (`MONTHLY | ONCE_OFF`), `InvoiceStatus` (`DRAFT | ISSUED | VOID`), `InvoiceLineKind` (`SERVICE | OPTIONAL | LATE_FEE | ADJUSTMENT | ONCE_OFF`), `InvoiceEmailStatus` (`NONE | SENT | FAILED`), `PaymentReportStatus` (`WAITING | CONFIRMED | DISMISSED`); models and delegates `InvoiceSettings` (`invoiceSettings`), `InvoiceCounter` (`invoiceCounter`), `Invoice` (`invoice`; relations `customer`, `billTo`, `sourceQuote`, `lines`, `lateFeeLines`, `allocations`, `reports`), `InvoiceLine` (`invoiceLine`; relations `invoice`, `property`, `sourceQuoteLine`, `forInvoice`), `Payment` (`payment`; relations `payer`, `allocations`), `PaymentAllocation` (`paymentAllocation`; relations `payment`, `invoice`), `PaymentReport` (`paymentReport`; relations `invoice`, `reportedByContact`).
- Database: the partial unique index `invoices_monthly_once_per_month` and the CHECK constraints named below.

The database tests written in this task cannot pass until the controller applies the migration (Task 2). Do NOT run `npm run test:db`, `db:deploy` or `db:migrate`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Edit `schema.prisma`**

Header comment: replace the line `// Modules 0 to 4: organisation settings, staff users, audit log, customers, contacts, properties,` and the next line with:

```prisma
// Modules 0 to 5a: organisation settings, staff users, audit log, customers, contacts, properties,
// services, quotes, client link, client portal, invoices, payments. Jobs arrive in Module 6, and so on.
```

Add this enum directly above `/// The account quotes and invoices go to. Archived, never deleted.`:

```prisma
/// Module 5: when a monthly client is billed. PRE: the run in month M bills service month M+1, due the last
/// day of M. POST: the run in month M bills service month M, due the 25th of M.
enum BillingTiming {
  PRE
  POST
}
```

In `model Customer`, after the `billsToManager` line, add:

```prisma
  /// Module 5: Pre or Post billing for the monthly run. Default Pre for every customer.
  billingTiming       BillingTiming   @default(PRE)
```

and in its relation block, after `managedCustomers Customer[] @relation("CustomerManagement")`:

```prisma

  invoices       Invoice[] @relation("InvoiceCustomer")
  billedInvoices Invoice[] @relation("InvoiceBillTo")
  payments       Payment[]
```

In `model Contact`, after `acceptedQuotes Quote[] @relation("QuoteAcceptedByContact")`, add:

```prisma
  paymentReports PaymentReport[]
```

In `model Property`, after `quotes   Quote[]`, add:

```prisma
  invoiceLines InvoiceLine[]
```

In `model Quote`, after `acceptedByContact Contact? @relation(...)` (the last relation line), add:

```prisma
  invoices          Invoice[]
```

In `model QuoteLine`, after `quote Quote @relation(fields: [quoteId], references: [id])`, add:

```prisma
  invoiceLines InvoiceLine[]
```

Append at the end of the file:

```prisma
enum InvoiceKind {
  MONTHLY
  ONCE_OFF
}

/// Only these three are stored. Unpaid, part paid, paid and overdue are worked out from the balance.
enum InvoiceStatus {
  DRAFT
  ISSUED
  VOID
}

enum InvoiceLineKind {
  SERVICE
  OPTIONAL
  LATE_FEE
  ADJUSTMENT
  ONCE_OFF
}

enum InvoiceEmailStatus {
  NONE
  SENT
  FAILED
}

enum PaymentReportStatus {
  WAITING
  CONFIRMED
  DISMISSED
}

/// Module 5: exactly one row (id = "singleton"), edited by the owner. Issued invoices keep their own frozen
/// copy of the bank details (Invoice.bankSnapshot). Bank details start empty: issuing is refused until set.
model InvoiceSettings {
  id             String   @id @default("singleton")
  bankName       String   @default("")
  accountHolder  String   @default("")
  accountNumber  String   @default("")
  branchCode     String   @default("")
  accountType    String   @default("")
  /// Once-off invoices: due this many days after the issue date unless staff set a due date.
  onceOffDueDays Int      @default(14)
  /// The suggested late payment fee in whole cents (0 = no suggestion).
  lateFeeCents   Int      @default(10000)
  updatedAt      DateTime @updatedAt
  updatedById    String?

  @@map("invoice_settings")
}

/// Module 5: the last invoice number given out in each year (South African year). Taken with one atomic statement.
model InvoiceCounter {
  year    Int @id
  lastSeq Int

  @@map("invoice_counters")
}

/// Module 5: one invoice. `customerId` is the client the work is for, `billToCustomerId` the payer (the agency
/// when the complex's bills go to it). Drafts have no number; issuing freezes the recipient, bank details and total.
model Invoice {
  id               String             @id @default(uuid()) @db.Uuid
  customerId       String             @db.Uuid
  billToCustomerId String             @db.Uuid
  kind             InvoiceKind
  /// First day of the month the service is for. Monthly invoices only.
  serviceMonth     DateTime?          @db.Date
  status           InvoiceStatus      @default(DRAFT)
  /// INV-<numberYear>-<numberSeq>, null while a draft.
  numberYear       Int?
  numberSeq        Int?
  dueDate          DateTime?          @db.Date
  issuedAt         DateTime?
  issuedById       String?
  createdAt        DateTime           @default(now())
  createdById      String?
  /// Raised on every draft save; guards against two devices editing one draft.
  draftVersion     Int                @default(1)
  sourceQuoteId    String?            @db.Uuid
  /// Set at issue: the sum of the line amounts.
  totalCents       Int?
  /// Frozen at issue. Personal data: scrubbed on anonymise.
  recipientName    String?
  recipientAddress String?
  /// Frozen at issue: { bank, business, paymentReference }. ACE's own details only.
  bankSnapshot     Json?
  /// SHA-256 of the canonical content at issue.
  contentHash      String?
  emailStatus      InvoiceEmailStatus @default(NONE)
  emailedAt        DateTime?
  voidedAt         DateTime?
  /// Typed by staff. Scrubbed on anonymise.
  voidReason       String?
  voidedById       String?

  customer     Customer            @relation("InvoiceCustomer", fields: [customerId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  billTo       Customer            @relation("InvoiceBillTo", fields: [billToCustomerId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  sourceQuote  Quote?              @relation(fields: [sourceQuoteId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)
  lines        InvoiceLine[]       @relation("InvoiceLines")
  lateFeeLines InvoiceLine[]       @relation("LateFeeFor")
  allocations  PaymentAllocation[]
  reports      PaymentReport[]

  @@unique([numberYear, numberSeq])
  @@index([customerId])
  @@index([billToCustomerId])
  @@index([status])
  @@map("invoices")
}

/// Module 5: one line of an invoice. Amounts are whole cents and never zero; only ADJUSTMENT may be negative.
model InvoiceLine {
  id                String          @id @default(uuid()) @db.Uuid
  invoiceId         String          @db.Uuid
  sortOrder         Int
  propertyId        String?         @db.Uuid
  kind              InvoiceLineKind
  /// Replaced by a fixed word per kind on anonymise (run lines carry the property label).
  description       String
  amountCents       Int
  sourceQuoteLineId String?         @db.Uuid
  /// LATE_FEE lines: the overdue invoice the fee is charged against.
  forInvoiceId      String?         @db.Uuid

  invoice         Invoice    @relation("InvoiceLines", fields: [invoiceId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  property        Property?  @relation(fields: [propertyId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)
  sourceQuoteLine QuoteLine? @relation(fields: [sourceQuoteLineId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)
  forInvoice      Invoice?   @relation("LateFeeFor", fields: [forInvoiceId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  @@index([invoiceId])
  @@index([forInvoiceId])
  @@map("invoice_lines")
}

/// Module 5: money received by EFT. A voided payment keeps its allocation rows as history; they stop counting.
model Payment {
  id              String    @id @default(uuid()) @db.Uuid
  payerCustomerId String    @db.Uuid
  receivedOn      DateTime  @db.Date
  amountCents     Int
  /// The bank reference as typed by staff. Scrubbed on anonymise.
  reference       String?
  /// Scrubbed on anonymise.
  note            String?
  recordedById    String
  createdAt       DateTime  @default(now())
  voidedAt        DateTime?
  voidReason      String?
  voidedById      String?

  payer       Customer            @relation(fields: [payerCustomerId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  allocations PaymentAllocation[]

  @@index([payerCustomerId])
  @@map("payments")
}

/// Module 5: part (or all) of a payment applied to one invoice.
model PaymentAllocation {
  id          String @id @default(uuid()) @db.Uuid
  paymentId   String @db.Uuid
  invoiceId   String @db.Uuid
  amountCents Int

  payment Payment @relation(fields: [paymentId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  invoice Invoice @relation(fields: [invoiceId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  @@unique([paymentId, invoiceId])
  @@index([invoiceId])
  @@map("payment_allocations")
}

/// Module 5: a client's "I have paid" (written by the portal in stage 5b; staff confirm or dismiss).
model PaymentReport {
  id                  String              @id @default(uuid()) @db.Uuid
  invoiceId           String              @db.Uuid
  reportedByContactId String?             @db.Uuid
  paidOn              DateTime            @db.Date
  /// The client's bank reference as typed. Personal data: scrubbed on anonymise.
  reference           String?
  status              PaymentReportStatus @default(WAITING)
  createdAt           DateTime            @default(now())
  resolvedAt          DateTime?
  resolvedById        String?

  invoice           Invoice  @relation(fields: [invoiceId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  reportedByContact Contact? @relation(fields: [reportedByContactId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)

  @@index([invoiceId])
  @@index([status])
  @@map("payment_reports")
}
```

Run in `packages/core`: `npm run db:validate` then `npm run db:generate`. Expected: both succeed. (Use `npx prisma format` only if the file is misaligned; it only re-spaces.)

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Protections (same rules as the earlier migrations). No rights for ace_portal here: the 5b migration
-- adds exactly the columns the portal needs, frozen by portal-role.db.test.ts.
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
ALTER TABLE "invoice_settings" ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE "invoice_counters" ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE "invoices" ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE "invoice_lines" ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE "payments" ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE "payment_allocations" ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE "payment_reports" ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

DO $$
DECLARE r text;
BEGIN
  FOREACH r IN ARRAY ARRAY['anon', 'authenticated'] LOOP
    IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname = r) THEN
      EXECUTE format('REVOKE ALL ON TABLE "invoice_settings", "invoice_counters", "invoices", "invoice_lines", "payments", "payment_allocations", "payment_reports" FROM %I', r);
    END IF;
  END LOOP;
END $$;

ALTER TABLE "invoice_settings"
  ADD CONSTRAINT "invoice_settings_singleton" CHECK ("id" = 'singleton');
ALTER TABLE "invoice_settings"
  ADD CONSTRAINT "invoice_settings_due_days_range" CHECK ("onceOffDueDays" BETWEEN 0 AND 90);
ALTER TABLE "invoice_settings"
  ADD CONSTRAINT "invoice_settings_late_fee_range" CHECK ("lateFeeCents" BETWEEN 0 AND 10000000);

ALTER TABLE "invoice_counters"
  ADD CONSTRAINT "invoice_counters_seq_positive" CHECK ("lastSeq" >= 1);

-- A draft has no number; an issued or void invoice has one, plus its due date, issue date and total.
ALTER TABLE "invoices"
  ADD CONSTRAINT "invoices_number_iff_not_draft"
  CHECK (("status" = 'DRAFT') = ("numberYear" IS NULL AND "numberSeq" IS NULL));
ALTER TABLE "invoices"
  ADD CONSTRAINT "invoices_number_pair" CHECK (("numberYear" IS NULL) = ("numberSeq" IS NULL));
ALTER TABLE "invoices"
  ADD CONSTRAINT "invoices_issued_complete"
  CHECK ("status" = 'DRAFT' OR ("dueDate" IS NOT NULL AND "issuedAt" IS NOT NULL AND "totalCents" IS NOT NULL));
ALTER TABLE "invoices"
  ADD CONSTRAINT "invoices_total_positive" CHECK ("totalCents" IS NULL OR "totalCents" > 0);
ALTER TABLE "invoices"
  ADD CONSTRAINT "invoices_service_month_iff_monthly" CHECK (("kind" = 'MONTHLY') = ("serviceMonth" IS NOT NULL));
ALTER TABLE "invoices"
  ADD CONSTRAINT "invoices_service_month_first_day"
  CHECK ("serviceMonth" IS NULL OR EXTRACT(DAY FROM "serviceMonth") = 1);
ALTER TABLE "invoices"
  ADD CONSTRAINT "invoices_void_iff_voided" CHECK (("status" = 'VOID') = ("voidedAt" IS NOT NULL));
ALTER TABLE "invoices"
  ADD CONSTRAINT "invoices_draft_version_positive" CHECK ("draftVersion" >= 1);

-- The monthly run is idempotent: one live (draft or issued) monthly invoice per customer and service month.
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX "invoices_monthly_once_per_month" ON "invoices" ("customerId", "serviceMonth")
  WHERE "kind" = 'MONTHLY' AND "status" <> 'VOID';

ALTER TABLE "invoice_lines"
  ADD CONSTRAINT "invoice_lines_amount_not_zero" CHECK ("amountCents" <> 0);
ALTER TABLE "invoice_lines"
  ADD CONSTRAINT "invoice_lines_negative_only_adjustment" CHECK ("amountCents" > 0 OR "kind" = 'ADJUSTMENT');
ALTER TABLE "invoice_lines"
  ADD CONSTRAINT "invoice_lines_late_fee_target" CHECK ("kind" <> 'LATE_FEE' OR "forInvoiceId" IS NOT NULL);
ALTER TABLE "invoice_lines"
  ADD CONSTRAINT "invoice_lines_description_length" CHECK (char_length("description") BETWEEN 1 AND 300);

ALTER TABLE "payments"
  ADD CONSTRAINT "payments_amount_positive" CHECK ("amountCents" > 0);
ALTER TABLE "payment_allocations"
  ADD CONSTRAINT "payment_allocations_amount_positive" CHECK ("amountCents" > 0);
ALTER TABLE "payment_reports"
  ADD CONSTRAINT "payment_reports_resolved_iff_not_waiting" CHECK (("status" = 'WAITING') = ("resolvedAt" IS NULL));

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- The settings row (bank details are entered by the owner in the app before the first issue)
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
INSERT INTO "invoice_settings" ("id", "onceOffDueDays", "lateFeeCents", "updatedAt")
VALUES ('singleton', 14, 10000, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP);
```

Before committing, cross-check every column and constraint name in the SQL against `schema.prisma` (Prisma's default names are `<table>_<column>_fkey`, `<table>_<column>_idx`, `<table>_<a>_<b>_key`). If one differs, fix the SQL, not the schema. The migration contains no `GRANT` and no `CREATE POLICY`.

Add these tables above `function check`:

```ts
// Module 5. Financial records are kept on anonymise (numbers, dates, amounts, allocations); the frozen
// recipient, typed references, notes and reasons are scrubbed, and line wording is replaced by a fixed word.
const INVOICE: Record<string, Rule> = {
  id: "kept: identifier",
  customerId: "kept: identifier",
  billToCustomerId: "kept: identifier",
  kind: "kept: enum (monthly or once-off)",
  serviceMonth: "kept: month the service was for",
  status: "kept: enum, not personal",
  numberYear: "kept: part of the invoice number",
  numberSeq: "kept: part of the invoice number",
  dueDate: "kept: date the invoice was due",
  issuedAt: "kept: timestamp",
  issuedById: "kept: staff id, not about the customer",
  createdAt: "kept: timestamp",
  createdById: "kept: staff id, not about the customer",
  draftVersion: "kept: whole number",
  sourceQuoteId: "kept: identifier",
  totalCents: "kept: money, the accounts must still add up",
  recipientName: "scrubbed",
  recipientAddress: "scrubbed",
  bankSnapshot: "kept: ACE's own bank and business details, nothing about the customer",
  contentHash: "kept: fingerprint, cannot be turned back into the content",
  emailStatus: "kept: enum (none, sent, failed)",
  emailedAt: "kept: timestamp",
  voidedAt: "kept: timestamp",
  voidReason: "scrubbed",
  voidedById: "kept: staff id, not about the customer",
};

const INVOICE_LINE: Record<string, Rule> = {
  id: "kept: identifier",
  invoiceId: "kept: identifier",
  sortOrder: "kept: whole number",
  propertyId: "kept: identifier (the property row itself is scrubbed)",
  kind: "kept: enum (service, late fee...)",
  description: "scrubbed",
  amountCents: "kept: money, the accounts must still add up",
  sourceQuoteLineId: "kept: identifier",
  forInvoiceId: "kept: identifier",
};

const PAYMENT: Record<string, Rule> = {
  id: "kept: identifier",
  payerCustomerId: "kept: identifier",
  receivedOn: "kept: date the money arrived",
  amountCents: "kept: money, the accounts must still add up",
  reference: "scrubbed",
  note: "scrubbed",
  recordedById: "kept: staff id, not about the customer",
  createdAt: "kept: timestamp",
  voidedAt: "kept: timestamp",
  voidReason: "scrubbed",
  voidedById: "kept: staff id, not about the customer",
};

const PAYMENT_ALLOCATION: Record<string, Rule> = {
  id: "kept: identifier",
  paymentId: "kept: identifier",
  invoiceId: "kept: identifier",
  amountCents: "kept: money, the accounts must still add up",
};

const PAYMENT_REPORT: Record<string, Rule> = {
  id: "kept: identifier",
  invoiceId: "kept: identifier",
  reportedByContactId: "kept: identifier (the contact row itself is scrubbed)",
  paidOn: "kept: date the client said they paid",
  reference: "scrubbed",
  status: "kept: enum (waiting, confirmed, dismissed)",
  createdAt: "kept: timestamp",
  resolvedAt: "kept: timestamp",
  resolvedById: "kept: staff id, not about the customer",
};

// ACE's own settings and counters: nothing about any customer.
const INVOICE_SETTINGS: Record<string, Rule> = {
  id: "kept: fixed singleton key",
  bankName: "kept: ACE's own bank details",
  accountHolder: "kept: ACE's own bank details",
  accountNumber: "kept: ACE's own bank details",
  branchCode: "kept: ACE's own bank details",
  accountType: "kept: ACE's own bank details",
  onceOffDueDays: "kept: whole number setting",
  lateFeeCents: "kept: money setting",
  updatedAt: "kept: timestamp",
  updatedById: "kept: staff id, not about the customer",
};

const INVOICE_COUNTER: Record<string, Rule> = {
  year: "kept: year number",
  lastSeq: "kept: whole number",
};
```

and in the final `describe`, after the portal sessions line:

  check("invoice lines", Prisma.InvoiceLineScalarFieldEnum, INVOICE_LINE);
  check("payments", Prisma.PaymentScalarFieldEnum, PAYMENT);
  check("payment allocations", Prisma.PaymentAllocationScalarFieldEnum, PAYMENT_ALLOCATION);
  check("payment reports", Prisma.PaymentReportScalarFieldEnum, PAYMENT_REPORT);
  check("invoice settings", Prisma.InvoiceSettingsScalarFieldEnum, INVOICE_SETTINGS);
  check("invoice counters", Prisma.InvoiceCounterScalarFieldEnum, INVOICE_COUNTER);
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `packages/core/src/invoices/db-protection.db.test.ts`**

```ts
import { randomInt } from "node:crypto";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { Prisma } from "../generated/prisma/client";
import { dbTestsEnabled, withRollback } from "../testing/rollback";

const TABLES = [
  "invoice_settings",
  "invoice_counters",
  "invoices",
  "invoice_lines",
  "payments",
  "payment_allocations",
  "payment_reports",
];

type Tx = Prisma.TransactionClient;
const day = (value: string) => new Date(`${value}T00:00:00.000Z`);

async function customer(tx: Tx): Promise<string> {
  const c = await tx.customer.create({
    data: { type: "COMPANY", displayName: "ZZ E2E Invoice Protection" },
    select: { id: true },
  });
  return c.id;
}

async function draft(tx: Tx, customerId: string, data: Partial<Prisma.InvoiceUncheckedCreateInput> = {}) {
  return tx.invoice.create({
    data: { customerId, billToCustomerId: customerId, kind: "ONCE_OFF", ...data },
    select: { id: true },
  });
}

async function issued(tx: Tx, customerId: string, data: Partial<Prisma.InvoiceUncheckedCreateInput> = {}) {
  return draft(tx, customerId, {
    status: "ISSUED",
    numberYear: 2099,
    numberSeq: randomInt(1, 2_000_000_000),
    dueDate: day("2099-01-31"),
    issuedAt: new Date(),
    totalCents: 1000,
    ...data,
  });
}

describe.skipIf(!dbTestsEnabled)("invoice database protections", () => {
  it("has row level security on for every invoice table", async () => {
    await withRollback(async (tx) => {
      const rls = await tx.$queryRaw<{ relname: string; relrowsecurity: boolean }[]>`
        select relname, relrowsecurity from pg_class
        where relname in ('invoice_settings', 'invoice_counters', 'invoices', 'invoice_lines',
                          'payments', 'payment_allocations', 'payment_reports')`;
      expect(rls.map((r) => r.relname).sort()).toEqual([...TABLES].sort());
      for (const row of rls) expect(row.relrowsecurity, row.relname).toBe(true);
    });
  });

  it("gives the public roles no access, and gives the portal role nothing yet", async () => {
    await withRollback(async (tx) => {
      const roles = await tx.$queryRaw<{ rolname: string }[]>`
        select rolname from pg_roles where rolname in ('anon', 'authenticated', 'ace_portal')`;
      expect(roles.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      for (const { rolname } of roles) {
        for (const table of TABLES) {
          for (const p of ["select", "insert", "update", "delete"]) {
            const [row] = await tx.$queryRaw<{ allowed: boolean }[]>`
              select has_table_privilege(${rolname}, ${table}, ${p}) as allowed`;
            expect(row?.allowed, `${rolname} ${p} on ${table}`).toBe(false);
          }
        }
      }
      const [col] = await tx.$queryRaw<{ allowed: boolean }[]>`
        select has_column_privilege('ace_portal', 'customers', 'billingTiming', 'select') as allowed`;
      expect(col?.allowed).toBe(false);
    });
  });

  it("blocks the public API from reading invoices and payments", async () => {
    const url = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL;
    const key = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY;
    expect(url && key).toBeTruthy();
    for (const table of ["invoices", "payments", "invoice_settings"]) {
      const res = await fetch(`${url}/rest/v1/${table}?select=*`, {
        headers: { apikey: key!, Authorization: `Bearer ${key}` },
      });
      expect([401, 403, 404], table).toContain(res.status);
    }
  });

  it("seeds the settings row with empty bank details, 14 due days and a R100 late fee", async () => {
    await withRollback(async (tx) => {
      const row = await tx.invoiceSettings.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: "singleton" } });
      expect(row.onceOffDueDays).toBe(14);
      expect(row.lateFeeCents).toBe(10000);
    });
  });

  it("gives every customer Pre billing by default", async () => {
    await withRollback(async (tx) => {
      const id = await customer(tx);
      const row = await tx.customer.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id }, select: { billingTiming: true } });
      expect(row.billingTiming).toBe("PRE");
    });
  });

  it("rejects a second settings row", async () => {
    await expect(
      withRollback((tx) => tx.invoiceSettings.create({ data: { id: "other" } })),
    ).rejects.toThrow(/invoice_settings_singleton/);
  });

  it("rejects a once-off due days setting above 90", async () => {
    await expect(
      withRollback((tx) =>
        tx.invoiceSettings.update({ where: { id: "singleton" }, data: { onceOffDueDays: 91 } }),
      ),
    ).rejects.toThrow(/invoice_settings_due_days_range/);
  });

  it("rejects a draft that has a number", async () => {
    await expect(
      withRollback(async (tx) => {
        await draft(tx, await customer(tx), { numberYear: 2099, numberSeq: 1 });
      }),
    ).rejects.toThrow(/invoices_number_iff_not_draft/);
  });

  it("rejects an issued invoice without a total", async () => {
    await expect(
      withRollback(async (tx) => {
        await issued(tx, await customer(tx), { totalCents: null });
      }),
    ).rejects.toThrow(/invoices_issued_complete/);
  });

  it("rejects a total of zero", async () => {
    await expect(
      withRollback(async (tx) => {
        await issued(tx, await customer(tx), { totalCents: 0 });
      }),
    ).rejects.toThrow(/invoices_total_positive/);
  });

  it("rejects a monthly invoice without a service month", async () => {
    await expect(
      withRollback(async (tx) => {
        await draft(tx, await customer(tx), { kind: "MONTHLY" });
      }),
    ).rejects.toThrow(/invoices_service_month_iff_monthly/);
  });

  it("rejects a service month that is not the first of the month", async () => {
    await expect(
      withRollback(async (tx) => {
        await draft(tx, await customer(tx), { kind: "MONTHLY", serviceMonth: day("2099-03-15") });
      }),
    ).rejects.toThrow(/invoices_service_month_first_day/);
  });

  it("rejects a void invoice without a void date", async () => {
    await expect(
      withRollback(async (tx) => {
        await issued(tx, await customer(tx), { status: "VOID" });
      }),
    ).rejects.toThrow(/invoices_void_iff_voided/);
  });

  it("allows only one live monthly invoice per customer and service month", async () => {
    await expect(
      withRollback(async (tx) => {
        const id = await customer(tx);
        const month = { kind: "MONTHLY" as const, serviceMonth: day("2099-04-01") };
        await draft(tx, id, month);
        await draft(tx, id, month);
      }),
    ).rejects.toThrow(/invoices_monthly_once_per_month|Unique constraint/);
  });

  it("allows a new monthly invoice for the month when the earlier one is void", async () => {
    await withRollback(async (tx) => {
      const id = await customer(tx);
      const month = { kind: "MONTHLY" as const, serviceMonth: day("2099-05-01") };
      await issued(tx, id, { ...month, status: "VOID", voidedAt: new Date() });
      const second = await draft(tx, id, month);
      expect(second.id).toBeTruthy();
    });
  });

  it("rejects a line of zero", async () => {
    await expect(
      withRollback(async (tx) => {
        const inv = await draft(tx, await customer(tx));
        await tx.invoiceLine.create({
          data: { invoiceId: inv.id, sortOrder: 1, kind: "ONCE_OFF", description: "x", amountCents: 0 },
        });
      }),
    ).rejects.toThrow(/invoice_lines_amount_not_zero/);
  });

  it("rejects a negative line that is not an adjustment", async () => {
    await expect(
      withRollback(async (tx) => {
        const inv = await draft(tx, await customer(tx));
        await tx.invoiceLine.create({
          data: { invoiceId: inv.id, sortOrder: 1, kind: "SERVICE", description: "x", amountCents: -100 },
        });
      }),
    ).rejects.toThrow(/invoice_lines_negative_only_adjustment/);
  });

  it("allows a negative adjustment", async () => {
    await withRollback(async (tx) => {
      const inv = await draft(tx, await customer(tx));
      const line = await tx.invoiceLine.create({
        data: { invoiceId: inv.id, sortOrder: 1, kind: "ADJUSTMENT", description: "Discount", amountCents: -500 },
        select: { amountCents: true },
      });
      expect(line.amountCents).toBe(-500);
    });
  });

  it("rejects a late fee line that names no invoice", async () => {
    await expect(
      withRollback(async (tx) => {
        const inv = await draft(tx, await customer(tx));
        await tx.invoiceLine.create({
          data: { invoiceId: inv.id, sortOrder: 1, kind: "LATE_FEE", description: "Late payment fee", amountCents: 10000 },
        });
      }),
    ).rejects.toThrow(/invoice_lines_late_fee_target/);
  });

  it("rejects an empty line description", async () => {
    await expect(
      withRollback(async (tx) => {
        const inv = await draft(tx, await customer(tx));
        await tx.invoiceLine.create({
          data: { invoiceId: inv.id, sortOrder: 1, kind: "ONCE_OFF", description: "", amountCents: 100 },
        });
      }),
    ).rejects.toThrow(/invoice_lines_description_length/);
  });

  it("rejects a payment of zero", async () => {
    await expect(
      withRollback(async (tx) => {
        await tx.payment.create({
          data: {
            payerCustomerId: await customer(tx),
            receivedOn: day("2099-01-01"),
            amountCents: 0,
            recordedById: "staff",
          },
        });
      }),
    ).rejects.toThrow(/payments_amount_positive/);
  });

  it("rejects an allocation of zero", async () => {
    await expect(
      withRollback(async (tx) => {
        const id = await customer(tx);
        const inv = await issued(tx, id);
        const pay = await tx.payment.create({
          data: { payerCustomerId: id, receivedOn: day("2099-01-01"), amountCents: 100, recordedById: "staff" },
          select: { id: true },
        });
        await tx.paymentAllocation.create({ data: { paymentId: pay.id, invoiceId: inv.id, amountCents: 0 } });
      }),
    ).rejects.toThrow(/payment_allocations_amount_positive/);
  });

  it("rejects a resolved payment report without a resolved date", async () => {
    await expect(
      withRollback(async (tx) => {
        const inv = await issued(tx, await customer(tx));
        await tx.paymentReport.create({
          data: { invoiceId: inv.id, paidOn: day("2099-01-01"), status: "CONFIRMED" },
        });
      }),
    ).rejects.toThrow(/payment_reports_resolved_iff_not_waiting/);
  });

  it("rejects an invoice counter below 1", async () => {
    await expect(
      withRollback((tx) => tx.invoiceCounter.create({ data: { year: 2098, lastSeq: 0 } })),
    ).rejects.toThrow(/invoice_counters_seq_positive/);
  });
});
```

### Task 3: Run the database test files one after another (small infrastructure change)

Why: every test file that issues a quote (and, from now on, an invoice) takes the counter row lock inside its rolled-back transaction and holds it until the test ends, so files running in parallel queue on that one row and hit statement timeouts. Running the files one after another removes the queue. The controller measures the total time before (Task 2, Step 4) and after (Step 3 below).

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/core/scripts/test-db.mjs` (shown as modified in `git status` by line endings only: run `git diff --stat packages/core/scripts/test-db.mjs` first and continue only if it prints nothing)

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `npm run test:db` runs the `.db.test.ts` files one at a time by default. Setting `ACE_DB_TEST_WORKERS` to a whole number from 2 to 8 runs that many files at once instead (for measuring).

- [ ] **Step 1: Replace the `spawnSync` call** in `packages/core/scripts/test-db.mjs`. Replace:

```js
const result = spawnSync(process.execPath, [vitestBin, "run", ".db.test.ts"], {
```

with:

```js
// Files that issue quote or invoice numbers hold the counter row lock for a whole (rolled-back) test,
// so parallel files queue on that row and time out. One file at a time by default; ACE_DB_TEST_WORKERS
// (2 to 8) runs that many files at once, for measuring.
const workers = Number(process.env.ACE_DB_TEST_WORKERS ?? "1");
const parallel =
  Number.isInteger(workers) && workers >= 2 && workers <= 8
    ? [`--maxWorkers=${workers}`]
    : ["--no-file-parallelism"];

const result = spawnSync(process.execPath, [vitestBin, "run", ".db.test.ts", ...parallel], {
```

git add packages/core/scripts/test-db.mjs
git commit -m "Run the database test files one at a time to stop counter row timeouts

Co-Authored-By: Claude Sonnet 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>"

If the serial run is more than twice as slow as the parallel run AND had no failures in the parallel run either, try `ACE_DB_TEST_WORKERS=2 npm run test:db` once and keep whichever is reliable; record the choice in ARCHITECTURE.md in Task 17.

```ts
export const BILLING_TIMINGS = ["PRE", "POST"] as const;
export type BillingTiming = (typeof BILLING_TIMINGS)[number];
export const BILLING_TIMING_LABELS: Record<BillingTiming, string> = {
  PRE: "Pre: billed in advance",
  POST: "Post: billed in the month of the service",
};

export const INVOICE_LINE_KINDS = ["SERVICE", "OPTIONAL", "LATE_FEE", "ADJUSTMENT", "ONCE_OFF"] as const;
export type InvoiceLineKindValue = (typeof INVOICE_LINE_KINDS)[number];
/** Kinds staff may choose for a line they add by hand. Late fees come only from the run. */
export const NEW_LINE_KINDS = ["SERVICE", "ADJUSTMENT", "ONCE_OFF"] as const;
export const INVOICE_LINE_KIND_LABELS: Record<InvoiceLineKindValue, string> = {
  SERVICE: "Service",
  OPTIONAL: "Optional extra",
  LATE_FEE: "Late payment fee",
  ADJUSTMENT: "Adjustment",
  ONCE_OFF: "Once-off work",
};
/** What a line says after its customer is anonymised. */
export const ANONYMISED_LINE_DESCRIPTIONS: Record<InvoiceLineKindValue, string> = {
  SERVICE: "Monthly service",
  OPTIONAL: "Optional extra",
  LATE_FEE: "Late payment fee",
  ADJUSTMENT: "Adjustment",
  ONCE_OFF: "Once-off work",
};

export const INVOICE_DISPLAY_STATUSES = [
  "DRAFT",
  "UNPAID",
  "PART_PAID",
  "PAID",
  "OVERDUE",
  "VOID",
] as const;
export type InvoiceDisplayStatus = (typeof INVOICE_DISPLAY_STATUSES)[number];
export const INVOICE_STATUS_LABELS: Record<InvoiceDisplayStatus, string> = {
  DRAFT: "Draft",
  UNPAID: "Unpaid",
  PART_PAID: "Part paid",
  PAID: "Paid",
  OVERDUE: "Overdue",
  VOID: "Void",
};

export const DRAFT_WARNINGS = ["noEmail", "firstMonth", "lateFee", "billToAgency"] as const;
export type DraftWarning = (typeof DRAFT_WARNINGS)[number];
export const DRAFT_WARNING_LABELS: Record<DraftWarning, string> = {
  noEmail: "No email on file: the invoice will not be emailed",
  firstMonth: "First month: check the amounts and any part month",
  lateFee: "Includes a suggested late payment fee",
  billToAgency: "Billed to the managing agency",
};

export const RUN_SKIP_REASONS = [
  "alreadyInvoiced",
  "notStarted",
  "lead",
  "paused",
  "archived",
  "anonymised",
  "failed",
] as const;
export type RunSkipReason = (typeof RUN_SKIP_REASONS)[number];
export const RUN_SKIP_LABELS: Record<RunSkipReason, string> = {
  alreadyInvoiced: "already invoiced for that month",
  notStarted: "service not started by then",
  lead: "still a lead",
  paused: "paused",
  archived: "archived",
  anonymised: "anonymised",
  failed: "could not be drafted (try again)",
};

export const PAYMENT_REPORT_STATUS_LABELS: Record<"WAITING" | "CONFIRMED" | "DISMISSED", string> = {
  WAITING: "Waiting",
  CONFIRMED: "Confirmed",
  DISMISSED: "Dismissed",
};

export const NOT_VAT_REGISTERED = "ACE Garden Services is not VAT registered, no VAT is charged.";

`packages/core/src/invoices/dates.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  addMonths,
  currentMonthSA,
  daysInMonth,
  dueDateFor,
  firstDayOf,
  formatDayMonth,
  formatMonthLabel,
  isMonthString,
  lastDayOf,
  monthOf,
  proRata,
  runMonthAllowed,
  serviceMonthFor,
} from "./dates";

describe("months", () => {
  it("accepts YYYY-MM only", () => {
    expect(isMonthString("2026-11")).toBe(true);
    for (const bad of ["2026-13", "2026-00", "2026-1", "26-11", "2026-11-01", "", "1999-12"]) {
      expect(isMonthString(bad), bad).toBe(false);
    }
  });
  it("adds months across year ends, both ways", () => {
    expect(addMonths("2026-11", 1)).toBe("2026-12");
    expect(addMonths("2026-12", 1)).toBe("2027-01");
    expect(addMonths("2027-01", -1)).toBe("2026-12");
    expect(addMonths("2026-05", -12)).toBe("2025-05");
  });
  it("knows 28, 29, 30 and 31 day months", () => {
    expect(daysInMonth("2027-02")).toBe(28);
    expect(daysInMonth("2028-02")).toBe(29);
    expect(daysInMonth("2026-11")).toBe(30);
    expect(daysInMonth("2026-12")).toBe(31);
    expect(firstDayOf("2026-12")).toBe("2026-12-01");
    expect(lastDayOf("2028-02")).toBe("2028-02-29");
    expect(lastDayOf("2026-11")).toBe("2026-11-30");
    expect(monthOf("2026-11-27")).toBe("2026-11");
  });
  it("reads the current month in South African time", () => {
    // 22:30 UTC on 31 Dec is already 1 Jan in South Africa.
    expect(currentMonthSA(new Date("2026-12-31T22:30:00Z"))).toBe("2027-01");
    expect(currentMonthSA(new Date("2026-11-27T10:00:00Z"))).toBe("2026-11");
  });
});

describe("the run on the 20th", () => {
  it("Pre clients: next month's service, due the last day of the run month", () => {
    expect(serviceMonthFor("2026-11", "PRE")).toBe("2026-12");
    expect(dueDateFor("2026-11", "PRE")).toBe("2026-11-30");
    expect(serviceMonthFor("2026-12", "PRE")).toBe("2027-01");
    expect(dueDateFor("2026-12", "PRE")).toBe("2026-12-31");
    expect(dueDateFor("2028-02", "PRE")).toBe("2028-02-29");
  });
  it("Post clients: this month's service, due the 25th", () => {
    expect(serviceMonthFor("2026-11", "POST")).toBe("2026-11");
    expect(dueDateFor("2026-11", "POST")).toBe("2026-11-25");
    expect(dueDateFor("2027-02", "POST")).toBe("2027-02-25");
  });
  it("allows run months from a year back to next month", () => {
    expect(runMonthAllowed("2026-11", "2026-11-20")).toBe(true);
    expect(runMonthAllowed("2026-12", "2026-11-20")).toBe(true);
    expect(runMonthAllowed("2025-11", "2026-11-20")).toBe(true);
    expect(runMonthAllowed("2027-01", "2026-11-20")).toBe(false);
    expect(runMonthAllowed("2025-10", "2026-11-20")).toBe(false);
    expect(runMonthAllowed("2026-13", "2026-11-20")).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("proRata", () => {
  it("charges the days from the start date, rounded to the cent", () => {
    // 22 of 31 days of R1,150.00 = 81612.903... cents
    expect(proRata(115000, "2026-12-10", "2026-12")).toEqual({
      amountCents: 81613,
      days: 22,
      daysInMonth: 31,
    });
    // 15 of 30 days: exactly half
    expect(proRata(100001, "2026-11-16", "2026-11")).toEqual({
      amountCents: 50001,
      days: 15,
      daysInMonth: 30,
    });
    // the last day of a leap February: 1 of 29 days
    expect(proRata(29000, "2028-02-29", "2028-02")).toEqual({
      amountCents: 1000,
      days: 1,
      daysInMonth: 29,
    });
  });
  it("is not used when the start is the 1st or outside the service month", () => {
    expect(proRata(115000, "2026-12-01", "2026-12")).toBeNull();
    expect(proRata(115000, "2026-11-20", "2026-12")).toBeNull();
    expect(proRata(115000, "2027-01-05", "2026-12")).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("labels", () => {
  it("names months and days in plain words", () => {
    expect(formatMonthLabel("2026-12")).toBe("December 2026");
    expect(formatMonthLabel("2027-01")).toBe("January 2027");
    expect(formatDayMonth("2026-12-10")).toBe("10 Dec");
    expect(formatDayMonth("2027-02-01")).toBe("1 Feb");
  });
});
```

`packages/core/src/invoices/money.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  fitDescription,
  formatBillingAddress,
  invoiceDisplayStatus,
  issueProblems,
  parseSignedRandToCents,
  suggestAllocations,
  sumLineCents,
} from "./money";

describe("parseSignedRandToCents", () => {
  it("reads positive and negative rand amounts", () => {
    expect(parseSignedRandToCents("1150")).toBe(115000);
    expect(parseSignedRandToCents("R 1 150,50")).toBe(115050);
    expect(parseSignedRandToCents("-100")).toBe(-10000);
    expect(parseSignedRandToCents(" -R50.5 ")).toBe(-5050);
  });
  it("refuses anything that is not an amount", () => {
    for (const bad of ["", "-", "abc", "1.234", "--5", "12-"]) {
      expect(parseSignedRandToCents(bad), bad).toBeNull();
    }
  });
});

describe("totals and status", () => {
  it("adds whole cents, adjustments included", () => {
    expect(sumLineCents([{ amountCents: 115000 }, { amountCents: 10000 }, { amountCents: -5000 }])).toBe(
      120000,
    );
    expect(sumLineCents([])).toBe(0);
  });
  it("works out the status shown from the stored status, balance and due date", () => {
    const today = "2026-12-05";
    const base = { status: "ISSUED" as const, totalCents: 100000, dueDate: "2026-12-31" };
    expect(invoiceDisplayStatus({ ...base, status: "DRAFT", allocatedCents: 0 }, today)).toBe("DRAFT");
    expect(invoiceDisplayStatus({ ...base, status: "VOID", allocatedCents: 0 }, today)).toBe("VOID");
    expect(invoiceDisplayStatus({ ...base, allocatedCents: 0 }, today)).toBe("UNPAID");
    expect(invoiceDisplayStatus({ ...base, allocatedCents: 40000 }, today)).toBe("PART_PAID");
    expect(invoiceDisplayStatus({ ...base, allocatedCents: 100000 }, today)).toBe("PAID");
    // due yesterday with money still owed
    expect(
      invoiceDisplayStatus({ ...base, dueDate: "2026-12-04", allocatedCents: 40000 }, today),
    ).toBe("OVERDUE");
    // due today is not yet overdue
    expect(invoiceDisplayStatus({ ...base, dueDate: "2026-12-05", allocatedCents: 0 }, today)).toBe(
      "UNPAID",
    );
    // paid in full is never overdue
    expect(
      invoiceDisplayStatus({ ...base, dueDate: "2026-11-01", allocatedCents: 100000 }, today),
    ).toBe("PAID");
  });
});

describe("suggestAllocations", () => {
  const invoices = [
    { id: "b", balanceCents: 50000, dueDate: "2026-11-30", reference: "INV-2026-0002" },
    { id: "a", balanceCents: 30000, dueDate: "2026-10-31", reference: "INV-2026-0001" },
    { id: "c", balanceCents: 20000, dueDate: "2026-11-30", reference: "INV-2026-0003" },
    { id: "z", balanceCents: 0, dueDate: "2026-09-30", reference: "INV-2026-0000" },
  ];
  it("pays the oldest due first, then by number, and stops when the money runs out", () => {
    expect(suggestAllocations(70000, invoices)).toEqual([
      { invoiceId: "a", amountCents: 30000 },
      { invoiceId: "b", amountCents: 40000 },
    ]);
  });
  it("leaves the rest unallocated when there is more money than owed", () => {
    expect(suggestAllocations(200000, invoices)).toEqual([
      { invoiceId: "a", amountCents: 30000 },
      { invoiceId: "b", amountCents: 50000 },
      { invoiceId: "c", amountCents: 20000 },
    ]);
  });
  it("suggests nothing for nothing", () => {
    expect(suggestAllocations(0, invoices)).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe("issueProblems", () => {
  const today = "2026-11-20";
  it("lists every problem in plain words", () => {
    expect(
      issueProblems(
        {
          lines: [
            { kind: "SERVICE", description: " ", amountCents: 100 },
            { kind: "SERVICE", description: "Garden", amountCents: -100 },
            { kind: "ADJUSTMENT", description: "Discount", amountCents: -500 },
          ],
          dueDate: "2026-11-19",
        },
        today,
      ),
    ).toEqual([
      "Line 1 needs a description.",
      "Line 2 cannot be negative. Only an adjustment can be.",
      "The total must be above zero.",
      "The due date has passed. Change it before issuing.",
    ]);
  });
  it("needs at least one line and no zero amounts", () => {
    expect(issueProblems({ lines: [], dueDate: today }, today)).toEqual([
      "Add at least one line.",
      "The total must be above zero.",
    ]);
    expect(
      issueProblems({ lines: [{ kind: "ONCE_OFF", description: "Clean-up", amountCents: 0 }], dueDate: today }, today),
    ).toEqual(["Line 1 needs an amount.", "The total must be above zero."]);
  });
  it("finds nothing wrong with a complete invoice", () => {
    expect(
      issueProblems(
        {
          lines: [
            { kind: "SERVICE", description: "Main: Weekly garden maintenance", amountCents: 115000 },
            { kind: "ADJUSTMENT", description: "Discount", amountCents: -15000 },
          ],
          dueDate: today,
        },
        today,
      ),
    ).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe("addresses and descriptions", () => {
  it("joins the billing address lines that are filled in", () => {
    expect(
      formatBillingAddress({
        billingLine1: "1 Test Road",
        billingLine2: null,
        billingSuburb: "Testville",
        billingCity: "Pretoria",
        billingPostalCode: "0001",
      }),
    ).toBe("1 Test Road, Testville, Pretoria, 0001");
    expect(
      formatBillingAddress({
        billingLine1: null,
        billingLine2: " ",
        billingSuburb: null,
        billingCity: null,
        billingPostalCode: null,
      }),
    ).toBeNull();
  });
  it("cuts descriptions to 300 characters", () => {
    expect(fitDescription("Short")).toBe("Short");
    const long = fitDescription("x".repeat(400));
    expect(long).toHaveLength(300);
    expect(long.endsWith("...")).toBe(true);
  });
});

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  buildStatementData,
  defaultStatementPeriod,
  statementPeriodProblem,
  type StatementInvoiceInput,
  type StatementPaymentInput,
} from "./statement-math";

const inv = (over: Partial<StatementInvoiceInput>): StatementInvoiceInput => ({
  id: "i",
  reference: "INV-2026-0001",
  issuedOn: "2026-10-20",
  dueOn: "2026-10-31",
  totalCents: 100000,
  allocatedCents: 0,
  customerId: "own",
  customerName: "ZZ Own",
  ...over,
});
const pay = (over: Partial<StatementPaymentInput>): StatementPaymentInput => ({
  id: "p",
  receivedOn: "2026-11-02",
  amountCents: 50000,
  allocatedCents: 50000,
  reference: null,
  ...over,
});

describe("buildStatementData", () => {
  it("works out the opening balance, the entries with a running balance and the closing balance", () => {
    const data = buildStatementData({
      from: "2026-11-01",
      to: "2026-11-30",
      today: "2026-12-05",
      ownCustomerId: "own",
      invoices: [
        inv({ id: "old", reference: "INV-2026-0001", issuedOn: "2026-10-20", allocatedCents: 100000 }),
        inv({ id: "nov", reference: "INV-2026-0002", issuedOn: "2026-11-20", dueOn: "2026-11-30" }),
        inv({ id: "dec", reference: "INV-2026-0003", issuedOn: "2026-12-01" }),
      ],
      payments: [
        pay({ id: "p-oct", receivedOn: "2026-10-25", amountCents: 60000, allocatedCents: 60000 }),
        pay({ id: "p-nov", receivedOn: "2026-11-02", amountCents: 50000, allocatedCents: 40000 }),
      ],
    });
    // before 1 Nov: R1,000 invoiced, R600 paid
    expect(data.openingCents).toBe(40000);
    expect(data.invoicedCents).toBe(100000);
    expect(data.paidCents).toBe(50000);
    expect(data.closingCents).toBe(90000);
    expect(data.entries.map((e) => [e.date, e.kind, e.debitCents, e.creditCents, e.balanceCents])).toEqual([
      ["2026-11-02", "PAYMENT", 0, 50000, -10000],
      ["2026-11-20", "INVOICE", 100000, 0, 90000],
    ]);
    // R100 of the November payment is not allocated yet
    expect(data.creditCents).toBe(10000);
    // the December invoice is after the period and not listed at all
    expect(data.outstanding.map((o) => o.id)).toEqual(["nov"]);
    expect(data.outstanding[0]).toMatchObject({ balanceCents: 100000, overdue: true });
    expect(data.byComplex).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("lists invoices before payments on the same day", () => {
    const data = buildStatementData({
      from: "2026-11-01",
      to: "2026-11-30",
      today: "2026-11-10",
      ownCustomerId: "own",
      invoices: [inv({ id: "a", issuedOn: "2026-11-05", dueOn: "2026-11-30", allocatedCents: 100000 })],
      payments: [pay({ id: "b", receivedOn: "2026-11-05", amountCents: 100000, allocatedCents: 100000 })],
    });
    expect(data.entries.map((e) => e.kind)).toEqual(["INVOICE", "PAYMENT"]);
    expect(data.closingCents).toBe(0);
    expect(data.outstanding).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("breaks an agency's statement down per complex", () => {
    const data = buildStatementData({
      from: "2026-11-01",
      to: "2026-11-30",
      today: "2026-11-25",
      ownCustomerId: "agency",
      invoices: [
        inv({ id: "a1", customerId: "a", customerName: "ZZ Complex A", issuedOn: "2026-11-20", totalCents: 30000 }),
        inv({ id: "b1", customerId: "b", customerName: "ZZ Complex B", issuedOn: "2026-11-20", totalCents: 50000, allocatedCents: 50000 }),
        inv({ id: "a0", customerId: "a", customerName: "ZZ Complex A", issuedOn: "2026-10-20", totalCents: 30000 }),
      ],
      payments: [pay({ id: "p", receivedOn: "2026-11-21", amountCents: 50000, allocatedCents: 50000 })],
    });
    expect(data.byComplex).toEqual([
      { customerId: "a", customerName: "ZZ Complex A", invoicedCents: 30000, outstandingCents: 60000 },
      { customerId: "b", customerName: "ZZ Complex B", invoicedCents: 50000, outstandingCents: 0 },
    ]);
    expect(data.entries.find((e) => e.id === "a1")?.customerName).toBe("ZZ Complex A");
    // closing = what is still owed minus unallocated credit
    expect(data.closingCents).toBe(60000);
  });
});

describe("statement periods", () => {
  it("defaults to the start of the month two months back until today", () => {
    expect(defaultStatementPeriod("2026-11-27")).toEqual({ from: "2026-09-01", to: "2026-11-27" });
    expect(defaultStatementPeriod("2027-01-05")).toEqual({ from: "2026-11-01", to: "2027-01-05" });
  });
  it("refuses a backwards, invalid or too long period", () => {
    expect(statementPeriodProblem("2026-11-01", "2026-11-30")).toBeNull();
    expect(statementPeriodProblem("2026-12-01", "2026-11-30")).toBe("The start date must be on or before the end date.");
    expect(statementPeriodProblem("2026-02-30", "2026-11-30")).toBe("Choose valid dates.");
    expect(statementPeriodProblem("2020-01-01", "2026-11-30")).toBe("Choose a period of three years or less.");
  });
});

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `packages/core/src/invoices/dates.ts`**

```ts
import { isValidDateString, todaySA } from "../quotes/reference";
import type { BillingTiming } from "./labels";

const MONTH = /^(\d{4})-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$/;

/** "YYYY-MM" with a year from 2000 to 2999. */
export function isMonthString(value: string): boolean {
  const m = MONTH.exec(value);
  if (!m) return false;
  const year = Number(m[1]);
  return year >= 2000 && year <= 2999;
}

function parts(month: string): { year: number; month: number } {
  return { year: Number(month.slice(0, 4)), month: Number(month.slice(5, 7)) };
}

/** The current month in South Africa as "YYYY-MM". */
export function currentMonthSA(now: Date = new Date()): string {
  return todaySA(now).slice(0, 7);
}

export function addMonths(month: string, n: number): string {
  const p = parts(month);
  const total = p.year * 12 + (p.month - 1) + n;
  const year = Math.floor(total / 12);
  const m = total - year * 12 + 1;
  return `${year}-${String(m).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

export function daysInMonth(month: string): number {
  const p = parts(month);
  return new Date(Date.UTC(p.year, p.month, 0)).getUTCDate();
}

export const firstDayOf = (month: string): string => `${month}-01`;
export const lastDayOf = (month: string): string =>
  `${month}-${String(daysInMonth(month)).padStart(2, "0")}`;
export const monthOf = (date: string): string => date.slice(0, 7);

/** Pre: the run in month M bills month M+1. Post: it bills month M. */
export function serviceMonthFor(runMonth: string, timing: BillingTiming): string {
  return timing === "PRE" ? addMonths(runMonth, 1) : runMonth;
}

/** Pre: due the last day of the run month. Post: due the 25th of the run month. */
export function dueDateFor(runMonth: string, timing: BillingTiming): string {
  return timing === "PRE" ? lastDayOf(runMonth) : `${runMonth}-25`;
}

/**
 * The part-month amount when a service starts inside the service month after the 1st: the days from the
 * start date to the month end, over the days in the month, rounded to the cent. Null when it does not apply.
 */
export function proRata(
  amountCents: number,
  startDate: string,
  serviceMonth: string,
): { amountCents: number; days: number; daysInMonth: number } | null {
  if (!isValidDateString(startDate) || monthOf(startDate) !== serviceMonth) return null;
  const startDay = Number(startDate.slice(8, 10));
  if (startDay <= 1) return null;
  const dim = daysInMonth(serviceMonth);
  const days = dim - startDay + 1;
  return { amountCents: Math.round((amountCents * days) / dim), days, daysInMonth: dim };
}

/** A run month from 12 months before the current month up to next month. */
export function runMonthAllowed(runMonth: string, today: string): boolean {
  if (!isMonthString(runMonth)) return false;
  const current = monthOf(today);
  return runMonth >= addMonths(current, -12) && runMonth <= addMonths(current, 1);
}

const MONTH_NAMES = [
  "January",
  "February",
  "March",
  "April",
  "May",
  "June",
  "July",
  "August",
  "September",
  "October",
  "November",
  "December",
];

/** "2026-12" -> "December 2026". */
export function formatMonthLabel(month: string): string {
  const p = parts(month);
  return `${MONTH_NAMES[p.month - 1] ?? ""} ${p.year}`;
}

/** "2026-12-10" -> "10 Dec". */
export function formatDayMonth(date: string): string {
  const name = MONTH_NAMES[Number(date.slice(5, 7)) - 1] ?? "";
  return `${Number(date.slice(8, 10))} ${name.slice(0, 3)}`;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `packages/core/src/invoices/money.ts`**

```ts
import { parseRandToCents } from "../customers/schemas";
import type { InvoiceDisplayStatus, InvoiceLineKindValue } from "./labels";

export const MAX_INVOICE_LINES = 40;
export const MAX_LINE_CENTS = 100_000_000; // R1 000 000
export const MAX_PAYMENT_CENTS = 100_000_000; // R1 000 000
export const MAX_DESCRIPTION = 300;

/** Like parseRandToCents, but a leading minus is allowed (adjustments). */
export function parseSignedRandToCents(input: string): number | null {
  const text = input.trim();
  const negative = text.startsWith("-");
  const cents = parseRandToCents(negative ? text.slice(1) : text);
  if (cents === null) return null;
  return negative ? -cents : cents;
}

export function sumLineCents(lines: readonly { amountCents: number }[]): number {
  return lines.reduce((total, l) => total + l.amountCents, 0);
}

/**
 * The status shown. Only DRAFT, ISSUED and VOID are stored; for an issued invoice the balance (total minus
 * allocations from payments that are not voided) and the due date decide the rest. Due today is not overdue.
 */
export function invoiceDisplayStatus(
  inv: {
    status: "DRAFT" | "ISSUED" | "VOID";
    totalCents: number;
    allocatedCents: number;
    dueDate: string | null;
  },
  today: string,
): InvoiceDisplayStatus {
  if (inv.status === "DRAFT") return "DRAFT";
  if (inv.status === "VOID") return "VOID";
  const balance = inv.totalCents - inv.allocatedCents;
  if (balance <= 0) return "PAID";
  if (inv.dueDate !== null && inv.dueDate < today) return "OVERDUE";
  return inv.allocatedCents > 0 ? "PART_PAID" : "UNPAID";
}

/** Oldest due first (then by reference), each up to its balance, until the money runs out. */
export function suggestAllocations(
  amountCents: number,
  invoices: readonly { id: string; balanceCents: number; dueDate: string; reference: string }[],
): { invoiceId: string; amountCents: number }[] {
  const ordered = [...invoices]
    .filter((i) => i.balanceCents > 0)
    .sort((a, b) =>
      a.dueDate === b.dueDate
        ? a.reference < b.reference
          ? -1
          : a.reference > b.reference
            ? 1
            : 0
        : a.dueDate < b.dueDate
          ? -1
          : 1,
    );
  const out: { invoiceId: string; amountCents: number }[] = [];
  let left = amountCents;
  for (const i of ordered) {
    if (left <= 0) break;
    const take = Math.min(left, i.balanceCents);
    out.push({ invoiceId: i.id, amountCents: take });
    left -= take;
  }
  return out;
}

/** What stops a draft being issued, in plain words. An empty list means it can be issued. */
export function issueProblems(
  input: {
    lines: readonly { kind: InvoiceLineKindValue; description: string; amountCents: number }[];
    dueDate: string;
  },
  today: string,
): string[] {
  const problems: string[] = [];
  if (input.lines.length === 0) problems.push("Add at least one line.");
  if (input.lines.length > MAX_INVOICE_LINES) {
    problems.push(`Keep the invoice to ${MAX_INVOICE_LINES} lines or fewer.`);
  }
  input.lines.forEach((line, index) => {
    const n = `Line ${index + 1}`;
    if (!line.description.trim()) problems.push(`${n} needs a description.`);
    if (line.amountCents === 0) problems.push(`${n} needs an amount.`);
    else if (line.amountCents < 0 && line.kind !== "ADJUSTMENT") {
      problems.push(`${n} cannot be negative. Only an adjustment can be.`);
    }
  });
  if (sumLineCents(input.lines) <= 0) problems.push("The total must be above zero.");
  if (input.dueDate < today) problems.push("The due date has passed. Change it before issuing.");
  return problems;
}

/** The billing address lines that are filled in, joined with ", ". Null when there are none. */
export function formatBillingAddress(c: {
  billingLine1: string | null;
  billingLine2: string | null;
  billingSuburb: string | null;
  billingCity: string | null;
  billingPostalCode: string | null;
}): string | null {
  const text = [c.billingLine1, c.billingLine2, c.billingSuburb, c.billingCity, c.billingPostalCode]
    .map((v) => (v ?? "").trim())
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(", ");
  return text === "" ? null : text;
}

/** Cuts a description to the 300 characters the database allows. */
export function fitDescription(text: string): string {
  const t = text.trim();
  return t.length <= MAX_DESCRIPTION ? t : `${t.slice(0, MAX_DESCRIPTION - 3)}...`;
}
```

```ts
/** "INV-2026-0001". Null for a draft (no number yet). */
export function formatInvoiceReference(i: {
  numberYear: number | null;
  numberSeq: number | null;
}): string | null {
  if (i.numberYear === null || i.numberSeq === null) return null;
  return `INV-${i.numberYear}-${String(i.numberSeq).padStart(4, "0")}`;
}

/** "INV-2026-0104", "2026-104" or "104" -> the number parts. Anything else -> null. */
export function parseInvoiceSearch(term: string): { numberYear?: number; numberSeq: number } | null {
  const m = /^(?:inv-)?(?:(\d{4})-)?(\d{1,6})$/i.exec(term.trim());
  if (!m?.[2]) return null;
  return { ...(m[1] ? { numberYear: Number(m[1]) } : {}), numberSeq: Number(m[2]) };
}

const safe = (text: string) => text.replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9._-]/g, "-");

export function invoicePdfFilename(reference: string): string {
  return `${safe(reference)}.pdf`;
}

export function statementPdfFilename(customerNumber: string, to: string): string {
  return `Statement-${safe(customerNumber)}-${safe(to)}.pdf`;
}
