- [ ] **Step 7: Write `packages/core/src/invoices/statement-math.ts`**

```ts
import { addDays, isValidDateString } from "../quotes/reference";
import { addMonths, firstDayOf, monthOf } from "./dates";

export type StatementInvoiceInput = {
  id: string;
  reference: string;
  /** South African date of issue, YYYY-MM-DD. */
  issuedOn: string;
  dueOn: string;
  totalCents: number;
  /** From payments that are not voided, as they stand now. */
  allocatedCents: number;
  /** The client the work was for (a complex, on an agency's statement). */
  customerId: string;
  customerName: string;
};

export type StatementPaymentInput = {
  id: string;
  receivedOn: string;
  amountCents: number;
  allocatedCents: number;
  reference: string | null;
};

export type StatementEntry = {
  id: string;
  date: string;
  kind: "INVOICE" | "PAYMENT";
  label: string;
  /** The complex, on an agency's statement; null otherwise. */
  customerName: string | null;
  debitCents: number;
  creditCents: number;
  balanceCents: number;
};

export type StatementData = {
  from: string;
  to: string;
  openingCents: number;
  invoicedCents: number;
  paidCents: number;
  closingCents: number;
  /** Money received but not allocated to any invoice. */
  creditCents: number;
  entries: StatementEntry[];
  outstanding: {
    id: string;
    reference: string;
    customerName: string | null;
    dueOn: string;
    balanceCents: number;
    overdue: boolean;
  }[];
  /** Agencies only: one row per complex, sorted by name. Empty for an ordinary customer. */
  byComplex: {
    customerId: string;
    customerName: string;
    invoicedCents: number;
    outstandingCents: number;
  }[];
};

/**
 * The statement for one bill-to customer. `invoices` are its ISSUED invoices (never void ones) and `payments`
 * its payments that are not voided; anything after `to` is ignored. Opening and closing balances are worked
 * out here, never stored. Positive balances are owed to ACE; a negative closing balance is money in credit.
 */
export function buildStatementData(input: {
  from: string;
  to: string;
  today: string;
  ownCustomerId: string;
  invoices: readonly StatementInvoiceInput[];
  payments: readonly StatementPaymentInput[];
}): StatementData {
  const invoices = input.invoices.filter((i) => i.issuedOn <= input.to);
  const payments = input.payments.filter((p) => p.receivedOn <= input.to);
  const forAgency = invoices.some((i) => i.customerId !== input.ownCustomerId);
  const nameFor = (i: StatementInvoiceInput) => (forAgency ? i.customerName : null);

  const before = (date: string) => date < input.from;
  const openingCents =
    invoices.filter((i) => before(i.issuedOn)).reduce((t, i) => t + i.totalCents, 0) -
    payments.filter((p) => before(p.receivedOn)).reduce((t, p) => t + p.amountCents, 0);

  const inPeriod = invoices.filter((i) => !before(i.issuedOn));
  const paidInPeriod = payments.filter((p) => !before(p.receivedOn));
  const raw = [
    ...inPeriod.map((i) => ({
      id: i.id,
      date: i.issuedOn,
      kind: "INVOICE" as const,
      label: `Invoice ${i.reference}`,
      customerName: nameFor(i),
      debitCents: i.totalCents,
      creditCents: 0,
      order: 0,
    })),
    ...paidInPeriod.map((p) => ({
      id: p.id,
      date: p.receivedOn,
      kind: "PAYMENT" as const,
      label: p.reference ? `Payment received, ref ${p.reference}` : "Payment received",
      customerName: null,
      debitCents: 0,
      creditCents: p.amountCents,
      order: 1,
    })),
  ].sort((a, b) =>
    a.date !== b.date ? (a.date < b.date ? -1 : 1) : a.order !== b.order ? a.order - b.order : a.label < b.label ? -1 : 1,
  );
  let running = openingCents;
  const entries: StatementEntry[] = raw.map((e) => {
    running += e.debitCents - e.creditCents;
    return {
      id: e.id,
      date: e.date,
      kind: e.kind,
      label: e.label,
      customerName: e.customerName,
      debitCents: e.debitCents,
      creditCents: e.creditCents,
      balanceCents: running,
    };
  });

  const invoicedCents = inPeriod.reduce((t, i) => t + i.totalCents, 0);
  const paidCents = paidInPeriod.reduce((t, p) => t + p.amountCents, 0);
  const balanceOf = (i: StatementInvoiceInput) => Math.max(0, i.totalCents - i.allocatedCents);

  const outstanding = invoices
    .filter((i) => balanceOf(i) > 0)
    .sort((a, b) => (a.dueOn !== b.dueOn ? (a.dueOn < b.dueOn ? -1 : 1) : a.reference < b.reference ? -1 : 1))
    .map((i) => ({
      id: i.id,
      reference: i.reference,
      customerName: nameFor(i),
      dueOn: i.dueOn,
      balanceCents: balanceOf(i),
      overdue: i.dueOn < input.today,
    }));

  const byComplex: StatementData["byComplex"] = [];
  if (forAgency) {
    const map = new Map<string, StatementData["byComplex"][number]>();
    for (const i of invoices) {
      const row = map.get(i.customerId) ?? {
        customerId: i.customerId,
        customerName: i.customerName,
        invoicedCents: 0,
        outstandingCents: 0,
      };
      if (!before(i.issuedOn)) row.invoicedCents += i.totalCents;
      row.outstandingCents += balanceOf(i);
      map.set(i.customerId, row);
    }
    byComplex.push(
      ...[...map.values()].sort((a, b) => (a.customerName < b.customerName ? -1 : a.customerName > b.customerName ? 1 : 0)),
    );
  }

  return {
    from: input.from,
    to: input.to,
    openingCents,
    invoicedCents,
    paidCents,
    closingCents: openingCents + invoicedCents - paidCents,
    creditCents: payments.reduce((t, p) => t + Math.max(0, p.amountCents - p.allocatedCents), 0),
    entries,
    outstanding,
    byComplex,
  };
}

/** The first day of the month two months back, until today. */
export function defaultStatementPeriod(today: string): { from: string; to: string } {
  return { from: firstDayOf(addMonths(monthOf(today), -2)), to: today };
}

export const MAX_STATEMENT_DAYS = 1100;

/** A plain sentence when the period cannot be used, else null. */
export function statementPeriodProblem(from: string, to: string): string | null {
  if (!isValidDateString(from) || !isValidDateString(to)) return "Choose valid dates.";
  if (from > to) return "The start date must be on or before the end date.";
  if (addDays(from, MAX_STATEMENT_DAYS) < to) return "Choose a period of three years or less.";
  return null;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write `packages/core/src/invoices/schemas.ts`**

```ts
import { z } from "zod";
import { parseRandToCents } from "../customers/schemas";
import { isValidDateString } from "../quotes/reference";
import { isMonthString } from "./dates";
import { BILLING_TIMINGS, INVOICE_LINE_KINDS } from "./labels";
import {
  MAX_DESCRIPTION,
  MAX_INVOICE_LINES,
  MAX_LINE_CENTS,
  MAX_PAYMENT_CENTS,
  parseSignedRandToCents,
} from "./money";

export type FormParse<T> = { ok: true; input: T } | { ok: false; fieldErrors: Record<string, string> };
const DAMAGED = { ok: false as const, fieldErrors: { form: "Refresh the page and try again." } };
const isUuid = (v: string) => z.uuid().safeParse(v).success;
const tooLong = (max: number) => `Keep this under ${max} characters.`;

export const billingTimingSchema = z.enum(BILLING_TIMINGS);
export const runMonthSchema = z.string().refine(isMonthString, "Choose a month.");
export const reasonSchema = z
  .string()
  .trim()
  .min(1, "Say why in a few words.")
  .max(300, "Keep the reason to 300 characters or fewer.");

// --- settings ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------

export const invoiceSettingsFormSchema = z
  .object({
    bankName: z.string().trim().max(80, tooLong(80)),
    accountHolder: z.string().trim().max(120, tooLong(120)),
    accountNumber: z
      .string()
      .transform((v) => v.replace(/[\s-]/g, ""))
      .refine((v) => v === "" || /^\d{6,16}$/.test(v), "Enter the account number as 6 to 16 digits."),
    branchCode: z
      .string()
      .trim()
      .refine((v) => v === "" || /^\d{6}$/.test(v), "Enter the 6-digit branch code."),
    accountType: z.string().trim().max(40, tooLong(40)),
    onceOffDueDays: z
      .string()
      .trim()
      .refine((v) => /^\d{1,2}$/.test(v) && Number(v) <= 90, "Enter a whole number of days from 0 to 90.")
      .transform(Number),
    lateFee: z.string().transform((v, ctx) => {
      const cents = v.trim() === "" ? 0 : parseRandToCents(v);
      if (cents === null || cents > 1_000_000) {
        ctx.addIssue({ code: "custom", message: "Enter an amount from 0 to 10000, for example 100." });
        return z.NEVER;
      }
      return cents;
    }),
  })
  .transform(({ lateFee, ...rest }) => ({ ...rest, lateFeeCents: lateFee }));
export type InvoiceSettingsInput = z.output<typeof invoiceSettingsFormSchema>;

/** Issuing needs at least these four. */
export function bankDetailsReady(s: {
  bankName: string;
  accountHolder: string;
  accountNumber: string;
  branchCode: string;
}): boolean {
  return [s.bankName, s.accountHolder, s.accountNumber, s.branchCode].every((v) => v.trim() !== "");
}

export const invoiceSnapshotSchema = z.object({
  bank: z.object({
    bankName: z.string(),
    accountHolder: z.string(),
    accountNumber: z.string(),
    branchCode: z.string(),
    accountType: z.string(),
  }),
  business: z.object({
    legalName: z.string(),
    tradingName: z.string(),
    registrationNumber: z.string(),
    phone: z.string(),
    email: z.string(),
    website: z.string(),
  }),
  paymentReference: z.string(),
});
export type InvoiceSnapshot = z.output<typeof invoiceSnapshotSchema>;

// --- drafts -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------

export const invoiceDraftSchema = z.object({
  version: z.number().int().min(1),
  dueDate: z.string().refine(isValidDateString, "Choose a valid date.").nullable(),
  lines: z
    .array(
      z.object({
        id: z.uuid().nullable(),
        kind: z.enum(INVOICE_LINE_KINDS),
        description: z.string().trim().min(1).max(MAX_DESCRIPTION),
        amountCents: z
          .number()
          .int()
          .refine((v) => v !== 0 && Math.abs(v) <= MAX_LINE_CENTS),
      }),
    )
    .max(MAX_INVOICE_LINES),
});
export type InvoiceDraftInput = z.output<typeof invoiceDraftSchema>;

/**
 * The draft form posts rows `line-<i>-id`, `-kind`, `-description`, `-amount` and `-remove` (a tick box).
 * A row with no id, no description and no amount is an unused empty row and is skipped.
 */
export function draftInputFromForm(values: Record<string, string>): FormParse<InvoiceDraftInput> {
  const version = Number(values.version);
  if (!/^\d+$/.test(values.version ?? "") || !Number.isSafeInteger(version) || version < 1) {
    return DAMAGED;
  }
  const fieldErrors: Record<string, string> = {};
  const dueText = (values.dueDate ?? "").trim();
  if (dueText !== "" && !isValidDateString(dueText)) fieldErrors.dueDate = "Choose a valid date.";

  const lines: InvoiceDraftInput["lines"] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < MAX_INVOICE_LINES; i += 1) {
    const p = `line-${i}-`;
    const id = (values[`${p}id`] ?? "").trim();
    const description = (values[`${p}description`] ?? "").trim();
    const amountText = (values[`${p}amount`] ?? "").trim();
    if (values[`${p}remove`] === "on") continue;
    if (id === "" && description === "" && amountText === "") continue;
    if (id !== "" && !isUuid(id)) return DAMAGED;
    const kind = INVOICE_LINE_KINDS.find((k) => k === values[`${p}kind`]);
    if (!kind) {
      fieldErrors[`${p}kind`] = "Choose what kind of line this is.";
      continue;
    }
    if (description === "") fieldErrors[`${p}description`] = "Enter a description.";
    else if (description.length > MAX_DESCRIPTION) fieldErrors[`${p}description`] = tooLong(MAX_DESCRIPTION);
    const cents = parseSignedRandToCents(amountText);
    if (cents === null || cents === 0) {
      fieldErrors[`${p}amount`] = "Enter an amount, for example 1150 or -100.";
    } else if (Math.abs(cents) > MAX_LINE_CENTS) {
      fieldErrors[`${p}amount`] = "Keep each line to R1 000 000 or less.";
    } else if (cents < 0 && kind !== "ADJUSTMENT") {
      fieldErrors[`${p}amount`] = "Only an adjustment can be negative.";
    }
    lines.push({ id: id === "" ? null : id, kind, description, amountCents: cents ?? 0 });
  }
  if (Object.keys(fieldErrors).length > 0) return { ok: false, fieldErrors };
  return { ok: true, input: { version, dueDate: dueText === "" ? null : dueText, lines } };
}

// --- payments ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------

export const EARLIEST_PAYMENT_DATE = "2020-01-01";
export const MAX_REFERENCE = 100;
export const MAX_NOTE = 500;

export type PaymentInput = {
  payerCustomerId: string;
  receivedOn: string;
  amountCents: number;
  reference: string | null;
  note: string | null;
  allocations: { invoiceId: string; amountCents: number }[];
  reportId: string | null;
};

/** The record-payment form: payer, date, amount, reference, note, an optional report id and `alloc-<invoiceId>` boxes. */
export function paymentInputFromForm(values: Record<string, string>, today: string): FormParse<PaymentInput> {
  const payerCustomerId = (values.payerCustomerId ?? "").trim();
  const reportText = (values.reportId ?? "").trim();
  if (!isUuid(payerCustomerId) || (reportText !== "" && !isUuid(reportText))) return DAMAGED;
  const fieldErrors: Record<string, string> = {};

  const receivedOn = (values.receivedOn ?? "").trim();
  if (!isValidDateString(receivedOn) || receivedOn < EARLIEST_PAYMENT_DATE || receivedOn > today) {
    fieldErrors.receivedOn = "Choose the date the money arrived (not in the future).";
  }
  const amountCents = parseRandToCents(values.amount ?? "");
  if (amountCents === null || amountCents <= 0 || amountCents > MAX_PAYMENT_CENTS) {
    fieldErrors.amount = "Enter the amount received, for example 1150.00.";
  }
  const reference = (values.reference ?? "").trim();
  if (reference.length > MAX_REFERENCE) fieldErrors.reference = tooLong(MAX_REFERENCE);
  const note = (values.note ?? "").trim();
  if (note.length > MAX_NOTE) fieldErrors.note = tooLong(MAX_NOTE);

  const allocations: PaymentInput["allocations"] = [];
  for (const [key, raw] of Object.entries(values)) {
    if (!key.startsWith("alloc-")) continue;
    const invoiceId = key.slice("alloc-".length);
    if (!isUuid(invoiceId)) return DAMAGED;
    const text = raw.trim();
    if (text === "") continue;
    const cents = parseRandToCents(text);
    if (cents === null || cents > MAX_PAYMENT_CENTS) {
      fieldErrors[key] = "Enter an amount or leave the box empty.";
      continue;
    }
    if (cents > 0) allocations.push({ invoiceId, amountCents: cents });
  }
  if (
    fieldErrors.amount === undefined &&
    amountCents !== null &&
    allocations.reduce((t, a) => t + a.amountCents, 0) > amountCents
  ) {
    fieldErrors.amount = "The amounts allocated add up to more than the payment.";
  }
  if (Object.keys(fieldErrors).length > 0 || amountCents === null) return { ok: false, fieldErrors };
  return {
    ok: true,
    input: {
      payerCustomerId,
      receivedOn,
      amountCents,
      reference: reference === "" ? null : reference,
      note: note === "" ? null : note,
      allocations,
      reportId: reportText === "" ? null : reportText,
    },
  };
}

export const allocationFormSchema = z
  .object({
    paymentId: z.uuid(),
    invoiceId: z.uuid(),
    amount: z.string().transform((v, ctx) => {
      const cents = parseRandToCents(v);
      if (cents === null || cents <= 0 || cents > MAX_PAYMENT_CENTS) {
        ctx.addIssue({ code: "custom", message: "Enter an amount above zero." });
        return z.NEVER;
      }
      return cents;
    }),
  })
  .transform(({ amount, ...rest }) => ({ ...rest, amountCents: amount }));
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `packages/core/src/invoices/store.ts`**

```ts
import { recordAudit, staffParty } from "../audit";
import { DomainError, type Actor } from "../customers/access";
import { lockCustomer } from "../customers/store";
import type { Db } from "../db";
import type { Prisma } from "../generated/prisma/client";

/** What the invoice functions need. Works with the normal connection or a transaction. */
export type InvoiceStore = Pick<
  Db,
  | "invoice"
  | "invoiceLine"
  | "invoiceSettings"
  | "invoiceCounter"
  | "payment"
  | "paymentAllocation"
  | "paymentReport"
  | "customer"
  | "contact"
  | "property"
  | "quote"
  | "quoteLine"
  | "quoteWording"
  | "organisationSettings"
  | "auditLog"
  | "staffUser"
  | "$queryRaw"
>;

/** Messages the app compares against (to choose a fixed notice code) as well as shows. */
export const INVOICE_MESSAGES = {
  notFound: "That invoice could not be found.",
  paymentNotFound: "That payment could not be found.",
  reportNotFound: "That payment report could not be found.",
  onlyDraftChange: "Only a draft can be changed.",
  onlyDraftIssue: "Only a draft can be issued.",
  onlyDraftDelete: "Only a draft can be deleted.",
  onlyIssuedVoid: "Only an issued invoice can be voided. Delete a draft instead.",
  onlyIssuedEmail: "Only an issued invoice can be emailed.",
  hasAllocations:
    "This invoice has payments allocated to it. Remove the allocations or void the payments first.",
  billingChanged: "The customer's billing changed while this was being done. Try again.",
  bankMissing: "Enter ACE's bank details in Invoice settings before issuing.",
  archived: "Restore this customer before invoicing.",
  paymentVoided: "This payment has been voided.",
  notPayersInvoice: "That invoice is not billed to this payer.",
  notIssued: "Payments can only be allocated to issued invoices.",
  overBalance: "That is more than the invoice still owes.",
  overPayment: "That is more than is left of the payment.",
  duplicateInvoice: "Each invoice can be listed only once.",
  reportResolved: "This report has already been dealt with.",
  quoteInvoiced: "This quote has already been invoiced.",
  quoteNotAccepted: "Only an accepted quote of this customer can be invoiced.",
  quoteNoOnceOff: "That quote has no accepted once-off work to invoice.",
  runMonth: "Choose a month from a year ago up to next month.",
} as const;

/** Two people saved the same draft; the later one must reload instead of overwriting. */
export class InvoiceDraftConflictError extends DomainError {
  constructor() {
    super("This invoice was changed somewhere else. Reload the page to see the latest version.");
    this.name = "InvoiceDraftConflictError";
  }
}

/** A draft that cannot be issued yet. `problems` are plain sentences; the message joins them. */
export class InvoiceIssueError extends DomainError {
  readonly problems: string[];
  constructor(problems: string[]) {
    super(problems.join("\n"));
    this.name = "InvoiceIssueError";
    this.problems = problems;
  }
}

async function logEvent(
  store: Pick<Db, "auditLog">,
  actor: Actor,
  entityType: string,
  action: string,
  entityId: string,
  after?: Prisma.InputJsonValue,
): Promise<void> {
  const party = staffParty(actor);
  await recordAudit(store, {
    actorType: party.actorType,
    actorId: actor.id,
    action,
    entityType,
    entityId,
    ...(after !== undefined ? { after } : {}),
    ...(party.ip ? { ip: party.ip } : {}),
    ...(party.userAgent ? { userAgent: party.userAgent } : {}),
  });
}

/** An audit line about an invoice. `after` holds field names, ids, counts and fixed words only. */
export function logInvoiceEvent(
  store: Pick<Db, "auditLog">,
  actor: Actor,
  action: string,
  invoiceId: string,
  after?: Prisma.InputJsonValue,
): Promise<void> {
  return logEvent(store, actor, "invoice", action, invoiceId, after);
}

/** An audit line about a payment. `after` holds field names, ids, counts and fixed words only. */
export function logPaymentEvent(
  store: Pick<Db, "auditLog">,
  actor: Actor,
  action: string,
  paymentId: string,
  after?: Prisma.InputJsonValue,
): Promise<void> {
  return logEvent(store, actor, "payment", action, paymentId, after);
}

const sortedUnique = (ids: readonly string[]) => [...new Set(ids)].sort();

/** Locks several customers, smaller id first (the lock order used everywhere). */
export async function lockCustomersInOrder(
  store: Pick<Db, "customer">,
  ids: readonly string[],
): Promise<void> {
  for (const id of sortedUnique(ids)) await lockCustomer(store, id);
}

/** Locks invoice rows until the transaction ends, smaller id first. */
export async function lockInvoiceRows(
  store: Pick<Db, "$queryRaw">,
  ids: readonly string[],
): Promise<void> {
  for (const id of sortedUnique(ids)) {
    const rows = await store.$queryRaw<{ id: string }[]>`
      select "id" from "invoices" where "id" = ${id}::uuid for update`;
    if (rows.length !== 1) throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.notFound);
  }
}

export async function lockPaymentRow(store: Pick<Db, "$queryRaw">, id: string): Promise<void> {
  const rows = await store.$queryRaw<{ id: string }[]>`
    select "id" from "payments" where "id" = ${id}::uuid for update`;
  if (rows.length !== 1) throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.paymentNotFound);
}

export async function lockReportRow(store: Pick<Db, "$queryRaw">, id: string): Promise<void> {
  const rows = await store.$queryRaw<{ id: string }[]>`
    select "id" from "payment_reports" where "id" = ${id}::uuid for update`;
  if (rows.length !== 1) throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.reportNotFound);
}

/** The money allocated to each invoice from payments that are not voided. Missing ids mean 0. */
export async function allocatedByInvoice(
  store: Pick<Db, "paymentAllocation">,
  invoiceIds: readonly string[],
): Promise<Map<string, number>> {
  const map = new Map<string, number>();
  if (invoiceIds.length === 0) return map;
  const rows = await store.paymentAllocation.findMany({
    where: { invoiceId: { in: [...new Set(invoiceIds)] }, payment: { voidedAt: null } },
    select: { invoiceId: true, amountCents: true },
  });
  for (const r of rows) map.set(r.invoiceId, (map.get(r.invoiceId) ?? 0) + r.amountCents);
  return map;
}

/** The money already allocated from each payment. Missing ids mean 0. */
export async function allocatedByPayment(
  store: Pick<Db, "paymentAllocation">,
  paymentIds: readonly string[],
): Promise<Map<string, number>> {
  const map = new Map<string, number>();
  if (paymentIds.length === 0) return map;
  const rows = await store.paymentAllocation.findMany({
    where: { paymentId: { in: [...new Set(paymentIds)] } },
    select: { paymentId: true, amountCents: true },
  });
  for (const r of rows) map.set(r.paymentId, (map.get(r.paymentId) ?? 0) + r.amountCents);
  return map;
}

/** Who pays: the managing agency when "Bills go to" the agency is on, else the customer itself. */
export function billToIdFor(c: { id: string; managedById: string | null; billsToManager: boolean }): string {
  return c.managedById && c.billsToManager ? c.managedById : c.id;
}

/** True when the database refused a second live monthly invoice for the same customer and month. */
export function isMonthlyDuplicate(error: unknown): boolean {
  if (typeof error !== "object" || error === null) return false;
  const { code, message } = error as { code?: unknown; message?: unknown };
  return (
    code === "P2002" ||
    (typeof message === "string" && message.includes("invoices_monthly_once_per_month"))
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `packages/core/src/invoices/settings.ts`**

```ts
import { recordAudit, staffParty } from "../audit";
import { assertRole, DomainError, MANAGEMENT, OWNER_ONLY, type Actor } from "../customers/access";
import { formatCustomerNumber } from "../customers/number";
import { changedFields, findEditableCustomer, lockCustomer, logCustomerEvent } from "../customers/store";
import type { Db } from "../db";
import { readQuoteWording } from "../quotes/wording";
import { BILLING_TIMINGS, type BillingTiming } from "./labels";
import { bankDetailsReady, type InvoiceSettingsInput, type InvoiceSnapshot } from "./schemas";

const SETTINGS_SELECT = {
  bankName: true,
  accountHolder: true,
  accountNumber: true,
  branchCode: true,
  accountType: true,
  onceOffDueDays: true,
  lateFeeCents: true,
  updatedAt: true,
} as const;

export type InvoiceSettingsRow = {
  bankName: string;
  accountHolder: string;
  accountNumber: string;
  branchCode: string;
  accountType: string;
  onceOffDueDays: number;
  lateFeeCents: number;
  updatedAt: Date;
};

/** Reads the one settings row. No role check: for use inside core functions only (not in the barrel). */
export async function readInvoiceSettings(
  store: Pick<Db, "invoiceSettings">,
): Promise<InvoiceSettingsRow> {
  return store.invoiceSettings.findUniqueOrThrow({
    where: { id: "singleton" },
    select: SETTINGS_SELECT,
  });
}

/** Owner only: the settings page. */
export async function getInvoiceSettings(
  store: Pick<Db, "invoiceSettings">,
  actor: Actor,
): Promise<InvoiceSettingsRow> {
  assertRole(actor, OWNER_ONLY);
  return readInvoiceSettings(store);
}

/** What managers need to know without seeing the bank details. */
export async function invoiceSettingsStatus(
  store: Pick<Db, "invoiceSettings">,
  actor: Actor,
): Promise<{ bankReady: boolean; onceOffDueDays: number; lateFeeCents: number }> {
  assertRole(actor, MANAGEMENT);
  const s = await readInvoiceSettings(store);
  return { bankReady: bankDetailsReady(s), onceOffDueDays: s.onceOffDueDays, lateFeeCents: s.lateFeeCents };
}

/**
 * Owner only. Changes apply to invoices issued afterwards; issued invoices keep their frozen copy. A save that
 * changes nothing writes nothing. The audit line names the changed fields only, never the values.
 */
export async function updateInvoiceSettings(
  store: Pick<Db, "invoiceSettings" | "auditLog">,
  actor: Actor,
  input: InvoiceSettingsInput,
): Promise<void> {
  assertRole(actor, OWNER_ONLY);
  const current = await readInvoiceSettings(store);
  const before: Record<string, unknown> = {
    bankName: current.bankName,
    accountHolder: current.accountHolder,
    accountNumber: current.accountNumber,
    branchCode: current.branchCode,
    accountType: current.accountType,
    onceOffDueDays: current.onceOffDueDays,
    lateFeeCents: current.lateFeeCents,
  };
  const changed = changedFields(before, input);
  if (changed.length === 0) return;
  await store.invoiceSettings.update({
    where: { id: "singleton" },
    data: { ...input, updatedById: actor.id },
    select: { id: true },
  });
  const party = staffParty(actor);
  await recordAudit(store, {
    actorType: party.actorType,
    actorId: actor.id,
    action: "invoice_settings.updated",
    entityType: "invoice_settings",
    entityId: "singleton",
    after: { changed },
    ...(party.ip ? { ip: party.ip } : {}),
    ...(party.userAgent ? { userAgent: party.userAgent } : {}),
  });
}

/** The Pre or Post switch on a customer. Owner or manager. A choice that is already set writes nothing. */
export async function setBillingTiming(
  store: Pick<Db, "customer" | "auditLog">,
  actor: Actor,
  customerId: string,
  timing: BillingTiming,
): Promise<void> {
  assertRole(actor, MANAGEMENT);
  if (!BILLING_TIMINGS.includes(timing)) throw new DomainError("Choose Pre or Post.");
  if ((await findEditableCustomer(store, customerId)).billingTiming === timing) return;
  await lockCustomer(store, customerId);
  if ((await findEditableCustomer(store, customerId)).billingTiming === timing) return;
  await store.customer.update({
    where: { id: customerId },
    data: { billingTiming: timing },
    select: { id: true },
  });
  await logCustomerEvent(store, actor, "customer.billing_timing_changed", customerId, {
    billingTiming: timing,
  });
}

/** ACE's details as printed on an invoice. No role check: core use only (not in the barrel). */
export async function buildInvoiceSnapshot(
  store: Pick<Db, "organisationSettings" | "quoteWording">,
  settings: InvoiceSettingsRow,
  billToNumber: number,
): Promise<InvoiceSnapshot> {
  const org = await store.organisationSettings.findUniqueOrThrow({
    where: { id: "singleton" },
    select: { legalName: true, tradingName: true },
  });
  const wording = await readQuoteWording(store);
  return {
    bank: {
      bankName: settings.bankName,
      accountHolder: settings.accountHolder,
      accountNumber: settings.accountNumber,
      branchCode: settings.branchCode,
      accountType: settings.accountType,
    },
    business: {
      legalName: org.legalName,
      tradingName: org.tradingName,
      registrationNumber: wording.registrationNumber,
      phone: wording.phone,
      email: wording.email,
      website: wording.website,
    },
    paymentReference: formatCustomerNumber(billToNumber),
  };
}
```

```ts
import { randomInt, randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import type { Prisma } from "../generated/prisma/client";
import type { TxRunner } from "../portal/access";
import { fromDateString } from "../quotes/reference";

export { crew, manager, owner } from "../quotes/test-helpers";

type Tx = Prisma.TransactionClient;

/** A short unique tag for made-up names. */
export const tag = () => randomUUID().replaceAll("-", "").slice(0, 8);

/**
 * A made-up customer with one contact who receives invoices and one property, written directly (no quote
 * flow). Pass `email: null` for a contact without an email.
 */
export async function makeBillingCustomer(
  tx: Tx,
  opts: {
    status?: "LEAD" | "ACTIVE" | "PAUSED" | "ARCHIVED";
    billingTiming?: "PRE" | "POST";
    managedById?: string;
    billsToManager?: boolean;
    email?: string | null;
    name?: string;
  } = {},
) {
  const t = tag();
  const created = await tx.customer.create({
    data: {
      type: "COMPANY",
      displayName: opts.name ?? `ZZ E2E Invoice ${t}`,
      status: opts.status ?? "ACTIVE",
      billingTiming: opts.billingTiming ?? "PRE",
      billingLine1: "1 Test Road",
      billingSuburb: "Testville",
      ...(opts.status === "ARCHIVED" ? { archivedAt: new Date() } : {}),
      ...(opts.managedById
        ? { managedById: opts.managedById, billsToManager: opts.billsToManager ?? true }
        : {}),
      contacts: {
        create: [
          {
            name: `ZZ E2E Contact ${t}`,
            email: opts.email === undefined ? `zz.${t}@example.com` : opts.email,
            phone: opts.email === null ? "+27820000000" : null,
            receivesInvoices: true,
          },
        ],
      },
      properties: { create: [{ label: `Site ${t}`, addressLine1: "1 Test Road" }] },
    },
    select: {
      id: true,
      number: true,
      displayName: true,
      properties: { select: { id: true, label: true } },
    },
  });
  const property = created.properties[0];
  if (!property) throw new Error("The test property was not created.");
  return {
    customerId: created.id,
    number: created.number,
    displayName: created.displayName,
    propertyId: property.id,
    propertyLabel: property.label,
  };
}

/** A second property on a customer. */
export async function addProperty(tx: Tx, customerId: string, label = `Site ${tag()}`): Promise<string> {
  const p = await tx.property.create({
    data: { customerId, label, addressLine1: "2 Test Road" },
    select: { id: true },
  });
  return p.id;
}

export type TestQuoteLine = {
  lineKind: "PRICED" | "OPTIONAL" | "INCLUDED";
  billing: "MONTHLY" | "ONCE_OFF" | null;
  frequency: "WEEKLY" | "FORTNIGHTLY" | "MONTHLY" | "AS_REQUIRED" | null;
  description: string;
  amountCents: number | null;
  chosen: boolean;
};

export const serviceLine = (amountCents: number, description = "Weekly garden maintenance"): TestQuoteLine => ({
  lineKind: "PRICED",
  billing: "MONTHLY",
  frequency: "WEEKLY",
  description,
  amountCents,
  chosen: false,
});

export const optionalLine = (amountCents: number, chosen: boolean, description = "Weed treatment"): TestQuoteLine => ({
  lineKind: "OPTIONAL",
  billing: "MONTHLY",
  frequency: "AS_REQUIRED",
  description,
  amountCents,
  chosen,
});

export const onceOffQuoteLine = (amountCents: number, description = "Once-off clean-up"): TestQuoteLine => ({
  lineKind: "PRICED",
  billing: "ONCE_OFF",
  frequency: null,
  description,
  amountCents,
  chosen: false,
});

/**
 * An ACCEPTED quote with these lines, written straight to the database: no issue flow and no quote number
 * counter (year 2099 and a random number, so it never meets a real number).
 */
export async function makeAcceptedQuote(
  tx: Tx,
  input: {
    customerId: string;
    propertyId: string;
    startDate: string;
    lines: TestQuoteLine[];
    acceptedAt?: Date;
    agreementEnded?: boolean;
  },
) {
  const quote = await tx.quote.create({
    data: {
      customerId: input.customerId,
      propertyId: input.propertyId,
      status: "ACCEPTED",
      numberYear: 2099,
      numberSeq: randomInt(1, 2_000_000_000),
      validUntil: fromDateString("2099-12-31"),
      issuedAt: new Date(),
      acceptedAt: input.acceptedAt ?? new Date(),
      acceptedMethod: "PHONE",
      acceptedByName: "ZZ E2E Acceptor",
      acceptedStartDate: fromDateString(input.startDate),
      ...(input.agreementEnded ? { agreementEndedAt: new Date() } : {}),
      lines: {
        create: input.lines.map((l, index) => ({
          sortOrder: index + 1,
          lineKind: l.lineKind,
          billing: l.billing,
          frequency: l.frequency,
          description: l.description,
          amountCents: l.amountCents,
          chosen: l.chosen,
        })),
      },
    },
    select: { id: true, lines: { orderBy: { sortOrder: "asc" }, select: { id: true } } },
  });
  return { quoteId: quote.id, lineIds: quote.lines.map((l) => l.id) };
}

/** An ISSUED invoice written straight to the database (year 2099, a random number: no counter). */
export async function makeIssuedInvoice(
  tx: Tx,
  input: {
    customerId: string;
    billToCustomerId?: string;
    totalCents?: number;
    dueDate: string;
    issuedOn?: string;
    kind?: "MONTHLY" | "ONCE_OFF";
    serviceMonth?: string;
  },
): Promise<string> {
  const kind = input.kind ?? "ONCE_OFF";
  const total = input.totalCents ?? 100000;
  const inv = await tx.invoice.create({
    data: {
      customerId: input.customerId,
      billToCustomerId: input.billToCustomerId ?? input.customerId,
      kind,
      serviceMonth: kind === "MONTHLY" ? fromDateString(`${input.serviceMonth ?? "2099-01"}-01`) : null,
      status: "ISSUED",
      numberYear: 2099,
      numberSeq: randomInt(1, 2_000_000_000),
      dueDate: fromDateString(input.dueDate),
      issuedAt: input.issuedOn ? fromDateString(input.issuedOn) : new Date(),
      totalCents: total,
      recipientName: "ZZ E2E Recipient",
      recipientAddress: "1 Test Road, Testville",
      lines: {
        create: [
          {
            sortOrder: 1,
            kind: kind === "MONTHLY" ? "SERVICE" : "ONCE_OFF",
            description: "ZZ E2E work",
            amountCents: total,
          },
        ],
      },
    },
    select: { id: true },
  });
  return inv.id;
}

/** A payment written straight to the database, optionally allocated and optionally voided. */
export async function makePayment(
  tx: Tx,
  input: {
    payerCustomerId: string;
    amountCents: number;
    receivedOn: string;
    allocations?: { invoiceId: string; amountCents: number }[];
    voided?: boolean;
    reference?: string;
  },
): Promise<string> {
  const p = await tx.payment.create({
    data: {
      payerCustomerId: input.payerCustomerId,
      amountCents: input.amountCents,
      receivedOn: fromDateString(input.receivedOn),
      recordedById: randomUUID(),
      reference: input.reference ?? null,
      ...(input.voided ? { voidedAt: new Date(), voidReason: "ZZ E2E test" } : {}),
      allocations: { create: input.allocations ?? [] },
    },
    select: { id: true },
  });
  return p.id;
}

/** Made-up bank details on the settings row (rolled back with the test). */
export async function fillBankDetails(tx: Tx): Promise<void> {
  await tx.invoiceSettings.update({
    where: { id: "singleton" },
    data: {
      bankName: "ZZ Test Bank",
      accountHolder: "ACE Garden Services",
      accountNumber: "1234567890",
      branchCode: "250655",
      accountType: "Business cheque",
    },
  });
}

/** A runner that keeps everything inside the test's rolled-back transaction. */
export function sameTx(tx: Tx): TxRunner {
  return <T>(fn: (t: Prisma.TransactionClient) => Promise<T>) => fn(tx);
}

/** The audit lines written about one record, oldest first. */
export function auditFor(tx: Tx, entityType: string, entityId: string) {
  return tx.auditLog.findMany({
    where: { entityType, entityId },
    orderBy: { at: "asc" },
    select: { action: true, after: true, actorId: true },
  });
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { DomainError, ForbiddenError } from "../customers/access";
import { dbTestsEnabled, withRollback } from "../testing/rollback";
import {
  getInvoiceSettings,
  invoiceSettingsStatus,
  setBillingTiming,
  updateInvoiceSettings,
} from "./settings";
import { auditFor, crew, makeBillingCustomer, manager, owner } from "./test-helpers";

const INPUT = {
  bankName: "ZZ Test Bank",
  accountHolder: "ACE Garden Services",
  accountNumber: "9876543210",
  branchCode: "250655",
  accountType: "Business cheque",
  onceOffDueDays: 10,
  lateFeeCents: 15000,
};

describe.skipIf(!dbTestsEnabled)("invoice settings", () => {
  it("only the owner can read and change them; managers see whether bank details are ready", async () => {
    await withRollback(async (tx) => {
      await expect(getInvoiceSettings(tx, manager)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ForbiddenError);
      await expect(updateInvoiceSettings(tx, manager, INPUT)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ForbiddenError);
      await expect(invoiceSettingsStatus(tx, crew)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ForbiddenError);
      await updateInvoiceSettings(tx, owner, INPUT);
      const row = await getInvoiceSettings(tx, owner);
      expect(row).toMatchObject(INPUT);
      expect(await invoiceSettingsStatus(tx, manager)).toEqual({
        bankReady: true,
        onceOffDueDays: 10,
        lateFeeCents: 15000,
      });
    });
  });

  it("records the changed field names only, and an unchanged save writes nothing", async () => {
    await withRollback(async (tx) => {
      await updateInvoiceSettings(tx, owner, INPUT);
      await updateInvoiceSettings(tx, owner, INPUT);
      await updateInvoiceSettings(tx, owner, { ...INPUT, lateFeeCents: 20000 });
      const lines = (await auditFor(tx, "invoice_settings", "singleton")).filter(
        (l) => l.actorId === owner.id,
      );
      expect(lines.map((l) => l.action)).toEqual(["invoice_settings.updated", "invoice_settings.updated"]);
      expect(lines[1]?.after).toEqual({ changed: ["lateFeeCents"] });
      const text = JSON.stringify(lines);
      expect(text).not.toContain("9876543210");
      expect(text).not.toContain("ZZ Test Bank");
    });
  });
});

      await expect(setBillingTiming(tx, manager, c.customerId, "POST")).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(
        DomainError,

});
```

### Task 6: Drafts: once-off drafts, saving, deleting, reading and the lists

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/core/src/invoices/drafts.ts`, `drafts.db.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/core/src/invoices/index.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 4 (`fitDescription`, `invoiceDisplayStatus`, `sumLineCents`, `formatInvoiceReference`, `parseInvoiceSearch`, `NEW_LINE_KINDS`, `DraftWarning`); Task 5 (`InvoiceStore`, `INVOICE_MESSAGES`, `InvoiceDraftConflictError`, `allocatedByInvoice`, `billToIdFor`, `lockInvoiceRows`, `logInvoiceEvent`, `InvoiceDraftInput`; test helpers); `findEditableCustomer`, `lockCustomer` (`customers/store.ts`); `formatQuoteReference`, `fromDateString`, `toDateString`, `todaySA` (`quotes/reference.ts`).
- Produces:
  - `ONCE_OFF_ACCEPTED: Prisma.QuoteLineWhereInput` (a priced once-off line, or a chosen optional once-off line)
  - `listAcceptedOnceOffQuotes(store, actor, customerId): Promise<{ id: string; reference: string; propertyLabel: string; acceptedAt: Date | null; onceOffCents: number; alreadyInvoiced: boolean }[]>`
  - `createOnceOffDraft(store, actor, input: { customerId: string; fromQuoteId?: string | null }): Promise<{ id: string }>`
  - `saveInvoiceDraft(store, actor, id, input: InvoiceDraftInput): Promise<{ version: number }>`
  - `deleteInvoiceDraft(store, actor, id): Promise<void>`
  - `getInvoice(store, actor, id)` -> the invoice with `reference`, `sourceQuoteReference`, `totalCents` (stored total, or the line sum for a draft), `allocatedCents`, `balanceCents` (0 unless issued), `displayStatus`, `dueDateString`, `serviceMonthString` (`"YYYY-MM"` or null), `billTo` (for a draft: the bill-to as it would be resolved now), `lines`, `allocations` (each with its payment), `reports`, `customer`; or null. `type InvoiceView = NonNullable<Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getInvoice>>>`.
  - `INVOICE_FILTERS = ["DRAFT","UNPAID","OVERDUE","PAID","VOID"] as const`; `type InvoiceListQuery = { status?: string; q?: string; page?: number; pageSize?: number }`
  - `type InvoiceListItem = { id; reference: string | null; kind: "MONTHLY" | "ONCE_OFF"; status: "DRAFT" | "ISSUED" | "VOID"; displayStatus: InvoiceDisplayStatus; customer: { id; number; displayName }; billTo: { id; number; displayName }; serviceMonth: string | null; dueDate: string | null; issuedAt: Date | null; totalCents: number; balanceCents: number; emailStatus: "NONE" | "SENT" | "FAILED" }`
  - `listInvoices(store, actor, query): Promise<{ items: InvoiceListItem[]; total: number; page: number; pageSize: number }>`
  - `type DraftReviewItem = { id; kind; customer: { id; number; displayName }; billTo: { id; number; displayName }; serviceMonth: string | null; dueDate: string | null; totalCents: number; lineCount: number; warnings: DraftWarning[] }`; `listDraftsForReview(store, actor): Promise<DraftReviewItem[]>`
  - `listCustomerInvoices(store, actor, customerId): Promise<{ items: InvoiceListItem[]; owedCents: number; creditCents: number; payments: { id; receivedOn: string; amountCents: number; reference: string | null; voided: boolean; unallocatedCents: number }[] }>`

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { DomainError, ForbiddenError } from "../customers/access";
import { fromDateString } from "../quotes/reference";
import { dbTestsEnabled, withRollback } from "../testing/rollback";
import {
  createOnceOffDraft,
  deleteInvoiceDraft,
  getInvoice,
  listCustomerInvoices,
  listDraftsForReview,
  listInvoices,
  saveInvoiceDraft,
} from "./drafts";
import { InvoiceDraftConflictError } from "./store";
import {
  auditFor,
  crew,
  makeAcceptedQuote,
  makeBillingCustomer,
  makeIssuedInvoice,
  makePayment,
  manager,
  onceOffQuoteLine,
  optionalLine,
  serviceLine,
} from "./test-helpers";

describe.skipIf(!dbTestsEnabled)("once-off drafts", () => {
  it("pre-fills from an accepted quote's once-off work, labelled with the property, billed to the agency", async () => {
    await withRollback(async (tx) => {
      const agency = await makeBillingCustomer(tx);
      const complex = await makeBillingCustomer(tx, { managedById: agency.customerId });
      const quote = await makeAcceptedQuote(tx, {
        customerId: complex.customerId,
        propertyId: complex.propertyId,
        startDate: "2026-12-01",
        lines: [
          serviceLine(115000),
          onceOffQuoteLine(240000),
          { ...optionalLine(30000, true, "Tree trim"), billing: "ONCE_OFF", frequency: null },
          { ...optionalLine(50000, false, "Not chosen"), billing: "ONCE_OFF", frequency: null },
        ],
      });
      await expect(
        createOnceOffDraft(tx, crew, { customerId: complex.customerId }),
      ).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ForbiddenError);
      const { id } = await createOnceOffDraft(tx, manager, {
        customerId: complex.customerId,
        fromQuoteId: quote.quoteId,
      });
      const inv = await getInvoice(tx, manager, id);
      expect(inv?.status).toBe("DRAFT");
      expect(inv?.kind).toBe("ONCE_OFF");
      expect(inv?.billTo.id).toBe(agency.customerId);
      expect(inv?.sourceQuoteId).toBe(quote.quoteId);
      expect(inv?.dueDateString).toBeNull();
      expect(inv?.lines.map((l) => [l.kind, l.description, l.amountCents])).toEqual([
        ["ONCE_OFF", `${complex.propertyLabel}: Once-off clean-up`, 240000],
        ["ONCE_OFF", `${complex.propertyLabel}: Tree trim`, 30000],
      ]);
      expect(inv?.totalCents).toBe(270000);
      const audit = await auditFor(tx, "invoice", id);
      expect(audit.map((a) => a.action)).toEqual(["invoice.drafted"]);
      expect(audit[0]?.after).toEqual({ kind: "ONCE_OFF", fromQuote: true, lineCount: 2 });
      await expect(
        createOnceOffDraft(tx, manager, { customerId: complex.customerId, fromQuoteId: quote.quoteId }),
      ).rejects.toThrow("This quote has already been invoiced.");
    });
  });

  it("refuses another customer's quote, and an archived customer", async () => {
    await withRollback(async (tx) => {
      const a = await makeBillingCustomer(tx);
      const b = await makeBillingCustomer(tx);
      const quote = await makeAcceptedQuote(tx, {
        customerId: a.customerId,
        propertyId: a.propertyId,
        startDate: "2026-12-01",
        lines: [onceOffQuoteLine(1000)],
      });
      await expect(
        createOnceOffDraft(tx, manager, { customerId: b.customerId, fromQuoteId: quote.quoteId }),
      ).rejects.toThrow("Only an accepted quote of this customer can be invoiced.");
      const archived = await makeBillingCustomer(tx, { status: "ARCHIVED" });
      await expect(
        createOnceOffDraft(tx, manager, { customerId: archived.customerId }),
      ).rejects.toThrow("Restore this customer before invoicing.");
    });
  });
});

describe.skipIf(!dbTestsEnabled)("saving and deleting drafts", () => {
  it("guards the version, keeps a late fee's target, adds and removes lines, and skips an unchanged save", async () => {
    await withRollback(async (tx) => {
      const c = await makeBillingCustomer(tx);
      const overdue = await makeIssuedInvoice(tx, { customerId: c.customerId, dueDate: "2026-01-31" });
      const draft = await tx.invoice.create({
        data: {
          customerId: c.customerId,
          billToCustomerId: c.customerId,
          kind: "MONTHLY",
          serviceMonth: fromDateString("2099-06-01"),
          dueDate: fromDateString("2099-05-31"),
          lines: {
            create: [
              { sortOrder: 1, kind: "SERVICE", description: "Main: Garden", amountCents: 115000 },
              { sortOrder: 2, kind: "LATE_FEE", description: "Late payment fee", amountCents: 10000, forInvoiceId: overdue },
            ],
          },
        },
        select: { id: true, lines: { orderBy: { sortOrder: "asc" }, select: { id: true } } },
      });
      const [serviceId, feeId] = draft.lines.map((l) => l.id);
      const lines = [
        { id: serviceId ?? null, kind: "SERVICE" as const, description: "Main: Garden", amountCents: 115000 },
        { id: feeId ?? null, kind: "LATE_FEE" as const, description: "Late payment fee", amountCents: 10000 },
      ];
      const same = await saveInvoiceDraft(tx, manager, draft.id, { version: 1, dueDate: "2099-05-31", lines });
      expect(same.version).toBe(1);

      const saved = await saveInvoiceDraft(tx, manager, draft.id, {
        version: 1,
        dueDate: "2099-05-30",
        lines: [
          lines[1]!,
          { id: null, kind: "ADJUSTMENT", description: "Discount", amountCents: -5000 },
        ],
      });
      expect(saved.version).toBe(2);
      const inv = await getInvoice(tx, manager, draft.id);
      expect(inv?.dueDateString).toBe("2099-05-30");
      expect(inv?.lines.map((l) => [l.kind, l.amountCents, l.forInvoiceId])).toEqual([
        ["LATE_FEE", 10000, overdue],
        ["ADJUSTMENT", -5000, null],
      ]);
      expect(inv?.totalCents).toBe(5000);

      await expect(
        saveInvoiceDraft(tx, manager, draft.id, { version: 1, dueDate: "2099-05-30", lines: [] }),
      ).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(InvoiceDraftConflictError);
      await expect(
        saveInvoiceDraft(tx, manager, draft.id, {
          version: 2,
          dueDate: "2099-05-30",
          lines: [{ id: null, kind: "LATE_FEE", description: "Fee", amountCents: 100 }],
        }),
      ).rejects.toThrow("Choose service, adjustment or once-off work for a new line.");
      await expect(
        saveInvoiceDraft(tx, manager, draft.id, {
          version: 2,
          dueDate: "2099-05-30",
          lines: [{ id: null, kind: "SERVICE", description: "Garden", amountCents: -100 }],
        }),
      ).rejects.toThrow("Only an adjustment can be negative.");
      await expect(
        saveInvoiceDraft(tx, manager, draft.id, { version: 2, dueDate: null, lines: [] }),
      ).rejects.toThrow("A monthly invoice needs a due date.");
      // Draft saves are not audited.
      expect(await auditFor(tx, "invoice", draft.id)).toEqual([]);
    });
  });

  it("refuses to change or delete an issued invoice, and deletes a draft with its lines", async () => {
    await withRollback(async (tx) => {
      const c = await makeBillingCustomer(tx);
      const issued = await makeIssuedInvoice(tx, { customerId: c.customerId, dueDate: "2099-01-31" });
      await expect(
        saveInvoiceDraft(tx, manager, issued, { version: 1, dueDate: null, lines: [] }),
      ).rejects.toThrow("Only a draft can be changed.");
      await expect(deleteInvoiceDraft(tx, manager, issued)).rejects.toThrow("Only a draft can be deleted.");
      const { id } = await createOnceOffDraft(tx, manager, { customerId: c.customerId });
      await saveInvoiceDraft(tx, manager, id, {
        version: 1,
        dueDate: null,
        lines: [{ id: null, kind: "ONCE_OFF", description: "Clean-up", amountCents: 50000 }],
      });
      await deleteInvoiceDraft(tx, manager, id);
      expect(await tx.invoice.findUnique({ where: { id } })).toBeNull();
      expect(await tx.invoiceLine.count({ where: { invoiceId: id } })).toBe(0);
      expect((await auditFor(tx, "invoice", id)).map((a) => a.action)).toEqual([
        "invoice.drafted",
        "invoice.deleted_draft",
      ]);
    });
  });
});

      expect(overdueList.items.map((i) => i.id)).toEqual([overdue]);
      const paidList = await listInvoices(tx, manager, { status: "PAID", q: c.displayName });
      expect(paidList.items.map((i) => i.id)).toEqual([paid]);

      const mine = await listCustomerInvoices(tx, manager, c.customerId);
      expect(mine.items.map((i) => i.id).sort()).toEqual([overdue, paid].sort());
      expect(mine.owedCents).toBe(70000);
      expect(mine.creditCents).toBe(0);
      expect(mine.payments.map((p) => p.voided).sort()).toEqual([false, true]);
      await expect(listInvoices(tx, crew, {})).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ForbiddenError);
    });
  });

  it("warns on drafts: no email, first month, late fee, billed to the agency", async () => {
    await withRollback(async (tx) => {
      const agency = await makeBillingCustomer(tx, { email: null });
      const complex = await makeBillingCustomer(tx, { managedById: agency.customerId });
      const earlier = await makeIssuedInvoice(tx, {
        customerId: complex.customerId,
        billToCustomerId: agency.customerId,
        dueDate: "2026-01-31",
        kind: "MONTHLY",
        serviceMonth: "2099-01",
      });
      const second = await tx.invoice.create({
        data: {
          customerId: complex.customerId,
          billToCustomerId: complex.customerId,
          kind: "MONTHLY",
          serviceMonth: fromDateString("2099-02-01"),
          dueDate: fromDateString("2099-01-31"),
          lines: {
            create: [
              { sortOrder: 1, kind: "SERVICE", description: "Garden", amountCents: 1000 },
              { sortOrder: 2, kind: "LATE_FEE", description: "Late payment fee", amountCents: 10000, forInvoiceId: earlier },
            ],
          },
        },
        select: { id: true },
      });
      const own = await makeBillingCustomer(tx);
      const first = await tx.invoice.create({
        data: {
          customerId: own.customerId,
          billToCustomerId: own.customerId,
          kind: "MONTHLY",
          serviceMonth: fromDateString("2099-02-01"),
          dueDate: fromDateString("2099-01-31"),
          lines: { create: [{ sortOrder: 1, kind: "SERVICE", description: "Garden", amountCents: 1000 }] },
        },
        select: { id: true },
      });
      const drafts = await listDraftsForReview(tx, manager);
      const a = drafts.find((d) => d.id === second.id);
      const b = drafts.find((d) => d.id === first.id);
      // the draft was stored as billed to the complex, but the complex's bills go to the agency now
      expect(a?.billTo.id).toBe(agency.customerId);
      expect(a?.warnings.sort()).toEqual(["billToAgency", "lateFee", "noEmail"]);
      expect(a?.totalCents).toBe(11000);
      expect(b?.warnings).toEqual(["firstMonth"]);
    });
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write `packages/core/src/invoices/drafts.ts`**

```ts
import { assertRole, DomainError, MANAGEMENT, type Actor } from "../customers/access";
import { findEditableCustomer, lockCustomer } from "../customers/store";
import type { Prisma } from "../generated/prisma/client";
import { formatQuoteReference, fromDateString, toDateString, todaySA } from "../quotes/reference";
import { NEW_LINE_KINDS, type DraftWarning, type InvoiceDisplayStatus } from "./labels";
import { fitDescription, invoiceDisplayStatus, sumLineCents } from "./money";
import { formatInvoiceReference, parseInvoiceSearch } from "./reference";
import type { InvoiceDraftInput } from "./schemas";
import {
  allocatedByInvoice,
  allocatedByPayment,
  billToIdFor,
  INVOICE_MESSAGES,
  InvoiceDraftConflictError,
  lockInvoiceRows,
  logInvoiceEvent,
  type InvoiceStore,
} from "./store";

/** An accepted once-off line: priced, or an optional one the client chose. */
export const ONCE_OFF_ACCEPTED: Prisma.QuoteLineWhereInput = {
  billing: "ONCE_OFF",
  OR: [{ lineKind: "PRICED" }, { lineKind: "OPTIONAL", chosen: true }],
};

const REFRESH = "Refresh the page and try again.";

/** The customer's accepted quotes with once-off work, for the New invoice page. */
export async function listAcceptedOnceOffQuotes(store: InvoiceStore, actor: Actor, customerId: string) {
  assertRole(actor, MANAGEMENT);
  const quotes = await store.quote.findMany({
    where: { customerId, status: "ACCEPTED", lines: { some: ONCE_OFF_ACCEPTED } },
    orderBy: [{ acceptedAt: "desc" }, { id: "desc" }],
    select: {
      id: true,
      numberYear: true,
      numberSeq: true,
      revision: true,
      acceptedAt: true,
      property: { select: { label: true } },
      lines: { where: ONCE_OFF_ACCEPTED, select: { amountCents: true } },
      invoices: { where: { status: { not: "VOID" } }, select: { id: true } },
    },
  });
  return quotes.map((q) => ({
    id: q.id,
    reference: formatQuoteReference(q) ?? "",
    propertyLabel: q.property.label,
    acceptedAt: q.acceptedAt,
    onceOffCents: q.lines.reduce((t, l) => t + (l.amountCents ?? 0), 0),
    alreadyInvoiced: q.invoices.length > 0,
  }));
}

/**
 * "New invoice" on a customer: a once-off draft, empty or pre-filled from an accepted quote's priced once-off
 * lines and chosen once-off options. Lock: the customer. The due date stays empty (the default applies at issue).
 */
export async function createOnceOffDraft(
  store: InvoiceStore,
  actor: Actor,
  input: { customerId: string; fromQuoteId?: string | null },
): Promise<{ id: string }> {
  assertRole(actor, MANAGEMENT);
  const readCustomer = async () => {
    const customer = await findEditableCustomer(store, input.customerId);
    if (customer.status === "ARCHIVED") throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.archived);
    return customer;
  };
  const readQuote = async () => {
    if (!input.fromQuoteId) return null;
    const quote = await store.quote.findUnique({
      where: { id: input.fromQuoteId },
      select: {
        id: true,
        customerId: true,
        status: true,
        property: { select: { id: true, label: true } },
        lines: {
          where: ONCE_OFF_ACCEPTED,
          orderBy: { sortOrder: "asc" },
          select: { id: true, description: true, amountCents: true },
        },
      },
    });
    if (!quote || quote.customerId !== input.customerId || quote.status !== "ACCEPTED") {
      throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.quoteNotAccepted);
    }
    const lines = quote.lines.filter((l) => (l.amountCents ?? 0) > 0);
    if (lines.length === 0) throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.quoteNoOnceOff);
    const invoiced = await store.invoice.count({
      where: { sourceQuoteId: quote.id, status: { not: "VOID" } },
    });
    if (invoiced > 0) throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.quoteInvoiced);
    return { id: quote.id, property: quote.property, lines };
  };

  await readCustomer();
  await readQuote();
  await lockCustomer(store, input.customerId);
  const customer = await readCustomer();
  const quote = await readQuote();
  const lines = quote
    ? quote.lines.map((l, index) => ({
        sortOrder: index + 1,
        kind: "ONCE_OFF" as const,
        propertyId: quote.property.id,
        description: fitDescription(`${quote.property.label}: ${l.description}`),
        amountCents: l.amountCents ?? 0,
        sourceQuoteLineId: l.id,
      }))
    : [];
  const created = await store.invoice.create({
    data: {
      customerId: customer.id,
      billToCustomerId: billToIdFor(customer),
      kind: "ONCE_OFF",
      createdById: actor.id,
      sourceQuoteId: quote?.id ?? null,
      lines: { create: lines },
    },
    select: { id: true },
  });
  await logInvoiceEvent(store, actor, "invoice.drafted", created.id, {
    kind: "ONCE_OFF",
    fromQuote: quote !== null,
    lineCount: lines.length,
  });
  return created;
}

const DRAFT_SELECT = {
  id: true,
  status: true,
  kind: true,
  customerId: true,
  draftVersion: true,
  dueDate: true,
  lines: {
    orderBy: { sortOrder: "asc" },
    select: {
      id: true,
      kind: true,
      description: true,
      amountCents: true,
      propertyId: true,
      sourceQuoteLineId: true,
      forInvoiceId: true,
    },
  },
} as const satisfies Prisma.InvoiceSelect;

/**
 * Saves the whole draft in one go: the due date and every line. Existing lines keep their kind, property,
 * quote line and late-fee target; new lines may be Service, Adjustment or Once-off work. `input.version` must
 * be the version the browser last saw. A save that changes nothing writes nothing. Drafts are not audited.
 * Lock: the customer, then the invoice.
 */
export async function saveInvoiceDraft(
  store: InvoiceStore,
  actor: Actor,
  id: string,
  input: InvoiceDraftInput,
): Promise<{ version: number }> {
  assertRole(actor, MANAGEMENT);
  const read = async () => {
    const inv = await store.invoice.findUnique({ where: { id }, select: DRAFT_SELECT });
    if (!inv) throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.notFound);
    if (inv.status !== "DRAFT") throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.onlyDraftChange);
    if (inv.draftVersion !== input.version) throw new InvoiceDraftConflictError();
    return inv;
  };
  const check = (inv: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof read>>) => {
    if (inv.kind === "MONTHLY" && input.dueDate === null) {
      throw new DomainError("A monthly invoice needs a due date.");
    }
    const existing = new Map(inv.lines.map((l) => [l.id, l]));
    const seen = new Set<string>();
    for (const line of input.lines) {
      if (line.id !== null) {
        const old = existing.get(line.id);
        if (!old || seen.has(line.id) || old.kind !== line.kind) throw new DomainError(REFRESH);
        seen.add(line.id);
      } else if (!NEW_LINE_KINDS.some((k) => k === line.kind)) {
        throw new DomainError("Choose service, adjustment or once-off work for a new line.");
      }
      if (line.amountCents < 0 && line.kind !== "ADJUSTMENT") {
        throw new DomainError("Only an adjustment can be negative.");
      }
    }
    return existing;
  };

  // Look first WITHOUT locking: a save that changes nothing must write nothing.
  const first = await read();
  check(first);
  const firstDue = first.dueDate ? toDateString(first.dueDate) : null;
  const same =
    firstDue === input.dueDate &&
    first.lines.length === input.lines.length &&
    first.lines.every((l, i) => {
      const n = input.lines[i];
      return n !== undefined && n.id === l.id && n.description === l.description && n.amountCents === l.amountCents;
    });
  if (same) return { version: first.draftVersion };

  await lockCustomer(store, first.customerId);
  await lockInvoiceRows(store, [id]);
  const existing = check(await read());
  await store.invoiceLine.deleteMany({ where: { invoiceId: id } });
  if (input.lines.length > 0) {
    await store.invoiceLine.createMany({
      data: input.lines.map((l, index) => {
        const old = l.id ? existing.get(l.id) : undefined;
        return {
          invoiceId: id,
          sortOrder: index + 1,
          kind: l.kind,
          description: l.description,
          amountCents: l.amountCents,
          propertyId: old?.propertyId ?? null,
          sourceQuoteLineId: old?.sourceQuoteLineId ?? null,
          forInvoiceId: old?.forInvoiceId ?? null,
        };
      }),
    });
  }
  const updated = await store.invoice.update({
    where: { id },
    data: {
      dueDate: input.dueDate ? fromDateString(input.dueDate) : null,
      draftVersion: { increment: 1 },
    },
    select: { draftVersion: true },
  });
  return { version: updated.draftVersion };
}

/** "Skip": a draft is deleted with its lines. Lock: the customer, then the invoice. */
export async function deleteInvoiceDraft(store: InvoiceStore, actor: Actor, id: string): Promise<void> {
  assertRole(actor, MANAGEMENT);
  const read = async () => {
    const inv = await store.invoice.findUnique({ where: { id }, select: { status: true, customerId: true } });
    if (!inv) throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.notFound);
    if (inv.status !== "DRAFT") throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.onlyDraftDelete);
    return inv;
  };
  const first = await read();
  await lockCustomer(store, first.customerId);
  await lockInvoiceRows(store, [id]);
  await read();
  await store.invoiceLine.deleteMany({ where: { invoiceId: id } });
  await store.invoice.delete({ where: { id } });
  await logInvoiceEvent(store, actor, "invoice.deleted_draft", id);
}

const CUSTOMER_BRIEF = { select: { id: true, number: true, displayName: true } } as const;

const VIEW_SELECT = {
  id: true,
  kind: true,
  status: true,
  numberYear: true,
  numberSeq: true,
  serviceMonth: true,
  dueDate: true,
  issuedAt: true,
  createdAt: true,
  draftVersion: true,
  totalCents: true,
  recipientName: true,
  recipientAddress: true,
  emailStatus: true,
  emailedAt: true,
  voidedAt: true,
  voidReason: true,
  sourceQuoteId: true,
  customerId: true,
  billToCustomerId: true,
  customer: {
    select: {
      id: true,
      number: true,
      displayName: true,
      status: true,
      anonymisedAt: true,
      managedById: true,
      billsToManager: true,
    },
  },
  billTo: CUSTOMER_BRIEF,
  sourceQuote: { select: { id: true, numberYear: true, numberSeq: true, revision: true } },
  lines: {
    orderBy: { sortOrder: "asc" },
    select: { id: true, kind: true, description: true, amountCents: true, propertyId: true, forInvoiceId: true },
  },
  allocations: {
    orderBy: { id: "asc" },
    select: {
      id: true,
      amountCents: true,
      payment: { select: { id: true, receivedOn: true, amountCents: true, voidedAt: true } },
    },
  },
  reports: {
    orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
    select: { id: true, paidOn: true, reference: true, status: true, createdAt: true },
  },
} as const satisfies Prisma.InvoiceSelect;

/** One invoice for the staff screen, with its balance and status worked out. Null when there is none. */
export async function getInvoice(store: InvoiceStore, actor: Actor, id: string) {
  assertRole(actor, MANAGEMENT);
  const row = await store.invoice.findUnique({ where: { id }, select: VIEW_SELECT });
  if (!row) return null;
  const allocatedCents = row.allocations
    .filter((a) => !a.payment.voidedAt)
    .reduce((t, a) => t + a.amountCents, 0);
  const totalCents = row.totalCents ?? sumLineCents(row.lines);
  const dueDateString = row.dueDate ? toDateString(row.dueDate) : null;
  // A draft is addressed as the customer's billing stands now; issuing freezes it.
  const billToNowId = billToIdFor(row.customer);
  const billTo =
    row.status === "DRAFT" && billToNowId !== row.billTo.id
      ? ((await store.customer.findUnique({ where: { id: billToNowId }, ...CUSTOMER_BRIEF })) ?? row.billTo)
      : row.billTo;
  return {
    ...row,
    billTo,
    reference: formatInvoiceReference(row),
    sourceQuoteReference: row.sourceQuote ? formatQuoteReference(row.sourceQuote) : null,
    totalCents,
    allocatedCents,
    balanceCents: row.status === "ISSUED" ? totalCents - allocatedCents : 0,
    displayStatus: invoiceDisplayStatus(
      { status: row.status, totalCents, allocatedCents, dueDate: dueDateString },
      todaySA(),
    ),
    dueDateString,
    serviceMonthString: row.serviceMonth ? toDateString(row.serviceMonth).slice(0, 7) : null,
  };
}
export type InvoiceView = NonNullable<Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getInvoice>>>;

const LIST_SELECT = {
  id: true,
  kind: true,
  status: true,
  numberYear: true,
  numberSeq: true,
  serviceMonth: true,
  dueDate: true,
  issuedAt: true,
  totalCents: true,
  emailStatus: true,
  customer: CUSTOMER_BRIEF,
  billTo: CUSTOMER_BRIEF,
  lines: { select: { amountCents: true } },
} as const satisfies Prisma.InvoiceSelect;
type ListRow = Prisma.InvoiceGetPayload<{ select: typeof LIST_SELECT }>;

export type InvoiceListItem = {
  id: string;
  reference: string | null;
  kind: "MONTHLY" | "ONCE_OFF";
  status: "DRAFT" | "ISSUED" | "VOID";
  displayStatus: InvoiceDisplayStatus;
  customer: { id: string; number: number; displayName: string };
  billTo: { id: string; number: number; displayName: string };
  serviceMonth: string | null;
  dueDate: string | null;
  issuedAt: Date | null;
  totalCents: number;
  balanceCents: number;
  emailStatus: "NONE" | "SENT" | "FAILED";
};

async function toListItems(store: InvoiceStore, rows: ListRow[]): Promise<InvoiceListItem[]> {
  const allocated = await allocatedByInvoice(
    store,
    rows.filter((r) => r.status === "ISSUED").map((r) => r.id),
  );
  const today = todaySA();
  return rows.map((r) => {
    const totalCents = r.totalCents ?? sumLineCents(r.lines);
    const allocatedCents = allocated.get(r.id) ?? 0;
    const dueDate = r.dueDate ? toDateString(r.dueDate) : null;
    return {
      id: r.id,
      reference: formatInvoiceReference(r),
      kind: r.kind,
      status: r.status,
      displayStatus: invoiceDisplayStatus({ status: r.status, totalCents, allocatedCents, dueDate }, today),
      customer: r.customer,
      billTo: r.billTo,
      serviceMonth: r.serviceMonth ? toDateString(r.serviceMonth).slice(0, 7) : null,
      dueDate,
      issuedAt: r.issuedAt,
      totalCents,
      balanceCents: r.status === "ISSUED" ? totalCents - allocatedCents : 0,
      emailStatus: r.emailStatus,
    };
  });
}

export const INVOICE_FILTERS = ["DRAFT", "UNPAID", "OVERDUE", "PAID", "VOID"] as const;
export type InvoiceListQuery = {
  status?: string | undefined;
  q?: string | undefined;
  page?: number | undefined;
  pageSize?: number | undefined;
};

function clampInt(value: unknown, min: number, max: number, fallback: number): number {
  const whole = typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value) ? Math.floor(value) : fallback;
  return Math.min(max, Math.max(min, whole));
}

/** The longest list worked through in memory (statuses depend on balances, which are not stored). */
const LIST_CAP = 2000;

export async function listInvoices(store: InvoiceStore, actor: Actor, query: InvoiceListQuery) {
  assertRole(actor, MANAGEMENT);
  const pageSize = clampInt(query.pageSize, 1, 100, 25);
  const page = clampInt(query.page, 1, 10_000, 1);
  const filter = INVOICE_FILTERS.find((f) => f === query.status);
  const and: Prisma.InvoiceWhereInput[] = [];
  if (filter === "DRAFT") and.push({ status: "DRAFT" });
  else if (filter === "VOID") and.push({ status: "VOID" });
  else if (filter) and.push({ status: "ISSUED" });
  const term = query.q?.trim();
  if (term) {
    const contains = { contains: term, mode: "insensitive" } as const;
    const or: Prisma.InvoiceWhereInput[] = [
      { customer: { displayName: contains } },
      { billTo: { displayName: contains } },
    ];
    const ref = parseInvoiceSearch(term);
    if (ref) or.push({ numberSeq: ref.numberSeq, ...(ref.numberYear ? { numberYear: ref.numberYear } : {}) });
    and.push({ OR: or });
  }
  const rows = await store.invoice.findMany({
    where: { AND: and },
    orderBy: [{ createdAt: "desc" }, { id: "desc" }],
    take: LIST_CAP,
    select: LIST_SELECT,
  });
  const all = await toListItems(store, rows);
  const wanted =
    filter === "UNPAID"
      ? all.filter((i) => i.displayStatus === "UNPAID" || i.displayStatus === "PART_PAID")
      : filter === "OVERDUE"
        ? all.filter((i) => i.displayStatus === "OVERDUE")
        : filter === "PAID"
          ? all.filter((i) => i.displayStatus === "PAID")
          : all;
  return {
    items: wanted.slice((page - 1) * pageSize, page * pageSize),
    total: wanted.length,
    page,
    pageSize,
  };
}

export type DraftReviewItem = {
  id: string;
  kind: "MONTHLY" | "ONCE_OFF";
  customer: { id: string; number: number; displayName: string };
  billTo: { id: string; number: number; displayName: string };
  serviceMonth: string | null;
  dueDate: string | null;
  totalCents: number;
  lineCount: number;
  warnings: DraftWarning[];
};

/** Every draft, oldest first, with the warnings the owner should look at before issuing. */
export async function listDraftsForReview(store: InvoiceStore, actor: Actor): Promise<DraftReviewItem[]> {
  assertRole(actor, MANAGEMENT);
  const drafts = await store.invoice.findMany({
    where: { status: "DRAFT" },
    orderBy: [{ createdAt: "asc" }, { id: "asc" }],
    take: 500,
    select: {
      id: true,
      kind: true,
      serviceMonth: true,
      dueDate: true,
      customer: {
        select: { id: true, number: true, displayName: true, managedById: true, billsToManager: true },
      },
      lines: { select: { kind: true, amountCents: true } },
    },
  });
  const billToIds = [...new Set(drafts.map((d) => billToIdFor(d.customer)))];
  const billTos = await store.customer.findMany({ where: { id: { in: billToIds } }, ...CUSTOMER_BRIEF });
  const billToById = new Map(billTos.map((b) => [b.id, b]));
  const withEmail = new Set(
    (
      await store.contact.findMany({
        where: { customerId: { in: billToIds }, receivesInvoices: true, email: { not: null } },
        select: { customerId: true },
      })
    ).map((c) => c.customerId),
  );
  const monthlyCustomerIds = [...new Set(drafts.filter((d) => d.kind === "MONTHLY").map((d) => d.customer.id))];
  const monthly = await store.invoice.findMany({
    where: { kind: "MONTHLY", status: { not: "VOID" }, customerId: { in: monthlyCustomerIds } },
    select: { id: true, customerId: true, serviceMonth: true },
  });
  return drafts.map((d) => {
    const billToId = billToIdFor(d.customer);
    const warnings: DraftWarning[] = [];
    if (!withEmail.has(billToId)) warnings.push("noEmail");
    if (
      d.kind === "MONTHLY" &&
      d.serviceMonth &&
      !monthly.some(
        (m) =>
          m.customerId === d.customer.id &&
          m.id !== d.id &&
          m.serviceMonth !== null &&
          m.serviceMonth.getTime() < (d.serviceMonth?.getTime() ?? 0),
      )
    ) {
      warnings.push("firstMonth");
    }
    if (d.lines.some((l) => l.kind === "LATE_FEE")) warnings.push("lateFee");
    if (billToId !== d.customer.id) warnings.push("billToAgency");
    return {
      id: d.id,
      kind: d.kind,
      customer: { id: d.customer.id, number: d.customer.number, displayName: d.customer.displayName },
      billTo: billToById.get(billToId) ?? {
        id: d.customer.id,
        number: d.customer.number,
        displayName: d.customer.displayName,
      },
      serviceMonth: d.serviceMonth ? toDateString(d.serviceMonth).slice(0, 7) : null,
      dueDate: d.dueDate ? toDateString(d.dueDate) : null,
      totalCents: sumLineCents(d.lines),
      lineCount: d.lines.length,
      warnings,
    };
  });
}

/**
 * The customer's Invoices tab: invoices for its work or billed to it (latest 100), what it owes as the payer,
 * its unallocated credit, and its latest 50 payments.
 */
export async function listCustomerInvoices(store: InvoiceStore, actor: Actor, customerId: string) {
  assertRole(actor, MANAGEMENT);
  const rows = await store.invoice.findMany({
    where: { OR: [{ customerId }, { billToCustomerId: customerId }] },
    orderBy: [{ createdAt: "desc" }, { id: "desc" }],
    take: 100,
    select: LIST_SELECT,
  });
  const items = await toListItems(store, rows);
  const open = await store.invoice.findMany({
    where: { billToCustomerId: customerId, status: "ISSUED" },
    select: LIST_SELECT,
  });
  const owedCents = (await toListItems(store, open)).reduce((t, i) => t + Math.max(0, i.balanceCents), 0);

  const live = await store.payment.findMany({
    where: { payerCustomerId: customerId, voidedAt: null },
    select: { id: true, amountCents: true },
  });
  const allocated = await allocatedByPayment(store, live.map((p) => p.id));
  const creditCents = live.reduce((t, p) => t + Math.max(0, p.amountCents - (allocated.get(p.id) ?? 0)), 0);

  const recent = await store.payment.findMany({
    where: { payerCustomerId: customerId },
    orderBy: [{ receivedOn: "desc" }, { createdAt: "desc" }],
    take: 50,
    select: { id: true, receivedOn: true, amountCents: true, reference: true, voidedAt: true },
  });
  const recentAllocated = await allocatedByPayment(store, recent.map((p) => p.id));
  return {
    items,
    owedCents,
    creditCents,
    payments: recent.map((p) => ({
      id: p.id,
      receivedOn: toDateString(p.receivedOn),
      amountCents: p.amountCents,
      reference: p.reference,
      voided: p.voidedAt !== null,
      unallocatedCents: p.voidedAt ? 0 : p.amountCents - (recentAllocated.get(p.id) ?? 0),
    })),
  };
}
```

### Task 7: The monthly run (`createMonthlyDrafts`)

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/core/src/invoices/run.ts`, `run.db.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/core/src/invoices/index.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 4 (`serviceMonthFor`, `dueDateFor`, `firstDayOf`, `lastDayOf`, `proRata`, `formatDayMonth`, `runMonthAllowed`, `fitDescription`, `formatInvoiceReference`, `RunSkipReason`); Task 5 (`InvoiceStore`, `INVOICE_MESSAGES`, `allocatedByInvoice`, `billToIdFor`, `isMonthlyDuplicate`, `logInvoiceEvent`, `readInvoiceSettings`; test helpers); `TxRunner` (type, `portal/access.ts`); `lockCustomer` (`customers/store.ts`); `ForbiddenError`, `DomainError` (`customers/access.ts`); `fromDateString`, `toDateString`, `todaySA` (`quotes/reference.ts`).
- Produces:
  - `MONTHLY_AGREEMENT: Prisma.QuoteLineWhereInput` (a priced monthly line, or a chosen optional monthly line; same rule as `quotes/outcomes.ts`)
  - `type RunResult = { runMonth: string; created: { invoiceId: string; customerId: string }[]; skipped: { customerId: string; displayName: string; reason: RunSkipReason }[] }`
  - `createMonthlyDrafts(store: InvoiceStore, actor: Actor, runMonth: string, deps: { run: TxRunner; only?: readonly string[]; today?: string }): Promise<RunResult>`. `only` limits the candidates to these customer ids (tests, and a future per-customer button); `today` defaults to `todaySA()` (tests pin it).

How the run works (write it exactly so):
1. `assertRole(actor, MANAGEMENT)`; refuse a month outside `runMonthAllowed(runMonth, today)` with `INVOICE_MESSAGES.runMonth`.
2. Read the settings once (`lateFeeCents`).
3. Candidates: distinct customer ids of quotes that are ACCEPTED, `agreementEndedAt` null, on a property that is not archived, with a line matching `MONTHLY_AGREEMENT` (limited to `only` when given), ordered by customer id.
4. For each candidate, in its OWN transaction (`deps.run`), `draftForCustomer`. A refusal because a live monthly invoice already exists for the month (`isMonthlyDuplicate`) counts as `alreadyInvoiced`; a `ForbiddenError` is re-thrown; any other error is logged with the fixed phrase "A monthly invoice draft could not be created." and counted as `failed`. One failure never stops the others.
5. `draftForCustomer` (inside the transaction): read the customer (no lock); skip `anonymised`, then `lead` / `paused` / `archived` by status; work out the service month and due date from its Billing timing; skip `alreadyInvoiced` when a non-void MONTHLY invoice exists for that customer and service month; load its current agreements (one per property: the latest accepted); keep those whose start date (or acceptance date when there is no start date) is on or before the last day of the service month, else skip `notStarted`; THEN `lockCustomer`, re-read and re-check everything above; build the lines; create the draft; write the audit line `invoice.drafted` with `{ kind: "MONTHLY", via: "run", lineCount, lateFees }`.
6. Lines, in order: for each agreement (properties sorted by label, then id), each of its quote lines in quote order that is a priced monthly line or a chosen optional monthly line with an amount above zero: kind SERVICE (priced) or OPTIONAL (chosen option), `propertyId`, `sourceQuoteLineId`, description `"<property label>: <line wording>"`; when `proRata(amount, start, serviceMonth)` applies, the amount is the pro-rata amount and the description gains `" (part month: <days> of <daysInMonth> days from <d Mon>)"`; descriptions go through `fitDescription`. Then one LATE_FEE line of `lateFeeCents` (when above 0) per ISSUED invoice of this customer whose due date is before today, whose balance is above zero, and that has no LATE_FEE line on a non-void invoice charged against it yet, ordered by invoice number: description `"Late payment fee: <INV reference>"`, `forInvoiceId` set.
