```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { ForbiddenError } from "../customers/access";
import { toDateString } from "../quotes/reference";
import { dbTestsEnabled, withRollback } from "../testing/rollback";
import { createMonthlyDrafts } from "./run";
import {
  addProperty,
  auditFor,
  crew,
  makeAcceptedQuote,
  makeBillingCustomer,
  makeIssuedInvoice,
  makePayment,
  manager,
  optionalLine,
  sameTx,
  serviceLine,
} from "./test-helpers";

const TODAY = "2026-11-20";

async function linesOf(tx: Parameters<Parameters<typeof withRollback>[0]>[0], invoiceId: string) {
  const inv = await tx.invoice.findUniqueOrThrow({
    where: { id: invoiceId },
    select: {
      kind: true,
      status: true,
      serviceMonth: true,
      dueDate: true,
      billToCustomerId: true,
      createdById: true,
      lines: {
        orderBy: { sortOrder: "asc" },
        select: { kind: true, description: true, amountCents: true, forInvoiceId: true, propertyId: true },
      },
    },
  });
  return {
    ...inv,
    serviceMonth: inv.serviceMonth ? toDateString(inv.serviceMonth) : null,
    dueDate: inv.dueDate ? toDateString(inv.dueDate) : null,
  };
}

describe.skipIf(!dbTestsEnabled)("the monthly run", () => {
  it("drafts Pre and Post clients with the right month and due date, and is idempotent", async () => {
    await withRollback(async (tx) => {
      const pre = await makeBillingCustomer(tx, { billingTiming: "PRE" });
      const post = await makeBillingCustomer(tx, { billingTiming: "POST" });
      for (const c of [pre, post]) {
        await makeAcceptedQuote(tx, {
          customerId: c.customerId,
          propertyId: c.propertyId,
          startDate: "2026-10-01",
          lines: [serviceLine(115000), optionalLine(10000, true), optionalLine(20000, false, "Not chosen")],
        });
      }
      const only = [pre.customerId, post.customerId];
      await expect(
        createMonthlyDrafts(tx, crew, "2026-11", { run: sameTx(tx), only, today: TODAY }),
      ).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ForbiddenError);
      const result = await createMonthlyDrafts(tx, manager, "2026-11", { run: sameTx(tx), only, today: TODAY });
      expect(result.created).toHaveLength(2);
      expect(result.skipped).toEqual([]);

      const byCustomer = new Map(result.created.map((c) => [c.customerId, c.invoiceId]));
      const preInv = await linesOf(tx, byCustomer.get(pre.customerId) ?? "");
      expect(preInv).toMatchObject({
        kind: "MONTHLY",
        status: "DRAFT",
        serviceMonth: "2026-12-01",
        dueDate: "2026-11-30",
        billToCustomerId: pre.customerId,
        createdById: manager.id,
      });
      expect(preInv.lines.map((l) => [l.kind, l.description, l.amountCents])).toEqual([
        ["SERVICE", `${pre.propertyLabel}: Weekly garden maintenance`, 115000],
        ["OPTIONAL", `${pre.propertyLabel}: Weed treatment`, 10000],
      ]);
      const postInv = await linesOf(tx, byCustomer.get(post.customerId) ?? "");
      expect(postInv).toMatchObject({ serviceMonth: "2026-11-01", dueDate: "2026-11-25" });

      const audit = await auditFor(tx, "invoice", byCustomer.get(pre.customerId) ?? "");
      expect(audit.map((a) => a.action)).toEqual(["invoice.drafted"]);
      expect(audit[0]?.after).toEqual({ kind: "MONTHLY", via: "run", lineCount: 2, lateFees: 0 });

      const again = await createMonthlyDrafts(tx, manager, "2026-11", { run: sameTx(tx), only, today: TODAY });
      expect(again.created).toEqual([]);
      expect(again.skipped.map((s) => s.reason)).toEqual(["alreadyInvoiced", "alreadyInvoiced"]);
    });
  });

  it("skips leads, paused, archived and anonymised customers and agreements that start later", async () => {
    await withRollback(async (tx) => {
      const lead = await makeBillingCustomer(tx, { status: "LEAD" });
      const paused = await makeBillingCustomer(tx, { status: "PAUSED" });
      const archived = await makeBillingCustomer(tx, { status: "ARCHIVED" });
      const gone = await makeBillingCustomer(tx);
      const later = await makeBillingCustomer(tx, { billingTiming: "POST" });
      const ended = await makeBillingCustomer(tx);
      for (const c of [lead, paused, archived, gone]) {
        await makeAcceptedQuote(tx, {
          customerId: c.customerId,
          propertyId: c.propertyId,
          startDate: "2026-10-01",
          lines: [serviceLine(50000)],
        });
      }
      await tx.customer.update({ where: { id: gone.customerId }, data: { anonymisedAt: new Date() } });
      // Post in November bills November: a December start is not started yet.
      await makeAcceptedQuote(tx, {
        customerId: later.customerId,
        propertyId: later.propertyId,
        startDate: "2026-12-01",
        lines: [serviceLine(50000)],
      });
      // An agreement that a later quote replaced is not a candidate at all.
      await makeAcceptedQuote(tx, {
        customerId: ended.customerId,
        propertyId: ended.propertyId,
        startDate: "2026-10-01",
        lines: [serviceLine(50000)],
        agreementEnded: true,
      });
      const only = [lead, paused, archived, gone, later, ended].map((c) => c.customerId);
      const result = await createMonthlyDrafts(tx, manager, "2026-11", { run: sameTx(tx), only, today: TODAY });
      expect(result.created).toEqual([]);
      const reasons = new Map(result.skipped.map((s) => [s.customerId, s.reason]));
      expect(reasons.get(lead.customerId)).toBe("lead");
      expect(reasons.get(paused.customerId)).toBe("paused");
      expect(reasons.get(archived.customerId)).toBe("archived");
      expect(reasons.get(gone.customerId)).toBe("anonymised");
      expect(reasons.get(later.customerId)).toBe("notStarted");
      expect(reasons.has(ended.customerId)).toBe(false);
      expect(result.skipped).toHaveLength(5);
    });
  });

  it("bills a complex's invoice to its agency, with a line per property and a part first month", async () => {
    await withRollback(async (tx) => {
      const agency = await makeBillingCustomer(tx);
      const complex = await makeBillingCustomer(tx, { managedById: agency.customerId, billingTiming: "PRE" });
      const second = await addProperty(tx, complex.customerId, "ZZZ Second site");
      await makeAcceptedQuote(tx, {
        customerId: complex.customerId,
        propertyId: complex.propertyId,
        startDate: "2026-10-01",
        lines: [serviceLine(100000)],
      });
      // Starts on 10 December: 22 of 31 days of R1,150.00 = R816.13
      await makeAcceptedQuote(tx, {
        customerId: complex.customerId,
        propertyId: second,
        startDate: "2026-12-10",
        lines: [serviceLine(115000, "Fortnightly garden care")],
      });
      const result = await createMonthlyDrafts(tx, manager, "2026-11", {
        run: sameTx(tx),
        only: [complex.customerId],
        today: TODAY,
      });
      const inv = await linesOf(tx, result.created[0]?.invoiceId ?? "");
      expect(inv.billToCustomerId).toBe(agency.customerId);
      expect(inv.lines.map((l) => [l.description, l.amountCents, l.propertyId])).toEqual([
        [`${complex.propertyLabel}: Weekly garden maintenance`, 100000, complex.propertyId],
        ["ZZZ Second site: Fortnightly garden care (part month: 22 of 31 days from 10 Dec)", 81613, second],
      ]);
    });
  });

  it("suggests one late fee per overdue invoice, once only, and again after that fee's invoice is voided", async () => {
    await withRollback(async (tx) => {
      // The dev settings row may have been edited by hand: pin the fee for this test (rolled back).
      await tx.invoiceSettings.update({ where: { id: "singleton" }, data: { lateFeeCents: 10000 } });
      const c = await makeBillingCustomer(tx, { billingTiming: "POST" });
      await makeAcceptedQuote(tx, {
        customerId: c.customerId,
        propertyId: c.propertyId,
        startDate: "2026-01-01",
        lines: [serviceLine(50000)],
      });
      const overdue = await makeIssuedInvoice(tx, { customerId: c.customerId, dueDate: "2026-10-25" });
      const paidUp = await makeIssuedInvoice(tx, { customerId: c.customerId, dueDate: "2026-10-25", totalCents: 30000 });
      await makePayment(tx, {
        payerCustomerId: c.customerId,
        amountCents: 30000,
        receivedOn: "2026-10-20",
        allocations: [{ invoiceId: paidUp, amountCents: 30000 }],
      });
      await makeIssuedInvoice(tx, { customerId: c.customerId, dueDate: "2026-12-25" });

      const run = { run: sameTx(tx), only: [c.customerId], today: TODAY };
      const first = await createMonthlyDrafts(tx, manager, "2026-11", run);
      const draftId = first.created[0]?.invoiceId ?? "";
      const lines = (await linesOf(tx, draftId)).lines;
      const fees = lines.filter((l) => l.kind === "LATE_FEE");
      expect(fees).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(fees[0]).toMatchObject({ amountCents: 10000, forInvoiceId: overdue });
      expect(fees[0]?.description).toMatch(/^Late payment fee: INV-2099-\d{4,}$/);
      expect((await auditFor(tx, "invoice", draftId))[0]?.after).toEqual({
        kind: "MONTHLY",
        via: "run",
        lineCount: 2,
        lateFees: 1,
      });

      // The fee is on a live draft: December's run does not suggest it again.
      const december = await createMonthlyDrafts(tx, manager, "2026-12", { ...run, today: "2026-12-20" });
      const decLines = (await linesOf(tx, december.created[0]?.invoiceId ?? "")).lines;
      expect(decLines.filter((l) => l.kind === "LATE_FEE")).toEqual([]);

      // Voiding the November invoice that carried the fee makes it suggestible again.
      await tx.invoice.update({
        where: { id: draftId },
        data: {
          status: "VOID",
          numberYear: 2099,
          numberSeq: 1_999_999_999,
          dueDate: new Date("2026-11-25T00:00:00Z"),
          issuedAt: new Date(),
          totalCents: 60000,
          voidedAt: new Date(),
        },
      });
      const novemberAgain = await createMonthlyDrafts(tx, manager, "2026-11", run);
      const againLines = (await linesOf(tx, novemberAgain.created[0]?.invoiceId ?? "")).lines;
      expect(againLines.filter((l) => l.kind === "LATE_FEE").map((l) => l.forInvoiceId)).toEqual([overdue]);
    });
  });

  it("keeps going when one customer fails, and refuses a month too far ahead", async () => {
    await withRollback(async (tx) => {
      const a = await makeBillingCustomer(tx);
      const b = await makeBillingCustomer(tx);
      for (const c of [a, b]) {
        await makeAcceptedQuote(tx, {
          customerId: c.customerId,
          propertyId: c.propertyId,
          startDate: "2026-10-01",
          lines: [serviceLine(50000)],
        });
      }
      let calls = 0;
      const flaky = <T,>(fn: (t: typeof tx) => Promise<T>): Promise<T> => {
        calls += 1;
        if (calls === 1) return Promise.reject(new Error("boom"));
        return fn(tx);
      };
      const result = await createMonthlyDrafts(tx, manager, "2026-11", {
        run: flaky,
        only: [a.customerId, b.customerId],
        today: TODAY,
      });
      expect(result.created).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(result.skipped.map((s) => s.reason)).toEqual(["failed"]);
      await expect(
        createMonthlyDrafts(tx, manager, "2027-01", { run: sameTx(tx), only: [a.customerId], today: TODAY }),
      ).rejects.toThrow("Choose a month from a year ago up to next month.");
    });
  });
});
```

Note: the `flaky` runner's generic arrow is written `<T,>` because the file is `.ts`; either form compiles.

Run `npm run typecheck`. Expected: errors (`./run` does not exist yet).

- [ ] **Step 2: Write `packages/core/src/invoices/run.ts`**

```ts
import { assertRole, DomainError, ForbiddenError, MANAGEMENT, type Actor } from "../customers/access";
import { lockCustomer } from "../customers/store";
import type { Prisma } from "../generated/prisma/client";
import type { TxRunner } from "../portal/access";
import { fromDateString, toDateString, todaySA } from "../quotes/reference";
import {
  dueDateFor,
  firstDayOf,
  formatDayMonth,
  lastDayOf,
  proRata,
  runMonthAllowed,
  serviceMonthFor,
} from "./dates";
import type { RunSkipReason } from "./labels";
import { fitDescription } from "./money";
import { formatInvoiceReference } from "./reference";
import { readInvoiceSettings } from "./settings";
import {
  allocatedByInvoice,
  billToIdFor,
  INVOICE_MESSAGES,
  isMonthlyDuplicate,
  logInvoiceEvent,
  type InvoiceStore,
} from "./store";

/** A monthly agreement line: priced monthly, or an optional monthly line the client chose. */
export const MONTHLY_AGREEMENT: Prisma.QuoteLineWhereInput = {
  billing: "MONTHLY",
  OR: [{ lineKind: "PRICED" }, { lineKind: "OPTIONAL", chosen: true }],
};

const CURRENT_AGREEMENT = {
  status: "ACCEPTED",
  agreementEndedAt: null,
  property: { archivedAt: null },
  lines: { some: MONTHLY_AGREEMENT },
} satisfies Prisma.QuoteWhereInput;

export type RunResult = {
  runMonth: string;
  created: { invoiceId: string; customerId: string }[];
  skipped: { customerId: string; displayName: string; reason: RunSkipReason }[];
};

type Outcome = { invoiceId: string } | { reason: RunSkipReason };

type Context = { actor: Actor; runMonth: string; today: string; lateFeeCents: number };

/** The current agreement of each of the customer's properties (the latest accepted), sorted by label. */
async function loadAgreements(tx: InvoiceStore, customerId: string) {
  const quotes = await tx.quote.findMany({
    where: { customerId, ...CURRENT_AGREEMENT },
    orderBy: [{ acceptedAt: "desc" }, { id: "desc" }],
    select: {
      id: true,
      propertyId: true,
      acceptedAt: true,
      acceptedStartDate: true,
      property: { select: { label: true } },
      lines: {
        orderBy: { sortOrder: "asc" },
        select: { id: true, lineKind: true, billing: true, description: true, amountCents: true, chosen: true },
      },
    },
  });
  const byProperty = new Map<string, (typeof quotes)[number]>();
  for (const q of quotes) if (!byProperty.has(q.propertyId)) byProperty.set(q.propertyId, q);
  return [...byProperty.values()]
    .map((q) => ({
      ...q,
      start: q.acceptedStartDate
        ? toDateString(q.acceptedStartDate)
        : todaySA(q.acceptedAt ?? new Date()),
    }))
    .sort((a, b) =>
      a.property.label !== b.property.label
        ? a.property.label < b.property.label
          ? -1
          : 1
        : a.propertyId < b.propertyId
          ? -1
          : 1,
    );
}

/** The suggested late-fee lines: one per overdue issued invoice that has no live fee charged against it yet. */
async function lateFeeLines(tx: InvoiceStore, customerId: string, ctx: Context) {
  if (ctx.lateFeeCents <= 0) return [];
  const candidates = await tx.invoice.findMany({
    where: { customerId, status: "ISSUED", dueDate: { lt: fromDateString(ctx.today) } },
    orderBy: [{ numberYear: "asc" }, { numberSeq: "asc" }],
    select: { id: true, numberYear: true, numberSeq: true, totalCents: true },
  });
  if (candidates.length === 0) return [];
  const ids = candidates.map((c) => c.id);
  const allocated = await allocatedByInvoice(tx, ids);
  const charged = new Set(
    (
      await tx.invoiceLine.findMany({
        where: { kind: "LATE_FEE", forInvoiceId: { in: ids }, invoice: { status: { not: "VOID" } } },
        select: { forInvoiceId: true },
      })
    ).flatMap((l) => (l.forInvoiceId ? [l.forInvoiceId] : [])),
  );
  return candidates
    .filter((c) => (c.totalCents ?? 0) - (allocated.get(c.id) ?? 0) > 0 && !charged.has(c.id))
    .map((c) => ({
      kind: "LATE_FEE" as const,
      description: fitDescription(`Late payment fee: ${formatInvoiceReference(c) ?? ""}`),
      amountCents: ctx.lateFeeCents,
      forInvoiceId: c.id,
    }));
}

/** One customer, inside its own transaction. */
async function draftForCustomer(tx: InvoiceStore, customerId: string, ctx: Context): Promise<Outcome> {
  const decide = async () => {
    const customer = await tx.customer.findUnique({
      where: { id: customerId },
      select: {
        id: true,
        status: true,
        anonymisedAt: true,
        billingTiming: true,
        managedById: true,
        billsToManager: true,
      },
    });
    if (!customer) throw new DomainError("That customer could not be found.");
    if (customer.anonymisedAt) return { reason: "anonymised" as const };
    if (customer.status === "LEAD") return { reason: "lead" as const };
    if (customer.status === "PAUSED") return { reason: "paused" as const };
    if (customer.status === "ARCHIVED") return { reason: "archived" as const };
    const serviceMonth = serviceMonthFor(ctx.runMonth, customer.billingTiming);
    const existing = await tx.invoice.count({
      where: {
        customerId,
        kind: "MONTHLY",
        status: { not: "VOID" },
        serviceMonth: fromDateString(firstDayOf(serviceMonth)),
      },
    });
    if (existing > 0) return { reason: "alreadyInvoiced" as const };
    const monthEnd = lastDayOf(serviceMonth);
    const agreements = (await loadAgreements(tx, customerId)).filter((a) => a.start <= monthEnd);
    if (agreements.length === 0) return { reason: "notStarted" as const };
    return { customer, serviceMonth, agreements };
  };

  const first = await decide();
  if ("reason" in first) return first;
  await lockCustomer(tx, customerId);
  const plan = await decide();
  if ("reason" in plan) return plan;

  const serviceLines = plan.agreements.flatMap((a) =>
    a.lines
      .filter(
        (l) =>
          l.billing === "MONTHLY" &&
          (l.lineKind === "PRICED" || (l.lineKind === "OPTIONAL" && l.chosen)) &&
          (l.amountCents ?? 0) > 0,
      )
      .map((l) => {
        const base = `${a.property.label}: ${l.description}`;
        const part = proRata(l.amountCents ?? 0, a.start, plan.serviceMonth);
        return {
          kind: l.lineKind === "PRICED" ? ("SERVICE" as const) : ("OPTIONAL" as const),
          description: fitDescription(
            part
              ? `${base} (part month: ${part.days} of ${part.daysInMonth} days from ${formatDayMonth(a.start)})`
              : base,
          ),
          amountCents: part ? part.amountCents : (l.amountCents ?? 0),
          propertyId: a.propertyId,
          sourceQuoteLineId: l.id,
        };
      }),
  );
  const fees = await lateFeeLines(tx, customerId, ctx);
  const lines = [...serviceLines, ...fees].filter((l) => l.amountCents > 0);
  const created = await tx.invoice.create({
    data: {
      customerId,
      billToCustomerId: billToIdFor(plan.customer),
      kind: "MONTHLY",
      serviceMonth: fromDateString(firstDayOf(plan.serviceMonth)),
      dueDate: fromDateString(dueDateFor(ctx.runMonth, plan.customer.billingTiming)),
      createdById: ctx.actor.id,
      lines: { create: lines.map((l, index) => ({ ...l, sortOrder: index + 1 })) },
    },
    select: { id: true },
  });
  await logInvoiceEvent(tx, ctx.actor, "invoice.drafted", created.id, {
    kind: "MONTHLY",
    via: "run",
    lineCount: lines.length,
    lateFees: fees.length,
  });
  return { invoiceId: created.id };
}

/**
 * "Create this month's invoices": one draft per eligible monthly client for the run month, each customer in its
 * own transaction so one failure never blocks the rest. Idempotent: a second run skips customers that already
 * have a live monthly invoice for their service month (the partial unique index backs this up). Only drafts
 * are created; nothing is issued or emailed.
 */
export async function createMonthlyDrafts(
  store: InvoiceStore,
  actor: Actor,
  runMonth: string,
  deps: { run: TxRunner; only?: readonly string[]; today?: string },
): Promise<RunResult> {
  assertRole(actor, MANAGEMENT);
  const today = deps.today ?? todaySA();
  if (!runMonthAllowed(runMonth, today)) throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.runMonth);
  const settings = await readInvoiceSettings(store);
  const ctx: Context = { actor, runMonth, today, lateFeeCents: settings.lateFeeCents };

  const rows = await store.quote.findMany({
    where: { ...CURRENT_AGREEMENT, ...(deps.only ? { customerId: { in: [...deps.only] } } : {}) },
    distinct: ["customerId"],
    orderBy: { customerId: "asc" },
    select: { customerId: true, customer: { select: { displayName: true } } },
  });

  const result: RunResult = { runMonth, created: [], skipped: [] };
  for (const row of rows) {
    const skip = (reason: RunSkipReason) =>
      result.skipped.push({ customerId: row.customerId, displayName: row.customer.displayName, reason });
    try {
      const outcome = await deps.run((tx) => draftForCustomer(tx, row.customerId, ctx));
      if ("invoiceId" in outcome) {
        result.created.push({ invoiceId: outcome.invoiceId, customerId: row.customerId });
      } else {
        skip(outcome.reason);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof ForbiddenError) throw error;
      if (isMonthlyDuplicate(error)) {
        skip("alreadyInvoiced");
      } else {
        // Fixed words only: never the error (it may hold names or addresses).
        console.error("A monthly invoice draft could not be created.");
        skip("failed");
      }
    }
  }
  return result;
}
```

### Task 8: Issuing (one and all), invoice numbers, voiding and the invoice history

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/core/src/invoices/issue.ts`, `issue.db.test.ts`
- Create: `packages/core/src/invoices/activity.ts`
- Modify: `packages/core/src/invoices/index.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 4 (`issueProblems`, `sumLineCents`, `formatBillingAddress`, `formatInvoiceReference`); Task 5 (`InvoiceStore`, `INVOICE_MESSAGES`, `InvoiceIssueError`, `allocatedByInvoice`, `billToIdFor`, `lockCustomersInOrder`, `lockInvoiceRows`, `logInvoiceEvent`, `readInvoiceSettings`, `buildInvoiceSnapshot`, `bankDetailsReady`, `reasonSchema`; test helpers); Task 6 (`createOnceOffDraft`, `saveInvoiceDraft` in tests); `contentHash` (`quotes/content.ts`); `addDays`, `fromDateString`, `toDateString`, `todaySA`, `yearSA` (`quotes/reference.ts`); `TxRunner` (type).
- Produces:
  - `allocateInvoiceNumber(store: Pick<Db, "$queryRaw">, year: number): Promise<number>` (no role check, NOT in the barrel)
  - `issueInvoice(store, actor, id): Promise<{ id: string; reference: string }>`: lock order customers (the work customer and the bill-to, smaller id first), then the invoice; re-reads after the locks; resolves the bill-to again (refuses with `INVOICE_MESSAGES.billingChanged` if it changed between the first read and the locks); refuses without bank details (`InvoiceIssueError([INVOICE_MESSAGES.bankMissing])`); an empty due date becomes today plus `onceOffDueDays`; `issueProblems` must be empty; then the number, the frozen recipient, `bankSnapshot`, `totalCents`, `contentHash`, `emailStatus` NONE, audit `invoice.issued` `{ lineCount }`.
  - `issueAllDrafts(store, actor, deps: { run: TxRunner; only?: readonly string[] }): Promise<{ issued: { id: string; reference: string }[]; failed: { id: string; problem: string }[] }>`: each draft (oldest first) in its own transaction.
  - `voidInvoice(store, actor, id, reason: string): Promise<void>`: ISSUED only, no allocation from a non-voided payment, reason 1 to 300 characters; audit `invoice.voided` with no details.
  - `listInvoiceActivity(store, actor, id, limit?)` -> `{ id; at; action; actorType; actorId; after; actorName }[]` (`activity.ts`).

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { DomainError, ForbiddenError } from "../customers/access";
import { formatCustomerNumber } from "../customers/number";
import { addDays, toDateString, todaySA, yearSA } from "../quotes/reference";
import { dbTestsEnabled, withRollback } from "../testing/rollback";
import { listInvoiceActivity } from "./activity";
import { createOnceOffDraft, saveInvoiceDraft } from "./drafts";
import { issueAllDrafts, issueInvoice, voidInvoice } from "./issue";
import { invoiceSnapshotSchema } from "./schemas";
import { InvoiceIssueError } from "./store";
import {
  auditFor,
  crew,
  fillBankDetails,
  makeBillingCustomer,
  makeIssuedInvoice,
  makePayment,
  manager,
  sameTx,
} from "./test-helpers";

type Tx = Parameters<Parameters<typeof withRollback>[0]>[0];

/** A once-off draft with one line, made through the real draft functions. */
async function onceOffDraft(tx: Tx, customerId: string, amountCents = 50000, dueDate: string | null = null) {
  const { id } = await createOnceOffDraft(tx, manager, { customerId });
  await saveInvoiceDraft(tx, manager, id, {
    version: 1,
    dueDate,
    lines: [{ id: null, kind: "ONCE_OFF", description: "Garden clean-up", amountCents }],
  });
  return id;
}

async function prepare(tx: Tx) {
  await fillBankDetails(tx);
  await tx.invoiceSettings.update({ where: { id: "singleton" }, data: { onceOffDueDays: 14 } });
}

describe.skipIf(!dbTestsEnabled)("issuing an invoice", () => {
  it("numbers it, freezes the recipient, bank details and total, fingerprints it and audits fixed words", async () => {
    await withRollback(async (tx) => {
      await prepare(tx);
      const c = await makeBillingCustomer(tx);
      const id = await onceOffDraft(tx, c.customerId);
      await expect(issueInvoice(tx, crew, id)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ForbiddenError);
      const issued = await issueInvoice(tx, manager, id);
      expect(issued.reference).toMatch(new RegExp(`^INV-${yearSA()}-\\d{4,}$`));
      const row = await tx.invoice.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id } });
      expect(row.status).toBe("ISSUED");
      expect(row.numberYear).toBe(yearSA());
      expect(row.totalCents).toBe(50000);
      expect(row.recipientName).toBe(c.displayName);
      expect(row.recipientAddress).toBe("1 Test Road, Testville");
      expect(toDateString(row.dueDate ?? new Date(0))).toBe(addDays(todaySA(), 14));
      expect(row.issuedById).toBe(manager.id);
      expect(row.contentHash).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/);
      expect(row.emailStatus).toBe("NONE");
      const snapshot = invoiceSnapshotSchema.parse(row.bankSnapshot);
      expect(snapshot.bank.accountNumber).toBe("1234567890");
      expect(snapshot.paymentReference).toBe(formatCustomerNumber(c.number));

      const audit = await auditFor(tx, "invoice", id);
      expect(audit.map((a) => a.action)).toEqual(["invoice.drafted", "invoice.issued"]);
      expect(audit[1]?.after).toEqual({ lineCount: 1 });
      const text = JSON.stringify(audit);
      expect(text).not.toContain(c.displayName);
      expect(text).not.toContain("Test Road");

      const history = await listInvoiceActivity(tx, manager, id);
      expect(history.map((h) => h.action)).toEqual(["invoice.issued", "invoice.drafted"]);

      await expect(issueInvoice(tx, manager, id)).rejects.toThrow("Only a draft can be issued.");
    });
  });

  it("refuses without bank details, and lists every problem of an incomplete draft", async () => {
    await withRollback(async (tx) => {
      await tx.invoiceSettings.update({
        where: { id: "singleton" },
        data: { bankName: "", accountHolder: "", accountNumber: "", branchCode: "" },
      });
      const c = await makeBillingCustomer(tx);
      const id = await onceOffDraft(tx, c.customerId);
      const noBank = await issueInvoice(tx, manager, id).catch((e: unknown) => e);
      expect(noBank).toBeInstanceOf(InvoiceIssueError);
      expect((noBank as InvoiceIssueError).problems).toEqual([
        "Enter ACE's bank details in Invoice settings before issuing.",
      ]);

      await fillBankDetails(tx);
      const empty = (await createOnceOffDraft(tx, manager, { customerId: c.customerId })).id;
      const problems = await issueInvoice(tx, manager, empty).catch((e: unknown) => e);
      expect((problems as InvoiceIssueError).problems).toEqual([
        "Add at least one line.",
        "The total must be above zero.",
      ]);
      const late = await onceOffDraft(tx, c.customerId, 1000, "2020-01-31");
      const past = await issueInvoice(tx, manager, late).catch((e: unknown) => e);
      expect((past as InvoiceIssueError).problems).toEqual([
        "The due date has passed. Change it before issuing.",
      ]);
      const rows = await tx.invoice.findMany({ where: { id: { in: [id, empty, late] } }, select: { status: true } });
      expect(rows.map((r) => r.status)).toEqual(["DRAFT", "DRAFT", "DRAFT"]);
    });
  });

  it("addresses a complex's invoice to its agency as the billing stands at issue", async () => {
    await withRollback(async (tx) => {
      await prepare(tx);
      const agency = await makeBillingCustomer(tx);
      const complex = await makeBillingCustomer(tx);
      const id = await onceOffDraft(tx, complex.customerId);
      // the complex is put under the agency after the draft was made
      await tx.customer.update({
        where: { id: complex.customerId },
        data: { managedById: agency.customerId, billsToManager: true },
      });
      await issueInvoice(tx, manager, id);
      const row = await tx.invoice.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id } });
      expect(row.billToCustomerId).toBe(agency.customerId);
      expect(row.customerId).toBe(complex.customerId);
      expect(row.recipientName).toBe(agency.displayName);
      expect(invoiceSnapshotSchema.parse(row.bankSnapshot).paymentReference).toBe(
        formatCustomerNumber(agency.number),
      );
    });
  });

  it("gives consecutive numbers, and issue-all carries on past a draft that cannot be issued", async () => {
    await withRollback(async (tx) => {
      await prepare(tx);
      const c = await makeBillingCustomer(tx);
      const a = await onceOffDraft(tx, c.customerId, 1000);
      const bad = (await createOnceOffDraft(tx, manager, { customerId: c.customerId })).id;
      const b = await onceOffDraft(tx, c.customerId, 2000);
      const result = await issueAllDrafts(tx, manager, { run: sameTx(tx), only: [a, bad, b] });
      expect(result.issued.map((i) => i.id).sort()).toEqual([a, b].sort());
      expect(result.failed).toEqual([{ id: bad, problem: "Add at least one line.\nThe total must be above zero." }]);
      const rows = await tx.invoice.findMany({
        where: { id: { in: [a, b] } },
        select: { numberSeq: true },
      });
      const seqs = rows.map((r) => r.numberSeq ?? 0).sort((x, y) => x - y);
      expect(seqs).toHaveLength(2);
      expect((seqs[1] ?? 0) - (seqs[0] ?? 0)).toBe(1);
    });
  });
});

describe.skipIf(!dbTestsEnabled)("voiding an invoice", () => {
  it("is refused while a live payment is allocated, then voids with the reason kept off the audit line", async () => {
    await withRollback(async (tx) => {
      const c = await makeBillingCustomer(tx);
      const id = await makeIssuedInvoice(tx, { customerId: c.customerId, dueDate: "2099-01-31" });
      const payment = await makePayment(tx, {

      );
      await tx.payment.update({ where: { id: payment }, data: { voidedAt: new Date(), voidReason: "x" } });
      await expect(voidInvoice(tx, manager, id, "   ")).rejects.toThrow("Say why in a few words.");
      await voidInvoice(tx, manager, id, "ZZ wrong amount");
      const row = await tx.invoice.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id } });
      expect(row.status).toBe("VOID");
      expect(row.voidReason).toBe("ZZ wrong amount");
      expect(row.voidedById).toBe(manager.id);
      const audit = await auditFor(tx, "invoice", id);
      expect(audit.map((a) => a.action)).toEqual(["invoice.voided"]);
      expect(JSON.stringify(audit)).not.toContain("ZZ wrong amount");
      await expect(voidInvoice(tx, manager, id, "again")).rejects.toThrow(
        "Only an issued invoice can be voided. Delete a draft instead.",
      );
    });
  });

  it("refuses a draft and crew", async () => {
    await withRollback(async (tx) => {
      const c = await makeBillingCustomer(tx);
      const draft = await tx.invoice.create({
        data: { customerId: c.customerId, billToCustomerId: c.customerId, kind: "ONCE_OFF" },
        select: { id: true },
      });
      await expect(voidInvoice(tx, crew, draft.id, "x")).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ForbiddenError);
      await expect(voidInvoice(tx, manager, draft.id, "x")).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(DomainError);
      const row = await tx.invoice.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: draft.id }, select: { status: true } });
      expect(row.status).toBe("DRAFT");
      expect(await auditFor(tx, "invoice", draft.id)).toEqual([]);
    });
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write `packages/core/src/invoices/issue.ts`**

```ts
import { assertRole, DomainError, ForbiddenError, MANAGEMENT, type Actor } from "../customers/access";
import type { Db } from "../db";
import type { Prisma } from "../generated/prisma/client";
import type { TxRunner } from "../portal/access";
import { contentHash } from "../quotes/content";
import { addDays, fromDateString, toDateString, todaySA, yearSA } from "../quotes/reference";
import { formatBillingAddress, issueProblems, sumLineCents } from "./money";
import { formatInvoiceReference } from "./reference";
import { bankDetailsReady, reasonSchema } from "./schemas";
import { buildInvoiceSnapshot, readInvoiceSettings } from "./settings";
import {
  allocatedByInvoice,
  billToIdFor,
  INVOICE_MESSAGES,
  InvoiceIssueError,
  lockCustomersInOrder,
  lockInvoiceRows,
  logInvoiceEvent,
  type InvoiceStore,
} from "./store";

/** The next invoice number for the year, taken with one atomic statement (no repeats, no gaps). */
export async function allocateInvoiceNumber(store: Pick<Db, "$queryRaw">, year: number): Promise<number> {
  const rows = await store.$queryRaw<{ lastSeq: number }[]>`
    insert into invoice_counters ("year", "lastSeq") values (${year}, 1)
    on conflict ("year") do update set "lastSeq" = invoice_counters."lastSeq" + 1
    returning "lastSeq"`;
  const seq = rows[0]?.lastSeq;
  if (seq === undefined) throw new Error("Could not allocate an invoice number.");
  return Number(seq);
}

const ISSUE_SELECT = {
  id: true,
  status: true,
  kind: true,
  customerId: true,
  serviceMonth: true,
  dueDate: true,
  customer: { select: { id: true, managedById: true, billsToManager: true } },
  lines: { orderBy: { sortOrder: "asc" }, select: { kind: true, description: true, amountCents: true } },
} as const satisfies Prisma.InvoiceSelect;

/**
 * Issues one draft: gives the number and freezes the recipient, ACE's bank and business details and the total.
 * Lock order: the work customer and the bill-to customer (smaller id first), then the invoice. Emails are NOT
 * sent here: the app sends them after the transaction commits.
 */
export async function issueInvoice(
  store: InvoiceStore,
  actor: Actor,
  id: string,
): Promise<{ id: string; reference: string }> {
  assertRole(actor, MANAGEMENT);
  const read = async () => {
    const inv = await store.invoice.findUnique({ where: { id }, select: ISSUE_SELECT });
    if (!inv) throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.notFound);
    if (inv.status !== "DRAFT") throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.onlyDraftIssue);
    return inv;
  };
  const first = await read();
  const billToId = billToIdFor(first.customer);
  await lockCustomersInOrder(store, [first.customerId, billToId]);
  await lockInvoiceRows(store, [id]);
  const inv = await read();
  if (billToIdFor(inv.customer) !== billToId) throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.billingChanged);

  const settings = await readInvoiceSettings(store);
  if (!bankDetailsReady(settings)) throw new InvoiceIssueError([INVOICE_MESSAGES.bankMissing]);
  const today = todaySA();
  const dueDate = inv.dueDate ? toDateString(inv.dueDate) : addDays(today, settings.onceOffDueDays);
  const problems = issueProblems({ lines: inv.lines, dueDate }, today);
  if (problems.length > 0) throw new InvoiceIssueError(problems);

  const billTo = await store.customer.findUniqueOrThrow({
    where: { id: billToId },
    select: {
      id: true,
      number: true,
      displayName: true,
      billingLine1: true,
      billingLine2: true,
      billingSuburb: true,
      billingCity: true,
      billingPostalCode: true,
    },
  });
  const totalCents = sumLineCents(inv.lines);
  const numberYear = yearSA();
  const numberSeq = await allocateInvoiceNumber(store, numberYear);
  const reference = formatInvoiceReference({ numberYear, numberSeq }) ?? "";
  const recipientName = billTo.displayName;
  const recipientAddress = formatBillingAddress(billTo);
  const snapshot = await buildInvoiceSnapshot(store, settings, billTo.number);
  const hash = contentHash({
    reference,
    kind: inv.kind,
    serviceMonth: inv.serviceMonth ? toDateString(inv.serviceMonth) : null,
    issuedOn: today,
    dueDate,
    customerId: inv.customerId,
    billTo: { id: billTo.id, name: recipientName, address: recipientAddress },
    lines: inv.lines.map((l) => ({ kind: l.kind, description: l.description, amountCents: l.amountCents })),
    totalCents,
    snapshot,
  });

  await store.invoice.update({
    where: { id },
    data: {
      status: "ISSUED",
      numberYear,
      numberSeq,
      dueDate: fromDateString(dueDate),
      issuedAt: new Date(),
      issuedById: actor.id,
      billToCustomerId: billTo.id,
      totalCents,
      recipientName,
      recipientAddress,
      bankSnapshot: snapshot as unknown as Prisma.InputJsonValue,
      contentHash: hash,
      emailStatus: "NONE",
      emailedAt: null,
    },
    select: { id: true },
  });
  await logInvoiceEvent(store, actor, "invoice.issued", id, { lineCount: inv.lines.length });
  return { id, reference };
}

/** "Issue all drafts": every draft (oldest first), each in its own transaction so one failure does not block the rest. */
export async function issueAllDrafts(
  store: InvoiceStore,
  actor: Actor,
  deps: { run: TxRunner; only?: readonly string[] },
): Promise<{ issued: { id: string; reference: string }[]; failed: { id: string; problem: string }[] }> {
  assertRole(actor, MANAGEMENT);
  const drafts = await store.invoice.findMany({
    where: { status: "DRAFT", ...(deps.only ? { id: { in: [...deps.only] } } : {}) },
    orderBy: [{ createdAt: "asc" }, { id: "asc" }],
    select: { id: true },
  });
  const issued: { id: string; reference: string }[] = [];
  const failed: { id: string; problem: string }[] = [];
  for (const d of drafts) {
    try {
      issued.push(await deps.run((tx) => issueInvoice(tx, actor, d.id)));
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof ForbiddenError) throw error;
      if (error instanceof DomainError) {
        failed.push({ id: d.id, problem: error.message });
      } else {
        console.error("An invoice could not be issued.");
        failed.push({ id: d.id, problem: "Something went wrong. Try this one again." });
      }
    }
  }
  return { issued, failed };
}

/**
 * Voids an issued invoice that has no money allocated from a live payment. The reason is kept on the invoice
 * (scrubbed on anonymise) and never written to the audit line. Lock order: customers, then the invoice.
 */
export async function voidInvoice(store: InvoiceStore, actor: Actor, id: string, reason: string): Promise<void> {
  assertRole(actor, MANAGEMENT);
  const text = reasonSchema.safeParse(reason);
  if (!text.success) throw new DomainError(text.error.issues[0]?.message ?? "Say why in a few words.");
  const read = async () => {
    const inv = await store.invoice.findUnique({
      where: { id },
      select: { status: true, customerId: true, billToCustomerId: true },
    });
    if (!inv) throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.notFound);
    if (inv.status !== "ISSUED") throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.onlyIssuedVoid);
    return inv;
  };
  const first = await read();
  await lockCustomersInOrder(store, [first.customerId, first.billToCustomerId]);
  await lockInvoiceRows(store, [id]);
  await read();
  if (((await allocatedByInvoice(store, [id])).get(id) ?? 0) > 0) {
    throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.hasAllocations);
  }
  const { count } = await store.invoice.updateMany({
    where: { id, status: "ISSUED" },
    data: { status: "VOID", voidedAt: new Date(), voidReason: text.data, voidedById: actor.id },
  });
  if (count !== 1) throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.onlyIssuedVoid);
  await logInvoiceEvent(store, actor, "invoice.voided", id);
}
```

```ts
import { assertRole, MANAGEMENT, type Actor } from "../customers/access";
import type { InvoiceStore } from "./store";

/** The invoice's history for the staff screen, newest first, with staff names. */
export async function listInvoiceActivity(store: InvoiceStore, actor: Actor, id: string, limit = 100) {
  assertRole(actor, MANAGEMENT);
  const entries = await store.auditLog.findMany({
    where: { entityType: "invoice", entityId: id },
    orderBy: { at: "desc" },
    take: limit,
    select: { id: true, at: true, action: true, actorType: true, actorId: true, after: true },
  });
  const ids = [
    ...new Set(
      entries
        .filter((e) => e.actorType === "STAFF")
        .map((e) => e.actorId)
        .filter((v): v is string => Boolean(v)),
    ),
  ];
  const staff = await store.staffUser.findMany({
    where: { id: { in: ids } },
    select: { id: true, name: true },
  });
  const names = new Map(staff.map((s) => [s.id, s.name]));
  return entries.map((e) => ({
    ...e,
    actorName:
      e.actorType === "CUSTOMER"
        ? "Client (portal)"
        : e.actorId
          ? (names.get(e.actorId) ?? "Staff")
          : "System",
  }));
}

/** Plain words for the history list. Unknown actions show as they are stored. */
export const INVOICE_ACTION_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  "invoice.drafted": "Draft created",
  "invoice.deleted_draft": "Draft deleted",
  "invoice.issued": "Issued",
  "invoice.voided": "Voided",
  "invoice.emailed": "Emailed",
  "invoice.email_failed": "Email not sent",
  "invoice.payment_allocated": "Payment allocated",
  "invoice.payment_unallocated": "Payment allocation removed",
  "invoice.report_confirmed": "Reported payment confirmed",
  "invoice.report_dismissed": "Reported payment dismissed",
};
```

### Task 9: Payments, allocations and reported payments

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/core/src/invoices/payments.ts`, `payments.db.test.ts`
- Create: `packages/core/src/invoices/reports.ts`, `reports.db.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/core/src/invoices/index.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 4 (`formatInvoiceReference`, `MAX_PAYMENT_CENTS`); Task 5 (`PaymentInput`, `EARLIEST_PAYMENT_DATE`, `MAX_REFERENCE`, `MAX_NOTE`, `reasonSchema`, `InvoiceStore`, `INVOICE_MESSAGES`, `allocatedByInvoice`, `allocatedByPayment`, `lockInvoiceRows`, `lockPaymentRow`, `lockReportRow`, `logInvoiceEvent`, `logPaymentEvent`; test helpers); `findEditableCustomer`, `lockCustomer` (`customers/store.ts`); `fromDateString`, `isValidDateString`, `toDateString`, `todaySA` (`quotes/reference.ts`).
- Produces (`payments.ts`):
  - `recordPayment(store, actor, input: PaymentInput): Promise<{ id: string }>`: lock order payer, then the allocated invoices (by id), then (when `input.reportId` is set) the report, which becomes CONFIRMED in the same transaction. Audit `payment.recorded` `{ allocationCount, fromReport }`, `invoice.payment_allocated` `{ paymentId }` per invoice, `invoice.report_confirmed` `{ reportId, paymentId }`.
  - `allocatePayment(store, actor, input: { paymentId: string; invoiceId: string; amountCents: number }): Promise<void>`: payer, invoice, payment; adds to an existing allocation for the same pair. Audit `payment.allocated` `{ invoiceId }` and `invoice.payment_allocated` `{ paymentId }`.
  - `unallocatePayment(store, actor, allocationId: string): Promise<void>`: audit `payment.unallocated` `{ invoiceId }` and `invoice.payment_unallocated` `{ paymentId }`.
  - `voidPayment(store, actor, paymentId: string, reason: string): Promise<void>`: audit `payment.voided` `{ allocationCount }`.
  - `getPayment(store, actor, id)` -> `{ id; receivedOn: string; amountCents; reference; note; voidedAt; voidReason; createdAt; payer: { id; number; displayName; anonymisedAt }; allocations: { id; amountCents; invoiceId; reference: string | null; forName: string | null }[]; allocatedCents; unallocatedCents }` or null; `type PaymentView`.
  - `listPayments(store, actor, query: { payerCustomerId?: string; page?: number; pageSize?: number })` -> `{ items: { id; receivedOn: string; amountCents; reference; voided: boolean; unallocatedCents; payer: { id; number; displayName } }[]; total; page; pageSize }`.
  - `type OpenInvoice = { id: string; reference: string; forName: string | null; dueDate: string; balanceCents: number }`; `openInvoicesForPayer(store, actor, payerCustomerId): Promise<OpenInvoice[]>` (issued, billed to the payer, balance above zero, oldest due first).
- Produces (`reports.ts`):
  - `listPaymentReports(store, actor, query?: { status?: "WAITING" | "ALL" })` -> `{ id; paidOn: string; reference: string | null; status; createdAt; reportedBy: string | null; invoice: { id; reference: string | null; totalCents: number | null; customer: { id; displayName }; billTo: { id; number; displayName } } }[]` (WAITING by default, newest first, at most 200).
  - `getPaymentReport(store, actor, id)` -> `{ id; status; paidOn: string; reference: string | null; invoiceId: string; billToCustomerId: string } | null`.
  - `dismissPaymentReport(store, actor, id): Promise<void>`: audit `invoice.report_dismissed` `{ reportId }`.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { ForbiddenError } from "../customers/access";
import { addDays, todaySA } from "../quotes/reference";
import { dbTestsEnabled, withRollback } from "../testing/rollback";
import { getInvoice } from "./drafts";
import {
  allocatePayment,
  getPayment,
  listPayments,
  openInvoicesForPayer,
  recordPayment,
  unallocatePayment,
  voidPayment,
} from "./payments";
import type { PaymentInput } from "./schemas";
import { auditFor, crew, makeBillingCustomer, makeIssuedInvoice, makePayment, manager } from "./test-helpers";

type Tx = Parameters<Parameters<typeof withRollback>[0]>[0];

const input = (over: Partial<PaymentInput> & { payerCustomerId: string }): PaymentInput => ({
  receivedOn: todaySA(),
  amountCents: 100000,
  reference: "ZZ BANK REF 77",
  note: "ZZ note",
  allocations: [],
  reportId: null,
  ...over,
});

async function agencyWithTwoComplexes(tx: Tx) {
  const agency = await makeBillingCustomer(tx);
  const a = await makeBillingCustomer(tx, { managedById: agency.customerId });
  const b = await makeBillingCustomer(tx, { managedById: agency.customerId });
  const invA = await makeIssuedInvoice(tx, {
    customerId: a.customerId,
    billToCustomerId: agency.customerId,
    dueDate: "2026-10-31",
    totalCents: 60000,
  });
  const invB = await makeIssuedInvoice(tx, {
    customerId: b.customerId,
    billToCustomerId: agency.customerId,
    dueDate: "2026-11-30",
    totalCents: 40000,
  });
  return { agency, a, b, invA, invB };
}

describe.skipIf(!dbTestsEnabled)("recording payments", () => {
  it("records one EFT from an agency over two complexes' invoices, with fixed-word audit lines", async () => {
    await withRollback(async (tx) => {
      const { agency, a, invA, invB } = await agencyWithTwoComplexes(tx);
      const open = await openInvoicesForPayer(tx, manager, agency.customerId);
      expect(open.map((o) => [o.id, o.balanceCents])).toEqual([
        [invA, 60000],
        [invB, 40000],
      ]);
      expect(open[0]?.forName).toBe(a.displayName);

      await expect(
        recordPayment(tx, crew, input({ payerCustomerId: agency.customerId })),
      ).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ForbiddenError);
      const { id } = await recordPayment(
        tx,
        manager,
        input({
          payerCustomerId: agency.customerId,
          amountCents: 120000,
          allocations: [
            { invoiceId: invA, amountCents: 60000 },
            { invoiceId: invB, amountCents: 40000 },
          ],
        }),
      );
      const view = await getPayment(tx, manager, id);
      expect(view).toMatchObject({ allocatedCents: 100000, unallocatedCents: 20000, reference: "ZZ BANK REF 77" });
      expect((await getInvoice(tx, manager, invA))?.displayStatus).toBe("PAID");
      expect((await getInvoice(tx, manager, invB))?.displayStatus).toBe("PAID");

      const paymentAudit = await auditFor(tx, "payment", id);
      expect(paymentAudit.map((l) => l.action)).toEqual(["payment.recorded"]);
      expect(paymentAudit[0]?.after).toEqual({ allocationCount: 2, fromReport: false });
      const invoiceAudit = await auditFor(tx, "invoice", invA);
      expect(invoiceAudit.map((l) => l.after)).toEqual([{ paymentId: id }]);
      const text = JSON.stringify([...paymentAudit, ...invoiceAudit]);
      expect(text).not.toContain("ZZ BANK REF 77");
      expect(text).not.toContain("ZZ note");

      const listed = await listPayments(tx, manager, { payerCustomerId: agency.customerId });
      expect(listed.items.map((p) => [p.id, p.unallocatedCents])).toEqual([[id, 20000]]);
    });
  });

  it("refuses allocations that break the rules", async () => {
    await withRollback(async (tx) => {
      const { agency, invA } = await agencyWithTwoComplexes(tx);
      const other = await makeBillingCustomer(tx);
      const otherInv = await makeIssuedInvoice(tx, { customerId: other.customerId, dueDate: "2026-10-31" });
      const draft = await tx.invoice.create({
        data: { customerId: agency.customerId, billToCustomerId: agency.customerId, kind: "ONCE_OFF" },
        select: { id: true },
      });
      const payer = agency.customerId;
      await expect(
        recordPayment(tx, manager, input({ payerCustomerId: payer, allocations: [{ invoiceId: otherInv, amountCents: 100 }] })),
      ).rejects.toThrow("That invoice is not billed to this payer.");
      await expect(
        recordPayment(tx, manager, input({ payerCustomerId: payer, allocations: [{ invoiceId: draft.id, amountCents: 100 }] })),
      ).rejects.toThrow("Payments can only be allocated to issued invoices.");
      await expect(
        recordPayment(tx, manager, input({ payerCustomerId: payer, allocations: [{ invoiceId: invA, amountCents: 60001 }] })),
      ).rejects.toThrow("That is more than the invoice still owes.");
      await expect(
        recordPayment(
          tx,
          manager,
          input({
            payerCustomerId: payer,
            amountCents: 1000,
            allocations: [{ invoiceId: invA, amountCents: 1001 }],
          }),
        ),
      ).rejects.toThrow("The amounts allocated add up to more than the payment.");
      await expect(
        recordPayment(
          tx,
          manager,
          input({
            payerCustomerId: payer,
            allocations: [
              { invoiceId: invA, amountCents: 100 },
              { invoiceId: invA, amountCents: 100 },
            ],
          }),
        ),
      ).rejects.toThrow("Each invoice can be listed only once.");
      await expect(
        recordPayment(tx, manager, input({ payerCustomerId: payer, receivedOn: addDays(todaySA(), 1) })),
      ).rejects.toThrow("Choose the date the money arrived (not in the future).");
      expect(await tx.payment.count({ where: { payerCustomerId: payer } })).toBe(0);
    });
  });

  it("allocates credit later, adds to an existing allocation, and removes one", async () => {
    await withRollback(async (tx) => {
      const { agency, invA } = await agencyWithTwoComplexes(tx);
      const { id } = await recordPayment(tx, manager, input({ payerCustomerId: agency.customerId, amountCents: 50000 }));
      await allocatePayment(tx, manager, { paymentId: id, invoiceId: invA, amountCents: 20000 });
      await allocatePayment(tx, manager, { paymentId: id, invoiceId: invA, amountCents: 10000 });
      const rows = await tx.paymentAllocation.findMany({ where: { paymentId: id }, select: { id: true, amountCents: true } });
      expect(rows.map((r) => r.amountCents)).toEqual([30000]);
      await expect(
        allocatePayment(tx, manager, { paymentId: id, invoiceId: invA, amountCents: 20001 }),
      ).rejects.toThrow("That is more than is left of the payment.");
      await unallocatePayment(tx, manager, rows[0]?.id ?? "");
      expect((await getPayment(tx, manager, id))?.unallocatedCents).toBe(50000);
      expect((await auditFor(tx, "payment", id)).map((l) => l.action)).toEqual([
        "payment.recorded",
        "payment.allocated",
        "payment.allocated",
        "payment.unallocated",
      ]);
    });
  });

  it("voiding a payment stops its allocations counting and blocks further changes", async () => {
    await withRollback(async (tx) => {
      const { agency, invA } = await agencyWithTwoComplexes(tx);
      const id = await makePayment(tx, {
        payerCustomerId: agency.customerId,
        amountCents: 60000,
        receivedOn: "2026-11-01",
        allocations: [{ invoiceId: invA, amountCents: 60000 }],
      });
      expect((await getInvoice(tx, manager, invA))?.displayStatus).toBe("PAID");
      await expect(voidPayment(tx, manager, id, " ")).rejects.toThrow("Say why in a few words.");
      await voidPayment(tx, manager, id, "ZZ bounced");
      expect((await getInvoice(tx, manager, invA))?.balanceCents).toBe(60000);
      const allocation = await tx.paymentAllocation.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { paymentId: id }, select: { id: true } });
      await expect(unallocatePayment(tx, manager, allocation.id)).rejects.toThrow("This payment has been voided.");
      await expect(
        allocatePayment(tx, manager, { paymentId: id, invoiceId: invA, amountCents: 1 }),
      ).rejects.toThrow("This payment has been voided.");
      await expect(voidPayment(tx, manager, id, "again")).rejects.toThrow("This payment has been voided.");
      const audit = await auditFor(tx, "payment", id);
      expect(audit.map((l) => l.after)).toEqual([{ allocationCount: 1 }]);
      expect(JSON.stringify(audit)).not.toContain("ZZ bounced");
    });
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { ForbiddenError } from "../customers/access";
import { fromDateString, todaySA } from "../quotes/reference";
import { dbTestsEnabled, withRollback } from "../testing/rollback";
import { recordPayment } from "./payments";
import { dismissPaymentReport, getPaymentReport, listPaymentReports } from "./reports";
import { auditFor, crew, makeBillingCustomer, makeIssuedInvoice, manager } from "./test-helpers";

type Tx = Parameters<Parameters<typeof withRollback>[0]>[0];

/** Stage 5b writes reports from the portal; here they are made directly. */
async function report(tx: Tx, invoiceId: string) {
  const r = await tx.paymentReport.create({
    data: { invoiceId, paidOn: fromDateString("2026-11-20"), reference: "ZZ CLIENT REF 9" },
    select: { id: true },
  });
  return r.id;
}

describe.skipIf(!dbTestsEnabled)("reported payments", () => {
  it("are listed while waiting, and confirming records the payment in the same step", async () => {
    await withRollback(async (tx) => {
      const c = await makeBillingCustomer(tx);
      const inv = await makeIssuedInvoice(tx, { customerId: c.customerId, dueDate: "2099-01-31", totalCents: 50000 });
      const id = await report(tx, inv);
      await expect(listPaymentReports(tx, crew)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ForbiddenError);
      const waiting = await listPaymentReports(tx, manager);
      const row = waiting.find((r) => r.id === id);
      expect(row).toMatchObject({ status: "WAITING", paidOn: "2026-11-20", reference: "ZZ CLIENT REF 9" });
      expect(row?.invoice.billTo.id).toBe(c.customerId);
      expect(await getPaymentReport(tx, manager, id)).toMatchObject({ invoiceId: inv, billToCustomerId: c.customerId });

      const payment = await recordPayment(tx, manager, {
        payerCustomerId: c.customerId,
        receivedOn: todaySA(),
        amountCents: 50000,
        reference: null,
        note: null,
        allocations: [{ invoiceId: inv, amountCents: 50000 }],
        reportId: id,
      });
      const after = await tx.paymentReport.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id } });
      expect(after.status).toBe("CONFIRMED");
      expect(after.resolvedById).toBe(manager.id);
      const audit = await auditFor(tx, "invoice", inv);
      expect(audit.map((l) => l.action).sort()).toEqual(["invoice.payment_allocated", "invoice.report_confirmed"]);
      expect(audit.find((l) => l.action === "invoice.report_confirmed")?.after).toEqual({
        reportId: id,
        paymentId: payment.id,
      });
      expect(JSON.stringify(audit)).not.toContain("ZZ CLIENT REF 9");
      expect((await listPaymentReports(tx, manager)).some((r) => r.id === id)).toBe(false);
      expect((await listPaymentReports(tx, manager, { status: "ALL" })).some((r) => r.id === id)).toBe(true);

      await expect(
        recordPayment(tx, manager, {
          payerCustomerId: c.customerId,
          receivedOn: todaySA(),
          amountCents: 100,
          reference: null,
          note: null,
          allocations: [],
          reportId: id,
        }),
      ).rejects.toThrow("This report has already been dealt with.");
    });
  });

  it("dismisses a report once, and refuses a report on someone else's invoice", async () => {
    await withRollback(async (tx) => {
      const c = await makeBillingCustomer(tx);
      const other = await makeBillingCustomer(tx);
      const inv = await makeIssuedInvoice(tx, { customerId: c.customerId, dueDate: "2099-01-31" });
      const id = await report(tx, inv);
      await expect(
        recordPayment(tx, manager, {
          payerCustomerId: other.customerId,
          receivedOn: todaySA(),
          amountCents: 100,
          reference: null,
          note: null,
          allocations: [],
          reportId: id,
        }),
      ).rejects.toThrow("That invoice is not billed to this payer.");
      await dismissPaymentReport(tx, manager, id);
      const row = await tx.paymentReport.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id } });
      expect(row.status).toBe("DISMISSED");
      expect(row.resolvedAt).toBeInstanceOf(Date);
      await expect(dismissPaymentReport(tx, manager, id)).rejects.toThrow("This report has already been dealt with.");
      const audit = await auditFor(tx, "invoice", inv);
      expect(audit).toEqual([{ action: "invoice.report_dismissed", after: { reportId: id }, actorId: manager.id }]);
    });
  });
});
```

```ts
import { assertRole, DomainError, MANAGEMENT, type Actor } from "../customers/access";
import { findEditableCustomer, lockCustomer } from "../customers/store";
import { fromDateString, isValidDateString, toDateString, todaySA } from "../quotes/reference";
import { MAX_PAYMENT_CENTS } from "./money";
import { formatInvoiceReference } from "./reference";
import { EARLIEST_PAYMENT_DATE, MAX_NOTE, MAX_REFERENCE, reasonSchema, type PaymentInput } from "./schemas";
import {
  allocatedByInvoice,
  allocatedByPayment,
  INVOICE_MESSAGES,
  lockInvoiceRows,
  lockPaymentRow,
  lockReportRow,
  logInvoiceEvent,
  logPaymentEvent,
  type InvoiceStore,
} from "./store";

type Allocation = { invoiceId: string; amountCents: number };

function checkPaymentInput(input: PaymentInput, today: string): void {
  if (
    !isValidDateString(input.receivedOn) ||
    input.receivedOn < EARLIEST_PAYMENT_DATE ||
    input.receivedOn > today
  ) {
    throw new DomainError("Choose the date the money arrived (not in the future).");
  }
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(input.amountCents) || input.amountCents <= 0 || input.amountCents > MAX_PAYMENT_CENTS) {
    throw new DomainError("Enter the amount received, for example 1150.00.");
  }
  if ((input.reference ?? "").length > MAX_REFERENCE || (input.note ?? "").length > MAX_NOTE) {
    throw new DomainError("Keep the reference to 100 characters and the note to 500.");
  }
  const ids = input.allocations.map((a) => a.invoiceId);
  if (new Set(ids).size !== ids.length) throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.duplicateInvoice);
  if (input.allocations.some((a) => !Number.isSafeInteger(a.amountCents) || a.amountCents <= 0)) {
    throw new DomainError("Each amount allocated must be above zero.");
  }
  if (input.allocations.reduce((t, a) => t + a.amountCents, 0) > input.amountCents) {
    throw new DomainError("The amounts allocated add up to more than the payment.");
  }
}

/** Every invoice must be issued, billed to the payer, and still owe at least the amount allocated to it. */
async function checkAllocations(store: InvoiceStore, payerId: string, allocations: readonly Allocation[]) {
  if (allocations.length === 0) return;
  const ids = allocations.map((a) => a.invoiceId);
  const invoices = await store.invoice.findMany({
    where: { id: { in: ids } },
    select: { id: true, status: true, billToCustomerId: true, totalCents: true },
  });
  const byId = new Map(invoices.map((i) => [i.id, i]));
  const allocated = await allocatedByInvoice(store, ids);
  for (const a of allocations) {
    const inv = byId.get(a.invoiceId);
    if (!inv) throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.notFound);
    if (inv.status !== "ISSUED") throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.notIssued);
    if (inv.billToCustomerId !== payerId) throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.notPayersInvoice);
    if (a.amountCents > (inv.totalCents ?? 0) - (allocated.get(inv.id) ?? 0)) {
      throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.overBalance);
    }
  }
}

async function checkReport(store: InvoiceStore, reportId: string, payerId: string) {
  const report = await store.paymentReport.findUnique({
    where: { id: reportId },
    select: { id: true, status: true, invoiceId: true, invoice: { select: { billToCustomerId: true } } },
  });
  if (!report) throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.reportNotFound);
  if (report.status !== "WAITING") throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.reportResolved);
  if (report.invoice.billToCustomerId !== payerId) throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.notPayersInvoice);
  return report;
}

/**
 * Records money received by EFT, optionally split over the payer's invoices, optionally confirming a reported
 * payment. Lock order: the payer, the invoices (by id), then the report. Unallocated money is a credit.
 */
export async function recordPayment(
  store: InvoiceStore,
  actor: Actor,
  input: PaymentInput,
): Promise<{ id: string }> {
  assertRole(actor, MANAGEMENT);
  checkPaymentInput(input, todaySA());
  const check = async () => {
    await findEditableCustomer(store, input.payerCustomerId);
    await checkAllocations(store, input.payerCustomerId, input.allocations);
    if (input.reportId) await checkReport(store, input.reportId, input.payerCustomerId);
  };
  await check();
  await lockCustomer(store, input.payerCustomerId);
  await lockInvoiceRows(store, input.allocations.map((a) => a.invoiceId));
  await check();

  const payment = await store.payment.create({
    data: {
      payerCustomerId: input.payerCustomerId,
      receivedOn: fromDateString(input.receivedOn),
      amountCents: input.amountCents,
      reference: input.reference,
      note: input.note,
      recordedById: actor.id,
      allocations: {
        create: input.allocations.map((a) => ({ invoiceId: a.invoiceId, amountCents: a.amountCents })),
      },
    },
    select: { id: true },
  });

  if (input.reportId) {
    await lockReportRow(store, input.reportId);
    const report = await checkReport(store, input.reportId, input.payerCustomerId);
    const { count } = await store.paymentReport.updateMany({
      where: { id: report.id, status: "WAITING" },
      data: { status: "CONFIRMED", resolvedAt: new Date(), resolvedById: actor.id },
    });
    if (count !== 1) throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.reportResolved);
    await logInvoiceEvent(store, actor, "invoice.report_confirmed", report.invoiceId, {
      reportId: report.id,
      paymentId: payment.id,
    });
  }
  await logPaymentEvent(store, actor, "payment.recorded", payment.id, {
    allocationCount: input.allocations.length,
    fromReport: Boolean(input.reportId),
  });
  for (const a of input.allocations) {
    await logInvoiceEvent(store, actor, "invoice.payment_allocated", a.invoiceId, { paymentId: payment.id });
  }
  return payment;
}

/** Allocates part of a payment's unallocated money to one invoice. Lock order: payer, invoice, payment. */
export async function allocatePayment(
  store: InvoiceStore,
  actor: Actor,
  input: { paymentId: string; invoiceId: string; amountCents: number },
): Promise<void> {
  assertRole(actor, MANAGEMENT);
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(input.amountCents) || input.amountCents <= 0) {
    throw new DomainError("Enter an amount above zero.");
  }
  const check = async () => {
    const p = await store.payment.findUnique({
      where: { id: input.paymentId },
      select: { id: true, payerCustomerId: true, amountCents: true, voidedAt: true },
    });
    if (!p) throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.paymentNotFound);
    if (p.voidedAt) throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.paymentVoided);
    await checkAllocations(store, p.payerCustomerId, [input]);
    const used = (await allocatedByPayment(store, [p.id])).get(p.id) ?? 0;
    if (input.amountCents > p.amountCents - used) throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.overPayment);
    return p;
  };
  const first = await check();
  await lockCustomer(store, first.payerCustomerId);
  await lockInvoiceRows(store, [input.invoiceId]);
  await lockPaymentRow(store, input.paymentId);
  await check();
  const existing = await store.paymentAllocation.findUnique({
    where: { paymentId_invoiceId: { paymentId: input.paymentId, invoiceId: input.invoiceId } },
    select: { id: true },
  });
  if (existing) {
    await store.paymentAllocation.update({
      where: { id: existing.id },
      data: { amountCents: { increment: input.amountCents } },
      select: { id: true },
    });
  } else {
    await store.paymentAllocation.create({
      data: { paymentId: input.paymentId, invoiceId: input.invoiceId, amountCents: input.amountCents },
      select: { id: true },
    });
  }
  await logPaymentEvent(store, actor, "payment.allocated", input.paymentId, { invoiceId: input.invoiceId });
  await logInvoiceEvent(store, actor, "invoice.payment_allocated", input.invoiceId, {
    paymentId: input.paymentId,
  });
}

/** Removes one allocation (the money becomes credit again). Lock order: payer, invoice, payment. */
export async function unallocatePayment(store: InvoiceStore, actor: Actor, allocationId: string): Promise<void> {
  assertRole(actor, MANAGEMENT);
  const read = async () => {
    const a = await store.paymentAllocation.findUnique({
      where: { id: allocationId },
      select: {
        id: true,
        invoiceId: true,
        paymentId: true,
        payment: { select: { payerCustomerId: true, voidedAt: true } },
      },
    });
    if (!a) throw new DomainError("That allocation could not be found.");
    if (a.payment.voidedAt) throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.paymentVoided);
    return a;
  };
  const first = await read();
  await lockCustomer(store, first.payment.payerCustomerId);
  await lockInvoiceRows(store, [first.invoiceId]);
  await lockPaymentRow(store, first.paymentId);
  const a = await read();
  await store.paymentAllocation.delete({ where: { id: a.id } });
  await logPaymentEvent(store, actor, "payment.unallocated", a.paymentId, { invoiceId: a.invoiceId });
  await logInvoiceEvent(store, actor, "invoice.payment_unallocated", a.invoiceId, { paymentId: a.paymentId });
}

/**
 * Voids a payment (for example a bounced EFT). Its allocation rows stay as history but stop counting, so the
 * invoices owe again. Lock order: payer, the allocated invoices, the payment.
 */
export async function voidPayment(store: InvoiceStore, actor: Actor, paymentId: string, reason: string): Promise<void> {
  assertRole(actor, MANAGEMENT);
  const text = reasonSchema.safeParse(reason);
  const read = async () => {
    const p = await store.payment.findUnique({
      where: { id: paymentId },
      select: { id: true, payerCustomerId: true, voidedAt: true, allocations: { select: { invoiceId: true } } },
    });
    if (!p) throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.paymentNotFound);
    if (p.voidedAt) throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.paymentVoided);
    return p;
  };
  const first = await read();
  if (!text.success) throw new DomainError(text.error.issues[0]?.message ?? "Say why in a few words.");
  await lockCustomer(store, first.payerCustomerId);
  await lockInvoiceRows(store, first.allocations.map((a) => a.invoiceId));
  await lockPaymentRow(store, paymentId);
  const p = await read();
  const { count } = await store.payment.updateMany({
    where: { id: paymentId, voidedAt: null },
    data: { voidedAt: new Date(), voidReason: text.data, voidedById: actor.id },
  });
  if (count !== 1) throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.paymentVoided);
  await logPaymentEvent(store, actor, "payment.voided", paymentId, { allocationCount: p.allocations.length });
}

/** One payment for the staff screen. Null when there is none. */
export async function getPayment(store: InvoiceStore, actor: Actor, id: string) {
  assertRole(actor, MANAGEMENT);
  const p = await store.payment.findUnique({
    where: { id },
    select: {
      id: true,
      receivedOn: true,
      amountCents: true,
      reference: true,
      note: true,
      voidedAt: true,
      voidReason: true,
      createdAt: true,
      payer: { select: { id: true, number: true, displayName: true, anonymisedAt: true } },
      allocations: {
        orderBy: { id: "asc" },
        select: {
          id: true,
          amountCents: true,
          invoice: {
            select: { id: true, numberYear: true, numberSeq: true, customer: { select: { id: true, displayName: true } } },
          },
        },
      },
    },
  });
  if (!p) return null;
  const allocatedCents = p.allocations.reduce((t, a) => t + a.amountCents, 0);
  return {
    ...p,
    receivedOn: toDateString(p.receivedOn),
    allocations: p.allocations.map((a) => ({
      id: a.id,
      amountCents: a.amountCents,
      invoiceId: a.invoice.id,
      reference: formatInvoiceReference(a.invoice),
      forName: a.invoice.customer.id !== p.payer.id ? a.invoice.customer.displayName : null,
    })),
    allocatedCents,
    unallocatedCents: p.voidedAt ? 0 : p.amountCents - allocatedCents,
  };
}
export type PaymentView = NonNullable<Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getPayment>>>;

function clampInt(value: unknown, min: number, max: number, fallback: number): number {
  const whole = typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value) ? Math.floor(value) : fallback;
  return Math.min(max, Math.max(min, whole));
}

export async function listPayments(
  store: InvoiceStore,
  actor: Actor,
  query: { payerCustomerId?: string | undefined; page?: number | undefined; pageSize?: number | undefined },
) {
  assertRole(actor, MANAGEMENT);
  const pageSize = clampInt(query.pageSize, 1, 100, 25);
  const page = clampInt(query.page, 1, 10_000, 1);
  const where = query.payerCustomerId ? { payerCustomerId: query.payerCustomerId } : {};
  const rows = await store.payment.findMany({
    where,
    orderBy: [{ receivedOn: "desc" }, { createdAt: "desc" }, { id: "desc" }],
    skip: (page - 1) * pageSize,
    take: pageSize,
    select: {
      id: true,
      receivedOn: true,
      amountCents: true,
      reference: true,
      voidedAt: true,
      payer: { select: { id: true, number: true, displayName: true } },
    },
  });
  const total = await store.payment.count({ where });
  const allocated = await allocatedByPayment(store, rows.map((r) => r.id));
  return {
    items: rows.map((r) => ({
      id: r.id,
      receivedOn: toDateString(r.receivedOn),
      amountCents: r.amountCents,
      reference: r.reference,
      voided: r.voidedAt !== null,
      unallocatedCents: r.voidedAt ? 0 : r.amountCents - (allocated.get(r.id) ?? 0),
      payer: r.payer,
    })),
    total,
    page,
    pageSize,
  };
}

export type OpenInvoice = {
  id: string;
  reference: string;
  forName: string | null;
  dueDate: string;
  balanceCents: number;
};

/** Issued invoices billed to the payer that still owe money, oldest due first (the allocation form). */
export async function openInvoicesForPayer(
  store: InvoiceStore,
  actor: Actor,
  payerCustomerId: string,
): Promise<OpenInvoice[]> {
  assertRole(actor, MANAGEMENT);
  const rows = await store.invoice.findMany({
    where: { billToCustomerId: payerCustomerId, status: "ISSUED" },
    orderBy: [{ dueDate: "asc" }, { numberYear: "asc" }, { numberSeq: "asc" }],
    select: {
      id: true,
      numberYear: true,
      numberSeq: true,
      dueDate: true,
      totalCents: true,
      customer: { select: { id: true, displayName: true } },
    },
  });
  const allocated = await allocatedByInvoice(store, rows.map((r) => r.id));
  return rows
    .map((r) => ({
      id: r.id,
      reference: formatInvoiceReference(r) ?? "",
      forName: r.customer.id !== payerCustomerId ? r.customer.displayName : null,
      dueDate: r.dueDate ? toDateString(r.dueDate) : "",
      balanceCents: (r.totalCents ?? 0) - (allocated.get(r.id) ?? 0),
    }))
    .filter((r) => r.balanceCents > 0);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `packages/core/src/invoices/reports.ts`**

```ts
import { assertRole, DomainError, MANAGEMENT, type Actor } from "../customers/access";
import { toDateString } from "../quotes/reference";
import { formatInvoiceReference } from "./reference";
import { INVOICE_MESSAGES, lockReportRow, logInvoiceEvent, type InvoiceStore } from "./store";

/** "I have paid" reports (written by the portal in stage 5b). Waiting ones by default, newest first. */
export async function listPaymentReports(
  store: InvoiceStore,
  actor: Actor,
  query: { status?: "WAITING" | "ALL" } = {},
) {
  assertRole(actor, MANAGEMENT);
  const rows = await store.paymentReport.findMany({
    where: query.status === "ALL" ? {} : { status: "WAITING" },
    orderBy: [{ createdAt: "desc" }, { id: "desc" }],
    take: 200,
    select: {
      id: true,
      paidOn: true,
      reference: true,
      status: true,
      createdAt: true,
      reportedByContact: { select: { name: true } },
      invoice: {
        select: {
          id: true,
          numberYear: true,
          numberSeq: true,
          totalCents: true,
          customer: { select: { id: true, displayName: true } },
          billTo: { select: { id: true, number: true, displayName: true } },
        },
      },
    },
  });
  return rows.map((r) => ({
    id: r.id,
    paidOn: toDateString(r.paidOn),
    reference: r.reference,
    status: r.status,
    createdAt: r.createdAt,
    reportedBy: r.reportedByContact?.name ?? null,
    invoice: {
      id: r.invoice.id,
      reference: formatInvoiceReference(r.invoice),
      totalCents: r.invoice.totalCents,
      customer: r.invoice.customer,
      billTo: r.invoice.billTo,
    },
  }));
}

/** For pre-filling the record-payment form from a report. */
export async function getPaymentReport(store: InvoiceStore, actor: Actor, id: string) {
  assertRole(actor, MANAGEMENT);
  const r = await store.paymentReport.findUnique({
    where: { id },
    select: {
      id: true,
      status: true,
      paidOn: true,
      reference: true,
      invoiceId: true,
      invoice: { select: { billToCustomerId: true } },
    },
  });
  if (!r) return null;
  return {
    id: r.id,
    status: r.status,
    paidOn: toDateString(r.paidOn),
    reference: r.reference,
    invoiceId: r.invoiceId,
    billToCustomerId: r.invoice.billToCustomerId,
  };
}

/** "Dismiss": the money was not found on the bank statement. Lock: the report. */
export async function dismissPaymentReport(store: InvoiceStore, actor: Actor, id: string): Promise<void> {
  assertRole(actor, MANAGEMENT);
  const read = async () => {
    const r = await store.paymentReport.findUnique({ where: { id }, select: { status: true, invoiceId: true } });
    if (!r) throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.reportNotFound);
    if (r.status !== "WAITING") throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.reportResolved);
    return r;
  };
  await read();
  await lockReportRow(store, id);
  const r = await read();
  const { count } = await store.paymentReport.updateMany({
    where: { id, status: "WAITING" },
    data: { status: "DISMISSED", resolvedAt: new Date(), resolvedById: actor.id },
  });
  if (count !== 1) throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.reportResolved);
  await logInvoiceEvent(store, actor, "invoice.report_dismissed", r.invoiceId, { reportId: id });
}
```
