```ts
import { ForbiddenError } from "../customers/access";
import { dbTestsEnabled, withRollback } from "../testing/rollback";
import { getStatement } from "./statements";
import { crew, makeBillingCustomer, makeIssuedInvoice, makePayment, manager } from "./test-helpers";

describe.skipIf(!dbTestsEnabled)("statements", () => {
  it("one customer: opening balance, the period's invoices and payments, closing balance and what is owed", async () => {
    await withRollback(async (tx) => {
      const c = await makeBillingCustomer(tx);
      const sept = await makeIssuedInvoice(tx, {
        customerId: c.customerId,
        totalCents: 100000,
        issuedOn: "2026-09-20",
        dueDate: "2026-09-30",
      });
      const oct = await makeIssuedInvoice(tx, {
        customerId: c.customerId,
        totalCents: 50000,
        issuedOn: "2026-10-20",
        dueDate: "2026-10-31",
      });
      const voided = await makeIssuedInvoice(tx, {
        customerId: c.customerId,
        totalCents: 99900,
        issuedOn: "2026-10-05",
        dueDate: "2026-10-31",
      });
      await tx.invoice.update({ where: { id: voided }, data: { status: "VOID", voidedAt: new Date() } });
      await makePayment(tx, {
        payerCustomerId: c.customerId,
        amountCents: 60000,
        receivedOn: "2026-09-25",
        allocations: [{ invoiceId: sept, amountCents: 60000 }],
      });
      await makePayment(tx, {
        payerCustomerId: c.customerId,
        amountCents: 70000,
        receivedOn: "2026-10-28",
        reference: "ZZ EFT",
        allocations: [
          { invoiceId: sept, amountCents: 40000 },
          { invoiceId: oct, amountCents: 30000 },
        ],
      });
      await makePayment(tx, {
        payerCustomerId: c.customerId,
        amountCents: 12345,
        receivedOn: "2026-10-10",
        voided: true,
      });

      await expect(
        getStatement(tx, crew, c.customerId, { from: "2026-10-01", to: "2026-10-31" }),
      ).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ForbiddenError);
      const statement = await getStatement(tx, manager, c.customerId, { from: "2026-10-01", to: "2026-10-31" });
      expect(statement?.customer).toMatchObject({ id: c.customerId, address: "1 Test Road, Testville" });
      const data = statement?.data;
      expect(data).toMatchObject({
        openingCents: 40000,
        invoicedCents: 50000,
        paidCents: 70000,
        closingCents: 20000,
        creditCents: 0,
        byComplex: [],
      });
      expect(data?.entries.map((e) => [e.date, e.kind, e.label, e.balanceCents])).toEqual([
        ["2026-10-20", "INVOICE", expect.stringMatching(/^Invoice INV-2099-\d{4,}$/), 90000],
        ["2026-10-28", "PAYMENT", "Payment received, ref ZZ EFT", 20000],
      ]);
      expect(data?.outstanding.map((o) => [o.id, o.balanceCents, o.overdue])).toEqual([[oct, 20000, true]]);
    });
  });

  it("an agency: invoices of each complex, one payment over both, and a breakdown per complex", async () => {
    await withRollback(async (tx) => {
      const agency = await makeBillingCustomer(tx);
      const a = await makeBillingCustomer(tx, { managedById: agency.customerId, name: "ZZ E2E Complex A" });
      const b = await makeBillingCustomer(tx, { managedById: agency.customerId, name: "ZZ E2E Complex B" });
      const invA = await makeIssuedInvoice(tx, {
        customerId: a.customerId,
        billToCustomerId: agency.customerId,
        totalCents: 30000,
        issuedOn: "2026-11-20",
        dueDate: "2099-11-30",
      });
      await makeIssuedInvoice(tx, {
        customerId: b.customerId,
        billToCustomerId: agency.customerId,
        totalCents: 50000,
        issuedOn: "2026-11-20",
        dueDate: "2099-11-30",
      });
      await makePayment(tx, {
        payerCustomerId: agency.customerId,
        amountCents: 40000,
        receivedOn: "2026-11-21",
        allocations: [{ invoiceId: invA, amountCents: 30000 }],
      });
      const statement = await getStatement(tx, manager, agency.customerId, { from: "2026-11-01", to: "2026-11-30" });
      expect(statement?.data.byComplex).toEqual([
        { customerId: a.customerId, customerName: "ZZ E2E Complex A", invoicedCents: 30000, outstandingCents: 0 },
        { customerId: b.customerId, customerName: "ZZ E2E Complex B", invoicedCents: 50000, outstandingCents: 50000 },
      ]);
      expect(statement?.data.creditCents).toBe(10000);
      expect(statement?.data.closingCents).toBe(40000);
      // the complex's own statement is empty: its bills go to the agency
      const own = await getStatement(tx, manager, a.customerId, { from: "2026-11-01", to: "2026-11-30" });
      expect(own?.data.entries).toEqual([]);
    });
  });

  it("refuses a bad period and returns null for an unknown customer", async () => {
    await withRollback(async (tx) => {
      const c = await makeBillingCustomer(tx);
      await expect(
        getStatement(tx, manager, c.customerId, { from: "2026-12-01", to: "2026-11-01" }),
      ).rejects.toThrow("The start date must be on or before the end date.");
      expect(
        await getStatement(tx, manager, "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111", {
          from: "2026-11-01",
          to: "2026-11-30",
        }),
      ).toBeNull();
    });
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write `packages/core/src/invoices/statements.ts`**

```ts
import { assertRole, DomainError, MANAGEMENT, type Actor } from "../customers/access";
import { toDateString, todaySA } from "../quotes/reference";
import { formatBillingAddress } from "./money";
import { formatInvoiceReference } from "./reference";
import { buildStatementData, statementPeriodProblem, type StatementData } from "./statement-math";
import { allocatedByInvoice, allocatedByPayment, type InvoiceStore } from "./store";

/**
 * The statement of one paying (bill-to) customer for a period: its issued invoices, for any of its complexes,
 * and its payments that are not voided. Worked out on demand; nothing is stored.
 */
export async function getStatement(
  store: InvoiceStore,
  actor: Actor,
  customerId: string,
  period: { from: string; to: string },
): Promise<{
  customer: { id: string; number: number; displayName: string; address: string | null };
  data: StatementData;
} | null> {
  assertRole(actor, MANAGEMENT);
  const problem = statementPeriodProblem(period.from, period.to);
  if (problem) throw new DomainError(problem);
  const customer = await store.customer.findUnique({
    where: { id: customerId },
    select: {
      id: true,
      number: true,
      displayName: true,
      billingLine1: true,
      billingLine2: true,
      billingSuburb: true,
      billingCity: true,
      billingPostalCode: true,
    },
  });
  if (!customer) return null;

  const invoices = await store.invoice.findMany({
    where: { billToCustomerId: customerId, status: "ISSUED" },
    select: {
      id: true,
      numberYear: true,
      numberSeq: true,
      issuedAt: true,
      dueDate: true,
      totalCents: true,
      customer: { select: { id: true, displayName: true } },
    },
  });
  const payments = await store.payment.findMany({
    where: { payerCustomerId: customerId, voidedAt: null },
    select: { id: true, receivedOn: true, amountCents: true, reference: true },
  });
  const byInvoice = await allocatedByInvoice(store, invoices.map((i) => i.id));
  const byPayment = await allocatedByPayment(store, payments.map((p) => p.id));

  const data = buildStatementData({
    from: period.from,
    to: period.to,
    today: todaySA(),
    ownCustomerId: customerId,
    invoices: invoices.map((i) => ({
      id: i.id,
      reference: formatInvoiceReference(i) ?? "",
      issuedOn: todaySA(i.issuedAt ?? new Date(0)),
      dueOn: i.dueDate ? toDateString(i.dueDate) : "",
      totalCents: i.totalCents ?? 0,
      allocatedCents: byInvoice.get(i.id) ?? 0,
      customerId: i.customer.id,
      customerName: i.customer.displayName,
    })),
    payments: payments.map((p) => ({
      id: p.id,
      receivedOn: toDateString(p.receivedOn),
      amountCents: p.amountCents,
      allocatedCents: byPayment.get(p.id) ?? 0,
      reference: p.reference,
    })),
  });
  return {
    customer: {
      id: customer.id,
      number: customer.number,
      displayName: customer.displayName,
      address: formatBillingAddress(customer),
    },
    data,
  };
}
```

**Files:**

- Create: `packages/ui/src/invoice-document.tsx`
- Create: `packages/ui/src/invoice-pdf.tsx`
- Modify: `packages/ui/package.json` (two exports)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 4 (`formatMonthLabel`, `INVOICE_LINE_KIND_LABELS`, `INVOICE_STATUS_LABELS`, `NOT_VAT_REGISTERED`, `InvoiceDisplayStatus`, `InvoiceLineKindValue`, `invoiceDisplayStatus`, `sumLineCents`, `formatBillingAddress`, `formatInvoiceReference`, `StatementData`); Task 5 (`invoiceSnapshotSchema`, `InvoiceSnapshot`, `bankDetailsReady`, `readInvoiceSettings`, `buildInvoiceSnapshot`, `billToIdFor`, `InvoiceStore`); Task 10 (`getStatement`); `formatZAR` (`money.ts`); `formatCustomerNumber`; `addDays`, `formatDateSA`, `fromDateString`, `toDateString`, `todaySA` (`quotes/reference.ts`).
- Produces (`document.ts`):
  - `type InvoiceDocumentInput = { reference: string | null; status: InvoiceDisplayStatus; kind: "MONTHLY" | "ONCE_OFF"; issuedOn: string | null; dueOn: string | null; serviceMonth: string | null; billTo: { name: string; address: string | null }; forCustomerName: string | null; lines: { id: string; kind: InvoiceLineKindValue; description: string; amountCents: number }[]; paidCents: number; snapshot: InvoiceSnapshot }`
  - `type InvoiceDocument = { reference; status; statusLabel; banner: string | null; title: string; issuedDate: string | null; dueDate: string | null; serviceMonthLabel: string | null; billTo: { name; address: string | null }; forCustomerName: string | null; business: InvoiceSnapshot["business"]; rows: { id; description; kindLabel; amountLabel }[]; totalLabel; paidLabel: string | null; balanceLabel: string | null; bank: InvoiceSnapshot["bank"]; bankReady: boolean; paymentReference: string; vatNote: string }`
  - `buildInvoiceDocument(input: InvoiceDocumentInput): InvoiceDocument` (pure)
  - `loadInvoiceDocument(store, actor, id): Promise<{ doc: InvoiceDocument } | null>` (owner, manager): an issued or void invoice uses its frozen recipient and snapshot; a draft is built from the live bill-to customer and settings, its empty due date shown as today plus `onceOffDueDays`.
  - `type StatementDocument = { periodLabel; generatedDate; customer: { name; number; address: string | null }; business; bank; bankReady: boolean; paymentReference; summary: { opening; invoiced; paid; closing; closingNote; credit: string | null }; rows: { key; date; description; forName: string | null; debit; credit; balance }[]; outstanding: { id; reference; forName: string | null; dueDate; balance; overdue: boolean }[]; byComplex: { name; invoiced; outstanding }[]; vatNote }`
  - `buildStatementDocument(input: { data: StatementData; customer: { name: string; number: number; address: string | null }; snapshot: InvoiceSnapshot; generatedOn: string }): StatementDocument` (pure)
  - `loadStatementDocument(store, actor, customerId, period): Promise<{ customer: { id; number; displayName; address }; data: StatementData; doc: StatementDocument } | null>`
- Produces (`@ace/ui`): `@ace/ui/invoice-document` exports `InvoiceDocumentView({ doc })` and `StatementDocumentView({ doc })` (server-safe: no hooks, no handlers); `@ace/ui/invoice-pdf` exports `InvoicePdf({ doc })` and `StatementPdf({ doc })` (hook-free, return `<Document>`, built-in Helvetica fonts).

```ts
import { assertRole, MANAGEMENT, type Actor } from "../customers/access";
import { formatCustomerNumber } from "../customers/number";
import { formatZAR } from "../money";
import { addDays, formatDateSA, fromDateString, toDateString, todaySA } from "../quotes/reference";
import { formatMonthLabel } from "./dates";
import {
  INVOICE_LINE_KIND_LABELS,
  INVOICE_STATUS_LABELS,
  NOT_VAT_REGISTERED,
  type InvoiceDisplayStatus,
  type InvoiceLineKindValue,
} from "./labels";
import { formatBillingAddress, invoiceDisplayStatus, sumLineCents } from "./money";
import { formatInvoiceReference } from "./reference";
import { bankDetailsReady, invoiceSnapshotSchema, type InvoiceSnapshot } from "./schemas";
import { buildInvoiceSnapshot, readInvoiceSettings } from "./settings";
import type { StatementData } from "./statement-math";
import { getStatement } from "./statements";
import { billToIdFor, type InvoiceStore } from "./store";

/** "2026-11-20" -> "20 Nov 2026" in South African style. */
const day = (date: string): string => formatDateSA(fromDateString(date));

export type InvoiceDocumentInput = {
  reference: string | null;
  status: InvoiceDisplayStatus;
  kind: "MONTHLY" | "ONCE_OFF";
  issuedOn: string | null;
  dueOn: string | null;
  serviceMonth: string | null;
  billTo: { name: string; address: string | null };
  forCustomerName: string | null;
  lines: { id: string; kind: InvoiceLineKindValue; description: string; amountCents: number }[];
  paidCents: number;
  snapshot: InvoiceSnapshot;
};

export type InvoiceDocument = {
  reference: string;
  status: InvoiceDisplayStatus;
  statusLabel: string;
  banner: string | null;
  title: string;
  issuedDate: string | null;
  dueDate: string | null;
  serviceMonthLabel: string | null;
  billTo: { name: string; address: string | null };
  forCustomerName: string | null;
  business: InvoiceSnapshot["business"];
  rows: { id: string; description: string; kindLabel: string; amountLabel: string }[];
  totalLabel: string;
  paidLabel: string | null;
  balanceLabel: string | null;
  bank: InvoiceSnapshot["bank"];
  bankReady: boolean;
  paymentReference: string;
  vatNote: string;
};

const BANNERS: Partial<Record<InvoiceDisplayStatus, string>> = {
  DRAFT: "Draft: not yet issued.",
  VOID: "This invoice has been voided. Nothing is owed on it.",
  OVERDUE: "This invoice is overdue.",
  PAID: "Paid in full. Thank you.",
};

/** Everything the screen and the PDF show, as plain data. No layout decisions live here. */
export function buildInvoiceDocument(input: InvoiceDocumentInput): InvoiceDocument {
  const total = sumLineCents(input.lines);
  const owing = input.status !== "DRAFT" && input.status !== "VOID";
  return {
    reference: input.reference ?? "Draft",
    status: input.status,
    statusLabel: INVOICE_STATUS_LABELS[input.status],
    banner: BANNERS[input.status] ?? null,
    title: input.kind === "MONTHLY" ? "Monthly invoice" : "Invoice",
    issuedDate: input.issuedOn ? day(input.issuedOn) : null,
    dueDate: input.dueOn ? day(input.dueOn) : null,
    serviceMonthLabel: input.serviceMonth ? formatMonthLabel(input.serviceMonth) : null,
    billTo: input.billTo,
    forCustomerName: input.forCustomerName,
    business: input.snapshot.business,
    rows: input.lines.map((l) => ({
      id: l.id,
      description: l.description,
      kindLabel: INVOICE_LINE_KIND_LABELS[l.kind],
      amountLabel: formatZAR(l.amountCents),
    })),
    totalLabel: formatZAR(total),
    paidLabel: owing && input.paidCents > 0 ? formatZAR(input.paidCents) : null,
    balanceLabel: owing && input.paidCents > 0 ? formatZAR(Math.max(0, total - input.paidCents)) : null,
    bank: input.snapshot.bank,
    bankReady: bankDetailsReady(input.snapshot.bank),
    paymentReference: input.snapshot.paymentReference,
    vatNote: NOT_VAT_REGISTERED,
  };
}

/**
 * The document of a stored invoice. An issued or void invoice uses its frozen recipient and snapshot; a draft
 * is built from the live bill-to customer and settings. Null when there is no such invoice.
 */
export async function loadInvoiceDocument(
  store: InvoiceStore,
  actor: Actor,
  id: string,
): Promise<{ doc: InvoiceDocument } | null> {
  assertRole(actor, MANAGEMENT);
  const inv = await store.invoice.findUnique({
    where: { id },
    select: {
      id: true,
      status: true,
      kind: true,
      numberYear: true,
      numberSeq: true,
      issuedAt: true,
      dueDate: true,
      serviceMonth: true,
      totalCents: true,
      recipientName: true,
      recipientAddress: true,
      bankSnapshot: true,
      customer: {
        select: { id: true, displayName: true, managedById: true, billsToManager: true },
      },
      billToCustomerId: true,
      lines: {
        orderBy: { sortOrder: "asc" },
        select: { id: true, kind: true, description: true, amountCents: true },
      },
      allocations: { where: { payment: { voidedAt: null } }, select: { amountCents: true } },
    },
  });
  if (!inv) return null;
  const today = todaySA();
  const paidCents = inv.allocations.reduce((t, a) => t + a.amountCents, 0);
  const frozen = inv.status !== "DRAFT";
  const billToId = frozen ? inv.billToCustomerId : billToIdFor(inv.customer);

  let snapshot: InvoiceSnapshot;
  let billTo: { name: string; address: string | null };
  let dueOn = inv.dueDate ? toDateString(inv.dueDate) : null;
  if (frozen) {
    snapshot = invoiceSnapshotSchema.parse(inv.bankSnapshot);
    billTo = { name: inv.recipientName ?? "Anonymised customer", address: inv.recipientAddress };
  } else {
    const settings = await readInvoiceSettings(store);
    const live = await store.customer.findUniqueOrThrow({
      where: { id: billToId },
      select: {
        number: true,
        displayName: true,
        billingLine1: true,
        billingLine2: true,
        billingSuburb: true,
        billingCity: true,
        billingPostalCode: true,
      },
    });
    snapshot = await buildInvoiceSnapshot(store, settings, live.number);
    billTo = { name: live.displayName, address: formatBillingAddress(live) };
    dueOn ??= addDays(today, settings.onceOffDueDays);
  }
  const totalCents = inv.totalCents ?? sumLineCents(inv.lines);
  const doc = buildInvoiceDocument({
    reference: formatInvoiceReference(inv),
    status: invoiceDisplayStatus(
      { status: inv.status, totalCents, allocatedCents: paidCents, dueDate: inv.dueDate ? toDateString(inv.dueDate) : null },
      today,
    ),
    kind: inv.kind,
    issuedOn: inv.issuedAt ? todaySA(inv.issuedAt) : null,
    dueOn,
    serviceMonth: inv.serviceMonth ? toDateString(inv.serviceMonth).slice(0, 7) : null,
    billTo,
    forCustomerName: billToId !== inv.customer.id ? inv.customer.displayName : null,
    lines: inv.lines,
    paidCents,
    snapshot,
  });
  return { doc };
}

export type StatementDocument = {
  periodLabel: string;
  generatedDate: string;
  customer: { name: string; number: string; address: string | null };
  business: InvoiceSnapshot["business"];
  bank: InvoiceSnapshot["bank"];
  bankReady: boolean;
  paymentReference: string;
  summary: {
    opening: string;
    invoiced: string;
    paid: string;
    closing: string;
    closingNote: string;
    credit: string | null;
  };
  rows: {
    key: string;
    date: string;
    description: string;
    forName: string | null;
    debit: string;
    credit: string;
    balance: string;
  }[];
  outstanding: {
    id: string;
    reference: string;
    forName: string | null;
    dueDate: string;
    balance: string;
    overdue: boolean;
  }[];
  byComplex: { name: string; invoiced: string; outstanding: string }[];
  vatNote: string;
};

export function buildStatementDocument(input: {
  data: StatementData;
  customer: { name: string; number: number; address: string | null };
  snapshot: InvoiceSnapshot;
  generatedOn: string;
}): StatementDocument {
  const d = input.data;
  return {
    periodLabel: `${day(d.from)} to ${day(d.to)}`,
    generatedDate: day(input.generatedOn),
    customer: {
      name: input.customer.name,
      number: formatCustomerNumber(input.customer.number),
      address: input.customer.address,
    },
    business: input.snapshot.business,
    bank: input.snapshot.bank,
    bankReady: bankDetailsReady(input.snapshot.bank),
    paymentReference: input.snapshot.paymentReference,
    summary: {
      opening: formatZAR(d.openingCents),
      invoiced: formatZAR(d.invoicedCents),
      paid: formatZAR(d.paidCents),
      closing: formatZAR(Math.abs(d.closingCents)),
      closingNote: d.closingCents > 0 ? "Amount due" : d.closingCents < 0 ? "In credit" : "Nothing owed",
      credit: d.creditCents > 0 ? formatZAR(d.creditCents) : null,
    },
    rows: d.entries.map((e) => ({
      key: `${e.kind}-${e.id}`,
      date: day(e.date),
      description: e.label,
      forName: e.customerName,
      debit: e.debitCents > 0 ? formatZAR(e.debitCents) : "",
      credit: e.creditCents > 0 ? formatZAR(e.creditCents) : "",
      balance: formatZAR(e.balanceCents),
    })),
    outstanding: d.outstanding.map((o) => ({
      id: o.id,
      reference: o.reference,
      forName: o.customerName,
      dueDate: o.dueOn ? day(o.dueOn) : "",
      balance: formatZAR(o.balanceCents),
      overdue: o.overdue,
    })),
    byComplex: d.byComplex.map((c) => ({
      name: c.customerName,
      invoiced: formatZAR(c.invoicedCents),
      outstanding: formatZAR(c.outstandingCents),
    })),
    vatNote: NOT_VAT_REGISTERED,
  };
}

/** The statement with its document, using ACE's current details (statements are made on demand, not frozen). */
export async function loadStatementDocument(
  store: InvoiceStore,
  actor: Actor,
  customerId: string,
  period: { from: string; to: string },
) {
  const statement = await getStatement(store, actor, customerId, period);
  if (!statement) return null;
  const settings = await readInvoiceSettings(store);
  const snapshot = await buildInvoiceSnapshot(store, settings, statement.customer.number);
  return {
    ...statement,
    doc: buildStatementDocument({
      data: statement.data,
      customer: {
        name: statement.customer.displayName,
        number: statement.customer.number,
        address: statement.customer.address,
      },
      snapshot,
      generatedOn: todaySA(),
    }),
  };
}
```

```tsx
import type { InvoiceDocument, StatementDocument } from "@ace/core";

function BankBlock({
  bank,
  bankReady,
  paymentReference,
}: Pick<InvoiceDocument, "bank" | "bankReady" | "paymentReference">) {
  return (
    <section className="mt-6 rounded-xl bg-forest/5 p-4 text-sm ring-1 ring-forest/15">
      <h3 className="font-display text-base font-semibold text-forest">How to pay (EFT)</h3>
      {bankReady ? (
        <dl className="mt-2 grid gap-x-4 gap-y-1 sm:grid-cols-2">
          <div>
            <dt className="text-charcoal/60">Bank</dt>
            <dd>{bank.bankName}</dd>
          </div>
          <div>
            <dt className="text-charcoal/60">Account holder</dt>
            <dd>{bank.accountHolder}</dd>
          </div>
          <div>
            <dt className="text-charcoal/60">Account number</dt>
            <dd>{bank.accountNumber}</dd>
          </div>
          <div>
            <dt className="text-charcoal/60">Branch code</dt>
            <dd>{bank.branchCode}</dd>
          </div>
          {bank.accountType ? (
            <div>
              <dt className="text-charcoal/60">Account type</dt>
              <dd>{bank.accountType}</dd>
            </div>
          ) : null}
        </dl>
      ) : (
        <p className="mt-2 text-charcoal/70">Bank details have not been entered yet.</p>
      )}
      <p className="mt-3">
        Use this payment reference: <span className="font-semibold text-forest">{paymentReference}</span>
      </p>
    </section>
  );
}

function Business({ business }: { business: InvoiceDocument["business"] }) {
  return (
    <dd className="break-words">
      <span className="font-semibold">{business.legalName}</span>
      <span className="block">t/a {business.tradingName}</span>
      {business.registrationNumber ? <span className="block">Reg {business.registrationNumber}</span> : null}
      {business.phone ? <span className="block">{business.phone}</span> : null}
      {business.email ? <span className="block">{business.email}</span> : null}
    </dd>
  );
}

export function InvoiceDocumentView({ doc }: { doc: InvoiceDocument }) {
  return (
    <article className="rounded-2xl bg-white p-5 shadow-sm ring-1 ring-charcoal/10 sm:p-8">
      <header>
        <p className="font-display text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-ochre">
          {doc.business.tradingName}
        </p>
        <h2 className="mt-1 font-display text-2xl font-semibold text-forest">{doc.title}</h2>
      </header>
      {doc.banner ? (
        <p className="mt-4 rounded-lg bg-ochre/10 px-3 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-charcoal ring-1 ring-ochre/30">
          {doc.banner}
        </p>
      ) : null}
      <dl className="mt-6 grid gap-4 text-sm sm:grid-cols-2">
        <div>
          <dt className="text-charcoal/60">Invoice number</dt>
          <dd className="font-semibold text-forest">{doc.reference}</dd>
        </div>
        <div>
          <dt className="text-charcoal/60">Dates</dt>
          <dd>
            {doc.issuedDate ? <span className="block">Issued {doc.issuedDate}</span> : null}
            {doc.dueDate ? <span className="block font-semibold">Due {doc.dueDate}</span> : null}
            {doc.serviceMonthLabel ? <span className="block">Service month: {doc.serviceMonthLabel}</span> : null}
          </dd>
        </div>
        <div>
          <dt className="text-charcoal/60">Bill to</dt>
          <dd className="break-words">
            <span className="font-semibold">{doc.billTo.name}</span>
            {doc.billTo.address ? <span className="block">{doc.billTo.address}</span> : null}
            {doc.forCustomerName ? <span className="block">For: {doc.forCustomerName}</span> : null}
          </dd>
        </div>
        <div>
          <dt className="text-charcoal/60">From</dt>
          <Business business={doc.business} />
        </div>
      </dl>
      <table className="mt-6 w-full table-fixed text-sm">
        <thead>
          <tr className="border-b border-charcoal/15 text-left text-charcoal/60">
            <th className="py-2 pr-2 font-normal">Description</th>
            <th className="w-28 py-2 text-right font-normal">Amount</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {doc.rows.map((r) => (
            <tr key={r.id} className="border-b border-charcoal/10 align-top">
              <td className="break-words py-2 pr-2">
                {r.description}
                <span className="block text-xs text-charcoal/50">{r.kindLabel}</span>
              </td>
              <td className="py-2 text-right font-semibold text-forest">{r.amountLabel}</td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
        <tfoot>
          <tr>
            <td className="py-2 pr-2 text-right font-semibold">Total</td>
            <td className="py-2 text-right font-semibold text-forest">{doc.totalLabel}</td>
          </tr>
          {doc.paidLabel ? (
            <tr>
              <td className="py-1 pr-2 text-right">Paid</td>
              <td className="py-1 text-right">{doc.paidLabel}</td>
            </tr>
          ) : null}
          {doc.balanceLabel ? (
            <tr>
              <td className="py-1 pr-2 text-right font-semibold">Balance due</td>
              <td className="py-1 text-right font-semibold">{doc.balanceLabel}</td>
            </tr>
          ) : null}
        </tfoot>
      </table>
      <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-charcoal/60">{doc.vatNote}</p>
      <BankBlock bank={doc.bank} bankReady={doc.bankReady} paymentReference={doc.paymentReference} />
    </article>
  );
}

export function StatementDocumentView({ doc }: { doc: StatementDocument }) {
  return (
    <article className="rounded-2xl bg-white p-5 shadow-sm ring-1 ring-charcoal/10 sm:p-8">
      <header>
        <p className="font-display text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-ochre">
          {doc.business.tradingName}
        </p>
        <h2 className="mt-1 font-display text-2xl font-semibold text-forest">Statement</h2>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-charcoal/70">
          {doc.periodLabel}, made {doc.generatedDate}
        </p>
      </header>
      <dl className="mt-6 grid gap-4 text-sm sm:grid-cols-2">
        <div>
          <dt className="text-charcoal/60">Account</dt>
          <dd className="break-words">
            <span className="font-semibold">{doc.customer.name}</span>
            <span className="block">{doc.customer.number}</span>
            {doc.customer.address ? <span className="block">{doc.customer.address}</span> : null}
          </dd>
        </div>
        <div>
          <dt className="text-charcoal/60">From</dt>
          <Business business={doc.business} />
        </div>
      </dl>
      <dl className="mt-6 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-sm sm:grid-cols-4">
        <div className="rounded-xl bg-forest/5 p-3">
          <dt className="text-charcoal/60">Opening balance</dt>
          <dd className="font-semibold">{doc.summary.opening}</dd>
        </div>
        <div className="rounded-xl bg-forest/5 p-3">
          <dt className="text-charcoal/60">Invoiced</dt>
          <dd className="font-semibold">{doc.summary.invoiced}</dd>
        </div>
        <div className="rounded-xl bg-forest/5 p-3">
          <dt className="text-charcoal/60">Paid</dt>
          <dd className="font-semibold">{doc.summary.paid}</dd>
        </div>
        <div className="rounded-xl bg-forest/10 p-3">
          <dt className="text-charcoal/60">{doc.summary.closingNote}</dt>
          <dd className="font-semibold text-forest">{doc.summary.closing}</dd>
        </div>
      </dl>
      {doc.summary.credit ? (
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-charcoal/70">
          Includes {doc.summary.credit} paid but not yet matched to an invoice (credit on account).
        </p>
      ) : null}
      <h3 className="mt-6 font-display text-base font-semibold text-forest">In this period</h3>
      {doc.rows.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-charcoal/60">Nothing invoiced or paid in this period.</p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="mt-2 divide-y divide-charcoal/10 text-sm">
          {doc.rows.map((r) => (
            <li key={r.key} className="grid grid-cols-[1fr_auto] gap-x-3 py-2">
              <span className="min-w-0 break-words">
                {r.date}: {r.description}
                {r.forName ? <span className="block text-xs text-charcoal/60">For: {r.forName}</span> : null}
              </span>
              <span className="text-right">
                {r.debit ? <span className="block">{r.debit}</span> : null}
                {r.credit ? <span className="block text-forest">Paid {r.credit}</span> : null}
                <span className="block text-xs text-charcoal/60">Balance {r.balance}</span>
              </span>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
      {doc.byComplex.length > 0 ? (
        <>
          <h3 className="mt-6 font-display text-base font-semibold text-forest">Per complex</h3>
          <ul className="mt-2 divide-y divide-charcoal/10 text-sm">
            {doc.byComplex.map((c) => (
              <li key={c.name} className="flex flex-wrap justify-between gap-2 py-2">
                <span className="min-w-0 break-words font-semibold">{c.name}</span>
                <span>
                  Invoiced {c.invoiced}, still owed {c.outstanding}
                </span>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </>
      ) : null}
      <h3 className="mt-6 font-display text-base font-semibold text-forest">Still owed</h3>
      {doc.outstanding.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-charcoal/60">No invoice is waiting for payment.</p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="mt-2 divide-y divide-charcoal/10 text-sm">
          {doc.outstanding.map((o) => (
            <li key={o.id} className="flex flex-wrap justify-between gap-2 py-2">
              <span className="min-w-0 break-words">
                {o.reference}
                {o.forName ? `, ${o.forName}` : ""}, due {o.dueDate}
                {o.overdue ? <span className="ml-2 font-semibold text-red-800">Overdue</span> : null}
              </span>
              <span className="font-semibold">{o.balance}</span>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
      <p className="mt-4 text-xs text-charcoal/60">{doc.vatNote}</p>
      <BankBlock bank={doc.bank} bankReady={doc.bankReady} paymentReference={doc.paymentReference} />
    </article>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import type { InvoiceDocument, StatementDocument } from "@ace/core";
import { Document, Page, StyleSheet, Text, View } from "@react-pdf/renderer";

const GREEN = "#1f4d36";
const OCHRE = "#b5832a";
const INK = "#2b2b2b";
const MUTED = "#6b6b6b";

const s = StyleSheet.create({
  page: { paddingTop: 40, paddingBottom: 56, paddingHorizontal: 40, fontSize: 10, fontFamily: "Helvetica", color: INK },
  brand: { fontSize: 9, fontFamily: "Helvetica-Bold", color: OCHRE, letterSpacing: 1 },
  title: { fontSize: 22, fontFamily: "Helvetica-Bold", color: GREEN, marginTop: 4 },
  banner: { marginTop: 10, padding: 6, backgroundColor: "#fbf3e2", fontFamily: "Helvetica-Bold" },
  threeCol: { flexDirection: "row", marginTop: 14, gap: 16 },
  col: { flex: 1 },
  label: { color: MUTED, fontSize: 8, marginBottom: 1 },
  strong: { fontFamily: "Helvetica-Bold" },
  h2: { fontSize: 13, fontFamily: "Helvetica-Bold", color: GREEN, marginTop: 18, marginBottom: 6 },
  row: { flexDirection: "row", justifyContent: "space-between", paddingVertical: 5, borderBottomWidth: 0.5, borderBottomColor: "#d6d6d6" },
  rowText: { flex: 1, paddingRight: 8 },
  rowMeta: { color: MUTED, fontSize: 8 },
  amount: { fontFamily: "Helvetica-Bold", color: GREEN, width: 90, textAlign: "right" },
  plain: { width: 90, textAlign: "right" },
  totalRow: { flexDirection: "row", justifyContent: "flex-end", marginTop: 3, gap: 12 },
  small: { fontSize: 8, color: MUTED, marginTop: 6 },
  bank: { marginTop: 16, padding: 10, backgroundColor: "#eef4f0" },
  cards: { flexDirection: "row", marginTop: 14, gap: 8 },
  card: { flex: 1, padding: 8, backgroundColor: "#eef4f0" },
  cardValue: { fontSize: 12, fontFamily: "Helvetica-Bold", color: GREEN, marginTop: 2 },
  footer: { position: "absolute", bottom: 24, left: 40, right: 40, flexDirection: "row", justifyContent: "space-between", fontSize: 8, color: MUTED },
});

type Business = InvoiceDocument["business"];
type Bank = Pick<InvoiceDocument, "bank" | "bankReady" | "paymentReference">;

function From({ business }: { business: Business }) {
  return (
    <View style={s.col}>
      <Text style={s.label}>FROM</Text>
      <Text style={s.strong}>{business.legalName}</Text>
      <Text>t/a {business.tradingName}</Text>
      {business.registrationNumber ? <Text>Reg {business.registrationNumber}</Text> : null}
      {business.phone ? <Text>{business.phone}</Text> : null}
      {business.email ? <Text>{business.email}</Text> : null}
    </View>
  );
}

function BankBox({ bank, bankReady, paymentReference }: Bank) {
  return (
    <View style={s.bank} wrap={false}>
      <Text style={s.strong}>How to pay (EFT)</Text>
      {bankReady ? (
        <>
          <Text>Bank: {bank.bankName}</Text>
          <Text>Account holder: {bank.accountHolder}</Text>
          <Text>Account number: {bank.accountNumber}</Text>
          <Text>Branch code: {bank.branchCode}</Text>
          {bank.accountType ? <Text>Account type: {bank.accountType}</Text> : null}
        </>
      ) : (
        <Text>Bank details have not been entered yet.</Text>
      )}
      <Text style={{ marginTop: 4 }}>
        Payment reference: <Text style={s.strong}>{paymentReference}</Text>
      </Text>
    </View>
  );
}

function Footer({ left, right }: { left: string; right: string }) {
  return (
    <View style={s.footer} fixed>
      <Text>{left}</Text>
      <Text render={({ pageNumber, totalPages }) => `${right}  ·  ${pageNumber} / ${totalPages}`} />
    </View>
  );
}

export function InvoicePdf({ doc }: { doc: InvoiceDocument }) {
  return (
    <Document title={`${doc.reference} ${doc.business.tradingName}`} author={doc.business.tradingName}>
      <Page size="A4" style={s.page}>
        <Text style={s.brand}>{doc.business.tradingName.toUpperCase()}</Text>
        <Text style={s.title}>{doc.title}</Text>
        {doc.banner ? <Text style={s.banner}>{doc.banner}</Text> : null}
        <View style={s.threeCol}>
          <View style={s.col}>
            <Text style={s.label}>INVOICE NUMBER</Text>
            <Text style={s.strong}>{doc.reference}</Text>
            {doc.issuedDate ? <Text>Issued {doc.issuedDate}</Text> : null}
            {doc.dueDate ? <Text style={s.strong}>Due {doc.dueDate}</Text> : null}
            {doc.serviceMonthLabel ? <Text>Service month: {doc.serviceMonthLabel}</Text> : null}
          </View>
          <View style={s.col}>
            <Text style={s.label}>BILL TO</Text>
            <Text style={s.strong}>{doc.billTo.name}</Text>
            {doc.billTo.address ? <Text>{doc.billTo.address}</Text> : null}
            {doc.forCustomerName ? <Text>For: {doc.forCustomerName}</Text> : null}
          </View>
          <From business={doc.business} />
        </View>
        <Text style={s.h2}>Details</Text>
        {doc.rows.map((r) => (
          <View key={r.id} style={s.row} wrap={false}>
            <View style={s.rowText}>
              <Text>{r.description}</Text>
              <Text style={s.rowMeta}>{r.kindLabel}</Text>
            </View>
            <Text style={s.amount}>{r.amountLabel}</Text>
          </View>
        ))}
        <View style={s.totalRow}>
          <Text style={s.strong}>Total</Text>
          <Text style={s.amount}>{doc.totalLabel}</Text>
        </View>
        {doc.paidLabel ? (
          <View style={s.totalRow}>
            <Text>Paid</Text>
            <Text style={s.plain}>{doc.paidLabel}</Text>
          </View>
        ) : null}
        {doc.balanceLabel ? (
          <View style={s.totalRow}>
            <Text style={s.strong}>Balance due</Text>
            <Text style={s.amount}>{doc.balanceLabel}</Text>
          </View>
        ) : null}
        <Text style={s.small}>{doc.vatNote}</Text>
        <BankBox bank={doc.bank} bankReady={doc.bankReady} paymentReference={doc.paymentReference} />
        <Footer
          left={`${doc.business.legalName} t/a ${doc.business.tradingName}${doc.business.website ? `, ${doc.business.website}` : ""}`}
          right={doc.reference}
        />
      </Page>
    </Document>
  );
}

export function StatementPdf({ doc }: { doc: StatementDocument }) {
  return (
    <Document title={`Statement ${doc.customer.number}`} author={doc.business.tradingName}>
      <Page size="A4" style={s.page}>
        <Text style={s.brand}>{doc.business.tradingName.toUpperCase()}</Text>
        <Text style={s.title}>Statement</Text>
        <Text>
          {doc.periodLabel}, made {doc.generatedDate}
        </Text>
        <View style={s.threeCol}>
          <View style={s.col}>
            <Text style={s.label}>ACCOUNT</Text>
            <Text style={s.strong}>{doc.customer.name}</Text>
            <Text>{doc.customer.number}</Text>
            {doc.customer.address ? <Text>{doc.customer.address}</Text> : null}
          </View>
          <From business={doc.business} />
        </View>
        <View style={s.cards}>
          <View style={s.card}>
            <Text style={s.label}>OPENING BALANCE</Text>
            <Text style={s.cardValue}>{doc.summary.opening}</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={s.card}>
            <Text style={s.label}>INVOICED</Text>
            <Text style={s.cardValue}>{doc.summary.invoiced}</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={s.card}>
            <Text style={s.label}>PAID</Text>
            <Text style={s.cardValue}>{doc.summary.paid}</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={s.card}>
            <Text style={s.label}>{doc.summary.closingNote.toUpperCase()}</Text>
            <Text style={s.cardValue}>{doc.summary.closing}</Text>
          </View>
        </View>
        {doc.summary.credit ? (
          <Text style={s.small}>
            Includes {doc.summary.credit} paid but not yet matched to an invoice (credit on account).
          </Text>
        ) : null}
        <Text style={s.h2}>In this period</Text>
        {doc.rows.length === 0 ? <Text>Nothing invoiced or paid in this period.</Text> : null}
        {doc.rows.map((r) => (
          <View key={r.key} style={s.row} wrap={false}>
            <View style={s.rowText}>
              <Text>
                {r.date}: {r.description}
              </Text>
              {r.forName ? <Text style={s.rowMeta}>For: {r.forName}</Text> : null}
            </View>
            <Text style={s.plain}>{r.debit}</Text>
            <Text style={s.plain}>{r.credit}</Text>
            <Text style={s.amount}>{r.balance}</Text>
          </View>
        ))}
        {doc.byComplex.length > 0 ? (
          <View wrap={false}>
            <Text style={s.h2}>Per complex</Text>
            {doc.byComplex.map((c) => (
              <View key={c.name} style={s.row}>
                <Text style={s.rowText}>{c.name}</Text>
                <Text style={s.plain}>{c.invoiced}</Text>
                <Text style={s.amount}>{c.outstanding}</Text>
              </View>
            ))}
          </View>
        ) : null}
        <Text style={s.h2}>Still owed</Text>
        {doc.outstanding.length === 0 ? <Text>No invoice is waiting for payment.</Text> : null}
        {doc.outstanding.map((o) => (
          <View key={o.id} style={s.row} wrap={false}>
            <Text style={s.rowText}>
              {o.reference}
              {o.forName ? `, ${o.forName}` : ""}, due {o.dueDate}
              {o.overdue ? " (overdue)" : ""}
            </Text>
            <Text style={s.amount}>{o.balance}</Text>
          </View>
        ))}
        <Text style={s.small}>{doc.vatNote}</Text>
        <BankBox bank={doc.bank} bankReady={doc.bankReady} paymentReference={doc.paymentReference} />
        <Footer
          left={`${doc.business.legalName} t/a ${doc.business.tradingName}`}
          right={`Statement ${doc.customer.number}`}
        />
      </Page>
    </Document>
  );
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { formatCustomerNumber } from "../customers/number";
import { anonymiseCustomer, exportCustomerData } from "../customers/privacy";
import { fromDateString } from "../quotes/reference";

describe.skipIf(!dbTestsEnabled)("anonymise and export with invoices", () => {
  it("exports the customer's invoices, payments and reports without ACE's bank snapshot", async () => {
    await withRollback(async (tx) => {
      const c = await makeBillingCustomer(tx);
      const inv = await makeIssuedInvoice(tx, { customerId: c.customerId, dueDate: "2099-01-31" });
      await tx.invoice.update({ where: { id: inv }, data: { bankSnapshot: { bank: "ZZ secret-ish" } } });
      await makePayment(tx, { payerCustomerId: c.customerId, amountCents: 500, receivedOn: "2026-11-01", reference: "ZZ REF" });
      await tx.paymentReport.create({
        data: { invoiceId: inv, paidOn: fromDateString("2026-11-02"), reference: "ZZ REPORT REF" },
      });
      const data = await exportCustomerData(tx, owner, c.customerId);
      expect(data.invoices.map((i) => i.id)).toEqual([inv]);
      expect(data.invoices[0]?.lines).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(data.invoices[0]).not.toHaveProperty("bankSnapshot");
      expect(data.payments.map((p) => p.reference)).toEqual(["ZZ REF"]);
      expect(data.paymentReports.map((r) => r.reference)).toEqual(["ZZ REPORT REF"]);
    });
  });

  it("keeps numbers, dates and amounts, scrubs the recipient, wording, references and notes, and deletes drafts", async () => {
    await withRollback(async (tx) => {
      const c = await makeBillingCustomer(tx);
      const issued = await makeIssuedInvoice(tx, { customerId: c.customerId, dueDate: "2099-01-31", totalCents: 115000 });
      await tx.invoiceLine.updateMany({
        where: { invoiceId: issued },
        data: { description: `${c.propertyLabel}: Weekly garden maintenance` },
      });
      const voided = await makeIssuedInvoice(tx, { customerId: c.customerId, dueDate: "2099-01-31" });
      await tx.invoice.update({
        where: { id: voided },
        data: { status: "VOID", voidedAt: new Date(), voidReason: "ZZ typed reason" },
      });
      const draft = await tx.invoice.create({
        data: {
          customerId: c.customerId,
          billToCustomerId: c.customerId,
          kind: "ONCE_OFF",
          lines: { create: [{ sortOrder: 1, kind: "ONCE_OFF", description: "ZZ draft work", amountCents: 100 }] },
        },
        select: { id: true },
      });
      const payment = await makePayment(tx, {
        payerCustomerId: c.customerId,
        amountCents: 115000,
        receivedOn: "2026-11-01",
        reference: "ZZ BANK REF",
        allocations: [{ invoiceId: issued, amountCents: 115000 }],
      });
      await tx.payment.update({ where: { id: payment }, data: { note: "ZZ note" } });
      const report = await tx.paymentReport.create({
        data: { invoiceId: issued, paidOn: fromDateString("2026-11-01"), reference: "ZZ CLIENT REF" },
        select: { id: true },
      });

      await anonymiseCustomer(tx, owner, c.customerId, formatCustomerNumber(c.number));

      const row = await tx.invoice.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: issued }, include: { lines: true } });
      expect(row).toMatchObject({ status: "ISSUED", totalCents: 115000, numberYear: 2099, recipientName: null, recipientAddress: null });
      expect(row.lines.map((l) => [l.description, l.amountCents])).toEqual([["Once-off work", 115000]]);
      expect((await tx.invoice.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: voided } })).voidReason).toBeNull();
      expect(await tx.invoice.findUnique({ where: { id: draft.id } })).toBeNull();
      const p = await tx.payment.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: payment }, include: { allocations: true } });
      expect(p).toMatchObject({ reference: null, note: null, amountCents: 115000 });
      expect(p.allocations.map((a) => a.amountCents)).toEqual([115000]);
      expect((await tx.paymentReport.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: report.id } })).reference).toBeNull();
    });
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `packages/core/src/invoices/anonymise.ts`**

```ts
import type { Store } from "../customers/store";
import { ANONYMISED_LINE_DESCRIPTIONS, INVOICE_LINE_KINDS } from "./labels";

/**
 * The invoice part of anonymising a customer. No role check: only `anonymiseCustomer` (owner, inside its
 * transaction, after locking the customer) calls it. Financial records stay (numbers, dates, amounts,
 * allocations); the frozen recipient, typed reasons, references and notes go, and line wording becomes a
 * fixed word per kind. Drafts (as the work customer or the bill-to) are deleted: they are not records yet.
 */
export async function scrubInvoicesForAnonymise(
  store: Pick<Store, "invoice" | "invoiceLine" | "payment" | "paymentReport">,
  customerId: string,
): Promise<void> {
  const mine = { OR: [{ customerId }, { billToCustomerId: customerId }] };
  await store.invoiceLine.deleteMany({ where: { invoice: { status: "DRAFT", ...mine } } });
  await store.invoice.deleteMany({ where: { status: "DRAFT", ...mine } });
  for (const kind of INVOICE_LINE_KINDS) {
    await store.invoiceLine.updateMany({
      where: { kind, invoice: { customerId } },
      data: { description: ANONYMISED_LINE_DESCRIPTIONS[kind] },
    });
  }
  await store.invoice.updateMany({
    where: mine,
    data: { recipientName: null, recipientAddress: null, voidReason: null },
  });
  await store.payment.updateMany({
    where: { payerCustomerId: customerId },
    data: { reference: null, note: null, voidReason: null },
  });
  await store.paymentReport.updateMany({ where: { invoice: mine }, data: { reference: null } });
}

/** The invoice part of the customer's data export. No role check: only `exportCustomerData` calls it. */
export async function invoiceExportFor(
  store: Pick<Store, "invoice" | "payment" | "paymentReport">,
  customerId: string,
) {
  const mine = { OR: [{ customerId }, { billToCustomerId: customerId }] };
  const invoices = await store.invoice.findMany({
    where: mine,
    orderBy: [{ createdAt: "asc" }, { id: "asc" }],
    // ACE's own bank and business details are not data about the customer.
    omit: { bankSnapshot: true },
    include: { lines: { orderBy: { sortOrder: "asc" } }, allocations: true },
  });
  const payments = await store.payment.findMany({
    where: { payerCustomerId: customerId },
    orderBy: [{ createdAt: "asc" }, { id: "asc" }],
    include: { allocations: true },
  });
  const paymentReports = await store.paymentReport.findMany({
    where: { invoice: mine },
    orderBy: [{ createdAt: "asc" }, { id: "asc" }],
  });
  return { invoices, payments, paymentReports };
}
```

```ts
    ]);
  });

  it("refuses unsafe names, too many files and too many bytes before anything is sent", async () => {
    const fetchImpl = vi.fn();
    const send = (attachments: { filename: string; content: Uint8Array }[]) =>
      sendEmail({ ...message, attachments }, { env: prod, fetchImpl: fetchImpl as unknown as typeof fetch });
    await expect(send([{ ...pdf, filename: "../x.pdf" }])).rejects.toThrow("An email attachment name is not allowed.");
    await expect(send(Array.from({ length: 21 }, () => pdf))).rejects.toThrow("Too many email attachments.");
    await expect(
      send([{ filename: "big.pdf", content: new Uint8Array(15 * 1024 * 1024 + 1) }]),
    ).rejects.toThrow("The email attachments are too large.");
    expect(fetchImpl).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("in development prints the attachment names and sizes, never the content", async () => {
    const log = vi.fn();
    await sendEmail({ ...message, attachments: [pdf] }, { env: env({ NODE_ENV: "development" }), log });
    const text = String(log.mock.calls[0]?.[0]);
    expect(text).toContain("Attachments: INV-2026-0001.pdf (1 KB)");
    expect(text).not.toContain("JVBERg");
  });
});
```

```ts
describe("invoiceEmail", () => {
  const bank = {
    bankName: "ZZ Test Bank",
    accountHolder: "ACE Garden Services",
    accountNumber: "1234567890",
    branchCode: "250655",
    accountType: "Business cheque",
  };
  it("lists the invoices with the payment reference and bank details, under a fixed subject", () => {
    const one = invoiceEmail({
      to: "zz.client@example.com",
      invoices: [{ reference: "INV-2026-0001", forName: null, amountLabel: "R1,150.00", dueDateLabel: "30 Nov 2026" }],
      paymentReference: "C-0123",
      bank,
    });
    expect(one.subject).toBe(INVOICE_SUBJECT);
    expect(one.to).toBe("zz.client@example.com");
    expect(one.text).toContain("INV-2026-0001: R1,150.00, due 30 Nov 2026");
    expect(one.text).toContain("C-0123");
    expect(one.text).toContain("Account number: 1234567890");
    expect(one.text).toContain("not VAT registered");
    const two = invoiceEmail({
      to: "zz.agency@example.com",
      invoices: [
        { reference: "INV-2026-0001", forName: "ZZ <Complex> A", amountLabel: "R1.00", dueDateLabel: "30 Nov 2026" },
        { reference: "INV-2026-0002", forName: "ZZ Complex B", amountLabel: "R2.00", dueDateLabel: "30 Nov 2026" },
      ],
      paymentReference: "C-0099",
      bank: null,
    });
    expect(two.subject).toBe(INVOICES_SUBJECT);
    expect(two.text).toContain("INV-2026-0001 (ZZ <Complex> A): R1.00");
    expect(two.html).toContain("ZZ &lt;Complex&gt; A");
    expect(two.html).not.toContain("<Complex>");
    for (const m of [one, two]) {
      expect(m.subject).not.toContain("example.com");
      expect(m.text).not.toContain("—");
      expect(m.html).not.toContain("—");
    }
  });
});
```

`packages/core/src/invoices/emails.test.ts` (unit test with a recording stand-in for the database):

```ts
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { ForbiddenError, type Actor } from "../customers/access";
import type { EmailMessage } from "../portal/email/send";
import { checkInvoiceEmailable, emailIssuedInvoices } from "./emails";
import type { InvoiceStore } from "./store";

const manager: Actor = { id: "staff-1", role: "MANAGER" };
const SNAPSHOT = {
  bank: { bankName: "B", accountHolder: "H", accountNumber: "123456", branchCode: "250655", accountType: "" },
  business: { legalName: "L", tradingName: "ACE Garden Services", registrationNumber: "", phone: "", email: "", website: "" },
  paymentReference: "C-0009",
};
const due = new Date("2026-11-30T00:00:00.000Z");
const invoice = (id: string, seq: number, customerId: string, billToCustomerId: string) => ({
  id,
  numberYear: 2026,
  numberSeq: seq,
  totalCents: 1000 * seq,
  dueDate: due,
  customerId,
  billToCustomerId,
  bankSnapshot: SNAPSHOT,
  customer: { displayName: `ZZ ${customerId}` },
});

function fakeStore(opts: { invoices: ReturnType<typeof invoice>[]; emails: Record<string, string[]>; status?: string }) {
  const updates: { where: unknown; data: Record<string, unknown> }[] = [];
  const audits: { action: string; entityId: string; after: unknown }[] = [];
  const store = {
    invoice: {
      findMany: async () => opts.invoices,
      findUnique: async () => (opts.status ? { status: opts.status } : null),
      updateMany: async (args: { where: unknown; data: Record<string, unknown> }) => {
        updates.push(args);
        return { count: 1 };
      },
    },
    contact: {
      findMany: async (args: { where: { customerId: string } }) =>
        (opts.emails[args.where.customerId] ?? []).map((email) => ({ email })),
    },
    auditLog: {
      create: async (args: { data: { action: string; entityId: string; after: unknown } }) => {
        audits.push({ action: args.data.action, entityId: args.data.entityId, after: args.data.after });
        return { id: "a" };
      },
    },
  } as unknown as InvoiceStore;
  return { store, updates, audits };
}

const pdf = async (id: string) => ({ filename: `${id}.pdf`, content: new Uint8Array([1]) });

afterEach(() => vi.restoreAllMocks());

describe("emailIssuedInvoices", () => {
  it("sends an agency's invoices together, one email per address, with every PDF attached", async () => {
    const { store, updates, audits } = fakeStore({
      invoices: [invoice("i1", 1, "complexA", "agency"), invoice("i2", 2, "complexB", "agency")],
      emails: { agency: ["zz.one@example.com", "zz.two@example.com"] },
    });
    const sent: EmailMessage[] = [];
    const result = await emailIssuedInvoices(store, manager, ["i1", "i2"], {
      send: async (m) => {
        sent.push(m);
      },
      attachmentFor: pdf,
    });
    expect(result).toEqual({ sent: 2, failed: 0 });
    expect(sent.map((m) => m.to)).toEqual(["zz.one@example.com", "zz.two@example.com"]);
    expect(sent[0]?.subject).toBe("Your ACE invoices");
    expect(sent[0]?.attachments?.map((a) => a.filename)).toEqual(["i1.pdf", "i2.pdf"]);
    expect(sent[0]?.text).toContain("(ZZ complexA)");
    expect(updates.map((u) => u.data.emailStatus)).toEqual(["SENT", "SENT"]);
    expect(audits).toEqual([
      { action: "invoice.emailed", entityId: "i1", after: { recipients: 2 } },
      { action: "invoice.emailed", entityId: "i2", after: { recipients: 2 } },
    ]);
  });

  it("marks the invoice failed when nobody receives invoices, without sending", async () => {
    const { store, updates, audits } = fakeStore({ invoices: [invoice("i1", 1, "c", "c")], emails: {} });
    const send = vi.fn();
    const result = await emailIssuedInvoices(store, manager, ["i1"], { send, attachmentFor: pdf });
    expect(result).toEqual({ sent: 0, failed: 1 });
    expect(send).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(updates[0]?.data).toEqual({ emailStatus: "FAILED" });
    expect(audits[0]).toEqual({ action: "invoice.email_failed", entityId: "i1", after: { reason: "no_recipient" } });
  });

  it("never throws when sending fails, and logs fixed words only", async () => {
    const error = vi.spyOn(console, "error").mockImplementation(() => {});
    const { store, audits } = fakeStore({ invoices: [invoice("i1", 1, "c", "c")], emails: { c: ["zz.x@example.com"] } });
    const result = await emailIssuedInvoices(store, manager, ["i1"], {
      send: async () => {
        throw new Error("Resend refused zz.x@example.com");
      },
      attachmentFor: pdf,
    });
    expect(result).toEqual({ sent: 0, failed: 1 });
    expect(audits[0]?.after).toEqual({ reason: "send" });
    const logged = error.mock.calls.map((c) => c.map(String).join(" ")).join("\n");
    expect(logged).toContain("An invoice email could not be sent.");
    expect(logged).not.toContain("example.com");
  });

  it("marks the invoice failed when its PDF cannot be made", async () => {
    vi.spyOn(console, "error").mockImplementation(() => {});
    const { store, audits } = fakeStore({ invoices: [invoice("i1", 1, "c", "c")], emails: { c: ["zz.x@example.com"] } });
    const send = vi.fn();
    await emailIssuedInvoices(store, manager, ["i1"], {
      send,
      attachmentFor: async () => {
        throw new Error("render failed");
      },
    });
    expect(send).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(audits[0]?.after).toEqual({ reason: "attachment" });
  });

  it("refuses crew, and only an issued invoice can be emailed again", async () => {
    const { store } = fakeStore({ invoices: [], emails: {}, status: "DRAFT" });
    const crew: Actor = { id: "c", role: "CREW" };
    await expect(
      emailIssuedInvoices(store, crew, ["i1"], { send: vi.fn(), attachmentFor: pdf }),
    ).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ForbiddenError);
    await expect(checkInvoiceEmailable(store, manager, "i1")).rejects.toThrow("Only an issued invoice can be emailed.");
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Attachments in `packages/core/src/portal/email/send.ts`**

Replace the first line (`export type EmailMessage = ...`) with:

```ts
export type EmailAttachment = { filename: string; content: Uint8Array };
export type EmailMessage = {
  to: string;
  subject: string;
  text: string;
  html: string;
  attachments?: readonly EmailAttachment[];
};

export const MAX_ATTACHMENTS = 20;
export const MAX_ATTACHMENT_BYTES = 15 * 1024 * 1024;
const SAFE_FILENAME = /^[A-Za-z0-9._-]{1,100}$/;

/** Throws a fixed-words error when the attachments cannot be sent. Never names a file or a person. */
export function checkAttachments(list: readonly EmailAttachment[] = []): void {
  if (list.length > MAX_ATTACHMENTS) throw new Error("Too many email attachments.");
  if (list.some((a) => !SAFE_FILENAME.test(a.filename) || a.filename.startsWith("."))) {
    throw new Error("An email attachment name is not allowed.");
  }
  if (list.reduce((t, a) => t + a.content.byteLength, 0) > MAX_ATTACHMENT_BYTES) {
    throw new Error("The email attachments are too large.");
  }
}
```

and replace the whole `sendEmail` function with:

```ts
/** Sends one email. The error, if any, never contains the recipient's address. */
export async function sendEmail(
  message: EmailMessage,
  opts: { env?: NodeJS.ProcessEnv; fetchImpl?: typeof fetch; log?: (text: string) => void } = {},
): Promise<void> {
  const attachments = message.attachments ?? [];
  checkAttachments(attachments);
  const driver = emailDriver(opts.env);
  if (driver.kind === "console") {
    const files = attachments.map(
      (a) => `Attachments: ${a.filename} (${Math.max(1, Math.ceil(a.content.byteLength / 1024))} KB)`,
    );
    (opts.log ?? console.info)(
      [
        `[email: development only, not sent]\nTo: ${message.to}\nSubject: ${message.subject}`,
        ...files,
        "",
        message.text,
      ].join("\n"),
    );
    return;
  }
  const res = await (opts.fetchImpl ?? fetch)(RESEND_URL, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${driver.apiKey}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      from: driver.from,
      to: [message.to],
      subject: message.subject,
      text: message.text,
      html: message.html,
      ...(attachments.length > 0
        ? {
            attachments: attachments.map((a) => ({
              filename: a.filename,
              content: Buffer.from(a.content).toString("base64"),
            })),
          }
        : {}),
    }),
    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(attachments.length > 0 ? 30_000 : 10_000),
  });
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`The email could not be sent (Resend answered ${res.status}).`);
}
```

Append at the end of the file:

```ts
export type InvoiceEmailItem = {
  reference: string;
  forName: string | null;
  amountLabel: string;
  dueDateLabel: string;
};

/** One email for one or more invoices of the same payer. The PDFs are attached by the caller. */
export function invoiceEmail(input: {
  to: string;
  invoices: readonly InvoiceEmailItem[];
  paymentReference: string;
  bank: {
    bankName: string;
    accountHolder: string;
    accountNumber: string;
    branchCode: string;
    accountType: string;
  } | null;
}): EmailMessage {
  const many = input.invoices.length > 1;
  const line = (i: InvoiceEmailItem) =>
    `${i.reference}${i.forName ? ` (${i.forName})` : ""}: ${i.amountLabel}, due ${i.dueDateLabel}`;
  const bankLines = input.bank
    ? [
        `Bank: ${input.bank.bankName}`,
        `Account holder: ${input.bank.accountHolder}`,
        `Account number: ${input.bank.accountNumber}`,
        `Branch code: ${input.bank.branchCode}`,
        ...(input.bank.accountType ? [`Account type: ${input.bank.accountType}`] : []),
      ]
    : [];
  const text = [
    "Hello,",
    "",
    many
      ? `Please find your ${input.invoices.length} invoices from ACE Garden Services attached.`
      : "Please find your invoice from ACE Garden Services attached.",
    "",
    ...input.invoices.map(line),
    "",
    `Please pay by EFT and use this payment reference: ${input.paymentReference}`,
    ...bankLines,
    "",
    NOT_VAT_REGISTERED,
    "",
    "ACE Garden Services",
  ].join("\n");
  const html = wrap(
    `<p style="margin:0 0 12px">Hello,</p>` +
      `<p style="margin:0 0 12px">${
        many
          ? `Please find your ${input.invoices.length} invoices from ACE Garden Services attached.`
          : "Please find your invoice from ACE Garden Services attached."
      }</p>` +
      `<ul style="margin:0 0 16px;padding-left:20px">${input.invoices
        .map((i) => `<li>${escapeHtml(line(i))}</li>`)
        .join("")}</ul>` +
      `<p style="margin:0 0 8px">Please pay by EFT and use this payment reference: <strong>${escapeHtml(input.paymentReference)}</strong></p>` +
      (bankLines.length > 0
        ? `<p style="margin:0 0 16px">${bankLines.map((l) => escapeHtml(l)).join("<br>")}</p>`
        : "") +
      `<p style="margin:0;color:#6b6457">${escapeHtml(NOT_VAT_REGISTERED)}</p>`,
  );
  return { to: input.to, subject: many ? INVOICES_SUBJECT : INVOICE_SUBJECT, text, html };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `packages/core/src/invoices/emails.ts`**

```ts
import { assertRole, DomainError, MANAGEMENT, type Actor } from "../customers/access";
import { formatZAR } from "../money";
import type { EmailAttachment, EmailMessage } from "../portal/email/send";
import { invoiceEmail } from "../portal/email/templates";
import { formatDateSA } from "../quotes/reference";
import { formatInvoiceReference } from "./reference";
import { bankDetailsReady, invoiceSnapshotSchema } from "./schemas";
import { INVOICE_MESSAGES, logInvoiceEvent, type InvoiceStore } from "./store";

export type InvoiceEmailDeps = {
  send: (message: EmailMessage) => Promise<void>;
  /** The PDF of one invoice (made by the app). */
  attachmentFor: (invoiceId: string) => Promise<EmailAttachment>;
};

type Outcome = "sent" | "no_recipient" | "attachment" | "send";

/** "Resend email": the invoice must be issued. Owner or manager. */
export async function checkInvoiceEmailable(store: Pick<InvoiceStore, "invoice">, actor: Actor, id: string): Promise<void> {
  assertRole(actor, MANAGEMENT);
  const inv = await store.invoice.findUnique({ where: { id }, select: { status: true } });
  if (!inv) throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.notFound);
  if (inv.status !== "ISSUED") throw new DomainError(INVOICE_MESSAGES.onlyIssuedEmail);
}

/**
 * Emails issued invoices, AFTER the issuing transaction has committed. Invoices are grouped by bill-to customer:
 * each address of its contacts who receive invoices gets one email with every PDF of the group attached. The
 * result is stored on each invoice (emailStatus) and in the audit log with fixed words. Never throws for an
 * email problem: each failure is logged with a fixed phrase only.
 */
export async function emailIssuedInvoices(
  store: Pick<InvoiceStore, "invoice" | "contact" | "auditLog">,
  actor: Actor,
  invoiceIds: readonly string[],
  deps: InvoiceEmailDeps,
): Promise<{ sent: number; failed: number }> {
  assertRole(actor, MANAGEMENT);
  const ids = [...new Set(invoiceIds)];
  if (ids.length === 0) return { sent: 0, failed: 0 };
  const invoices = await store.invoice.findMany({
    where: { id: { in: ids }, status: "ISSUED" },
    orderBy: [{ numberYear: "asc" }, { numberSeq: "asc" }],
    select: {
      id: true,
      numberYear: true,
      numberSeq: true,
      totalCents: true,
      dueDate: true,
      customerId: true,
      billToCustomerId: true,
      bankSnapshot: true,
      customer: { select: { displayName: true } },
    },
  });
  const groups = new Map<string, typeof invoices>();
  for (const inv of invoices) groups.set(inv.billToCustomerId, [...(groups.get(inv.billToCustomerId) ?? []), inv]);

  let sent = 0;
  let failed = 0;
  for (const [billToId, group] of groups) {
    let outcome: Outcome = "send";
    let recipients = 0;
    try {
      const contacts = await store.contact.findMany({
        where: { customerId: billToId, receivesInvoices: true, email: { not: null } },
        orderBy: { id: "asc" },
        select: { email: true },
      });
      const emails = [...new Set(contacts.flatMap((c) => (c.email ? [c.email] : [])))];
      if (emails.length === 0) {
        outcome = "no_recipient";
      } else {
        const attachments: EmailAttachment[] = [];
        let attached = true;
        try {
          for (const inv of group) attachments.push(await deps.attachmentFor(inv.id));
        } catch {
          console.error("An invoice PDF could not be made for an email.");
          attached = false;
        }
        if (!attached) {
          outcome = "attachment";
        } else {
          const snapshot = invoiceSnapshotSchema.parse(group[0]?.bankSnapshot);
          const items = group.map((inv) => ({
            reference: formatInvoiceReference(inv) ?? "",
            forName: inv.customerId !== billToId ? inv.customer.displayName : null,
            amountLabel: formatZAR(inv.totalCents ?? 0),
            dueDateLabel: inv.dueDate ? formatDateSA(inv.dueDate) : "",
          }));
          for (const to of emails) {
            try {
              await deps.send({
                ...invoiceEmail({
                  to,
                  invoices: items,
                  paymentReference: snapshot.paymentReference,
                  bank: bankDetailsReady(snapshot.bank) ? snapshot.bank : null,
                }),
                attachments,
              });
              recipients += 1;
            } catch {
              // Fixed words only: never the error (it may hold the address) or the address itself.
              console.error("An invoice email could not be sent.");
            }
          }
          outcome = recipients > 0 ? "sent" : "send";
        }
      }
    } catch {
      console.error("Invoice emails could not be prepared.");
      outcome = "send";
    }
    for (const inv of group) {
      try {
        await store.invoice.updateMany({
          where: { id: inv.id, status: "ISSUED" },
          data: outcome === "sent" ? { emailStatus: "SENT", emailedAt: new Date() } : { emailStatus: "FAILED" },
        });
        await logInvoiceEvent(
          store,
          actor,
          outcome === "sent" ? "invoice.emailed" : "invoice.email_failed",
          inv.id,
          outcome === "sent" ? { recipients } : { reason: outcome },
        );
      } catch {
        console.error("An invoice email result could not be saved.");
      }
      if (outcome === "sent") sent += 1;
      else failed += 1;
    }
  }
  return { sent, failed };
}
```

Note: the `updateMany` for SENT also writes `emailedAt`, so the first unit test's `updates.map((u) => u.data.emailStatus)` reads `["SENT", "SENT"]`.

- Consumes (`@ace/core`): `loadInvoiceDocument`, `loadStatementDocument`, `emailIssuedInvoices`, `invoicePdfFilename`, `statementPdfFilename`, `defaultStatementPeriod`, `statementPeriodProblem`, `sendEmail`, `getDb`, `inTx`, `MANAGEMENT`, `DomainError`, `ForbiddenError`, `billingTimingSchema`, `setBillingTiming`, `getInvoiceSettings`, `updateInvoiceSettings`, `invoiceSettingsFormSchema`, `formatCentsForInput`, `listCustomerInvoices`, `BILLING_TIMINGS`, `BILLING_TIMING_LABELS`, `INVOICE_STATUS_LABELS`, `formatZAR`, `formatDateSA`, `fromDateString`, `todaySA`, types `Actor`, `InvoiceDocument`, `StatementDocument`, `InvoiceDisplayStatus`, `BillingTiming`; (`@ace/ui`): `InvoicePdf`, `StatementPdf`.
- Produces (staff app):
  - `renderInvoicePdf(doc: InvoiceDocument): Promise<Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>>`, `renderStatementPdf(doc: StatementDocument): Promise<Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>>` (`lib/invoice-pdf.ts`, server only)
  - `emailInvoicesLater(actor: Actor, invoiceIds: readonly string[]): void` (`lib/invoice-email.ts`): schedules `emailIssuedInvoices` with `after()`; a failure is logged with a fixed phrase only.
  - `Notice({ notice }: { notice: { text: string; ok: boolean } | null })` and `noticeFor(code: string | undefined, table: Record<string, { text: string; ok: boolean }>): { text: string; ok: boolean } | null` (`components/notice.tsx`)
  - `InvoiceStatusBadge({ status }: { status: InvoiceDisplayStatus })`
  - `setBillingTimingAction(formData: FormData): Promise<void>` (in `invoices/actions.ts`)
  - `GET /invoices/[id]/pdf`, `GET /statements/[customerId]/pdf?from=YYYY-MM-DD&to=YYYY-MM-DD`
  - `/settings/invoices` (owner only), `saveInvoiceSettingsAction(prev: FormState, formData: FormData): Promise<FormState>`
  - The customer page's `invoices` tab (`InvoicesTab`).

Read `node_modules/next/dist/docs/01-app/03-api-reference/04-functions/after.md`, `.../redirect.md` and the route handler page (`node_modules/next/dist/docs/01-app/03-api-reference/03-file-conventions/route.md`) before writing this task's code.

- [ ] **Step 1: `apps/admin/src/lib/invoice-pdf.ts`**

```ts
import "server-only";
import type { InvoiceDocument, StatementDocument } from "@ace/core";
import { InvoicePdf, StatementPdf } from "@ace/ui/invoice-pdf";
import { renderToBuffer } from "@react-pdf/renderer";

// Called as functions (not createElement) so the element is typed as the Document renderToBuffer expects;
// the layouts must stay hook-free.
export async function renderInvoicePdf(doc: InvoiceDocument): Promise<Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>> {
  return new Uint8Array(await renderToBuffer(InvoicePdf({ doc })));
}

export async function renderStatementPdf(doc: StatementDocument): Promise<Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>> {
  return new Uint8Array(await renderToBuffer(StatementPdf({ doc })));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: `apps/admin/src/lib/invoice-email.ts`**

```ts
import "server-only";
import {
  emailIssuedInvoices,
  getDb,
  invoicePdfFilename,
  loadInvoiceDocument,
  sendEmail,
  type Actor,
} from "@ace/core";
import { after } from "next/server";
import { renderInvoicePdf } from "@/lib/invoice-pdf";

/**
 * After an issue has committed: emails the invoices (grouped per payer, PDFs attached) once the response has
 * gone. An email problem never touches the issued invoices; it is noted on each invoice and in its history,
 * and staff can press Resend email.
 */
export function emailInvoicesLater(actor: Actor, invoiceIds: readonly string[]): void {
  if (invoiceIds.length === 0) return;
  const ids = [...invoiceIds];
  after(async () => {
    try {
      await emailIssuedInvoices(getDb(), actor, ids, {
        send: (message) => sendEmail(message),
        attachmentFor: async (id) => {
          const loaded = await loadInvoiceDocument(getDb(), actor, id);
          if (!loaded) throw new Error("Invoice not found.");
          return {
            filename: invoicePdfFilename(loaded.doc.reference),
            content: await renderInvoicePdf(loaded.doc),
          };
        },
      });
    } catch {
      // Fixed words only: never the error (it may hold an address).
      console.error("Invoice emails could not be sent after an issue.");
    }
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `apps/admin/src/components/notice.tsx`** (no hooks, no handlers: used by server pages)

```tsx
export type NoticeText = { text: string; ok: boolean };

/** Only a fixed code arrives in the address bar; anything unknown shows nothing. */
export function noticeFor(
  code: string | undefined,
  table: Record<string, NoticeText>,
): NoticeText | null {
  if (!code || !Object.hasOwn(table, code)) return null;
  return table[code] ?? null;
}

export function Notice({ notice }: { notice: NoticeText | null }) {
  if (!notice) return null;
  return (
    <p
      role={notice.ok ? "status" : "alert"}
      className={`rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm ring-1 ${notice.ok ? "bg-forest/10 text-forest ring-forest/25" : "bg-red-50 text-red-800 ring-red-200"}`}
    >
      {notice.text}
    </p>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: `apps/admin/src/app/(staff)/invoices/actions.ts`** (first version; Task 15 appends the other actions to this file)

```ts
"use server";

import {
  billingTimingSchema,
  DomainError,
  ForbiddenError,
  inTx,
  MANAGEMENT,
  setBillingTiming,
} from "@ace/core";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { z } from "zod";
import { toActor } from "@/lib/actor";
import { requireStaff } from "@/lib/auth";

const idSchema = z.uuid();

/**
 * For buttons that cannot show an error themselves: a refused role goes to /forbidden, a refusal the user can
 * fix becomes a fixed notice code (never typed text), anything unexpected is re-thrown.
 */
function refusal(error: unknown): void {
  if (error instanceof ForbiddenError) redirect("/forbidden");
  if (!(error instanceof DomainError)) throw error;
}

/** The Pre or Post switch on the customer's Invoices tab. */
export async function setBillingTimingAction(formData: FormData): Promise<void> {
  const staff = await requireStaff(MANAGEMENT);
  const actor = await toActor(staff);
  const id = idSchema.safeParse(formData.get("customerId"));
  if (!id.success) redirect("/customers");
  const timing = billingTimingSchema.safeParse(formData.get("billingTiming"));
  let code = "timing-saved";
  if (!timing.success) {
    code = "timing-generic";
  } else {
    try {
      await inTx((tx) => setBillingTiming(tx, actor, id.data, timing.data));
    } catch (error) {
      refusal(error);
      code = "timing-generic";
    }
  }
  redirect(`/customers/${id.data}?tab=invoices&notice=${code}`);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: `apps/admin/src/app/(staff)/invoices/[id]/pdf/route.ts`**

```ts
import { getDb, invoicePdfFilename, loadInvoiceDocument } from "@ace/core";
import { z } from "zod";
import { toActor } from "@/lib/actor";
import { requireStaff } from "@/lib/auth";
import { renderInvoicePdf } from "@/lib/invoice-pdf";

export async function GET(_request: Request, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const staff = await requireStaff(["OWNER", "MANAGER"]); // every route starts with this
  const { id } = await params;
  if (!z.uuid().safeParse(id).success) return new Response("Not found", { status: 404 });
  const actor = await toActor(staff);
  const loaded = await loadInvoiceDocument(getDb(), actor, id);
  if (!loaded) return new Response("Not found", { status: 404 });
  const bytes = await renderInvoicePdf(loaded.doc);
  return new Response(bytes, {
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "application/pdf",
      "Content-Disposition": `inline; filename="${invoicePdfFilename(loaded.doc.reference)}"`,
      "Cache-Control": "private, no-store",
      "X-Robots-Tag": "noindex, nofollow",
    },
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: `apps/admin/src/app/(staff)/statements/[customerId]/pdf/route.ts`**

```ts
import {
  defaultStatementPeriod,
  getDb,
  loadStatementDocument,
  statementPdfFilename,
  statementPeriodProblem,
  todaySA,
} from "@ace/core";
import { z } from "zod";
import { toActor } from "@/lib/actor";
import { requireStaff } from "@/lib/auth";
import { renderStatementPdf } from "@/lib/invoice-pdf";

export async function GET(request: Request, { params }: { params: Promise<{ customerId: string }> }) {
  const staff = await requireStaff(["OWNER", "MANAGER"]); // every route starts with this
  const { customerId } = await params;
  if (!z.uuid().safeParse(customerId).success) return new Response("Not found", { status: 404 });
  const search = new URL(request.url).searchParams;
  const fallback = defaultStatementPeriod(todaySA());
  const period = { from: search.get("from") ?? fallback.from, to: search.get("to") ?? fallback.to };
  if (statementPeriodProblem(period.from, period.to)) return new Response("Not found", { status: 404 });
  const actor = await toActor(staff);
  const loaded = await loadStatementDocument(getDb(), actor, customerId, period);
  if (!loaded) return new Response("Not found", { status: 404 });
  const bytes = await renderStatementPdf(loaded.doc);
  return new Response(bytes, {
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "application/pdf",
      "Content-Disposition": `inline; filename="${statementPdfFilename(loaded.doc.customer.number, period.to)}"`,
      "Cache-Control": "private, no-store",
      "X-Robots-Tag": "noindex, nofollow",
    },
  });
}
```
