`apps/admin/src/app/(staff)/settings/invoices/actions.ts`:

```ts
"use server";

import { inTx, invoiceSettingsFormSchema, updateInvoiceSettings } from "@ace/core";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { toActor } from "@/lib/actor";
import { requireStaff } from "@/lib/auth";
import { errorsFrom, failure, formValues, type FormState } from "@/lib/form";

export async function saveInvoiceSettingsAction(_prev: FormState, formData: FormData): Promise<FormState> {
  const staff = await requireStaff(["OWNER"]);
  const actor = await toActor(staff);
  const values = formValues(formData);
  const parsed = invoiceSettingsFormSchema.safeParse({
    bankName: values.bankName ?? "",
    accountHolder: values.accountHolder ?? "",
    accountNumber: values.accountNumber ?? "",
    branchCode: values.branchCode ?? "",
    accountType: values.accountType ?? "",
    onceOffDueDays: values.onceOffDueDays ?? "",
    lateFee: values.lateFee ?? "",
  });
  if (!parsed.success) return { fieldErrors: errorsFrom(parsed.error), values, nonce: Date.now() };
  try {
    await inTx((tx) => updateInvoiceSettings(tx, actor, parsed.data));
  } catch (error) {
    return failure(error, values);
  }
  redirect("/settings/invoices?saved=1");
}
```

`apps/admin/src/app/(staff)/settings/invoices/settings-form.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client";

import { useActionState } from "react";
import { buttonClass } from "@/components/auth-card";
import { FormMessage, TextField } from "@/components/form";
import { saveInvoiceSettingsAction } from "./actions";

export type SettingsValues = {
  bankName: string;
  accountHolder: string;
  accountNumber: string;
  branchCode: string;
  accountType: string;
  onceOffDueDays: string;
  lateFee: string;
};

export function SettingsForm({ initial, saved }: { initial: SettingsValues; saved: boolean }) {
  const [state, action, pending] = useActionState(saveInvoiceSettingsAction, undefined);
  const v = { ...initial, ...(state?.values ?? {}) };
  const e = state?.fieldErrors ?? {};
  return (
    <form action={action} className="mt-6 space-y-6">
      {saved && !state ? (
        <p role="status" className="rounded-lg bg-forest/10 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-forest ring-1 ring-forest/25">
          Saved. Invoices issued from now on use these details.
        </p>
      ) : null}
      <div key={state?.nonce ?? "initial"} className="space-y-6">
        <fieldset className="space-y-4 rounded-2xl bg-white/70 p-4 ring-1 ring-charcoal/10">
          <legend className="px-1 font-display text-lg font-semibold text-forest">Bank details</legend>
          <TextField label="Bank" name="bankName" defaultValue={v.bankName} error={e.bankName} />
          <TextField label="Account holder" name="accountHolder" defaultValue={v.accountHolder} error={e.accountHolder} />
          <TextField
            label="Account number"
            name="accountNumber"
            inputMode="numeric"
            defaultValue={v.accountNumber}
            error={e.accountNumber}
          />
          <TextField
            label="Branch code"
            name="branchCode"
            inputMode="numeric"
            defaultValue={v.branchCode}
            error={e.branchCode}
          />
          <TextField
            label="Account type"
            name="accountType"
            hint="For example Business cheque."
            defaultValue={v.accountType}
            error={e.accountType}
          />
        </fieldset>
        <fieldset className="space-y-4 rounded-2xl bg-white/70 p-4 ring-1 ring-charcoal/10">
          <legend className="px-1 font-display text-lg font-semibold text-forest">Defaults</legend>
          <TextField
            label="Once-off invoices are due after (days)"
            name="onceOffDueDays"
            inputMode="numeric"
            required
            hint="Counted from the day the invoice is issued. Staff can change it on each invoice."
            defaultValue={v.onceOffDueDays}
            error={e.onceOffDueDays}
          />
          <TextField
            label="Late payment fee (rand)"
            name="lateFee"
            inputMode="decimal"
            hint="Suggested on the next monthly invoice for each overdue invoice. 0 switches the suggestion off."
            defaultValue={v.lateFee}
            error={e.lateFee}
          />
        </fieldset>
      </div>
      <FormMessage error={state?.error} />
      <button type="submit" disabled={pending} className={buttonClass}>
        {pending ? "Saving..." : "Save invoice settings"}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

`apps/admin/src/app/(staff)/settings/invoices/page.tsx`:

```tsx
import { formatCentsForInput, getDb, getInvoiceSettings } from "@ace/core";
import { toActor } from "@/lib/actor";
import { requireStaff } from "@/lib/auth";
import { first } from "@/lib/search-params";
import { SettingsForm } from "./settings-form";

export default async function InvoiceSettingsPage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<{ saved?: string | string[] }>;
}) {
  const staff = await requireStaff(["OWNER"]); // owner only
  const actor = await toActor(staff);
  const sp = await searchParams;
  const s = await getInvoiceSettings(getDb(), actor);
  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-2xl">
      <h1 className="font-display text-2xl font-semibold text-forest">Invoice settings</h1>
      <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-charcoal/70">
        ACE&apos;s bank details are printed on every invoice and statement. Changes apply to invoices issued
        afterwards: an invoice that has been issued keeps the details it was issued with. Invoices cannot be
        issued until the bank, account holder, account number and branch code are filled in.
      </p>
      {/* A new key after every save starts the form afresh, so its "saved" message shows again. */}
      <SettingsForm
        key={s.updatedAt.toISOString()}
        saved={first(sp.saved) === "1"}
        initial={{
          bankName: s.bankName,
          accountHolder: s.accountHolder,
          accountNumber: s.accountNumber,
          branchCode: s.branchCode,
          accountType: s.accountType,
          onceOffDueDays: String(s.onceOffDueDays),
          lateFee: formatCentsForInput(s.lateFeeCents),
        }}
      />
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 10: `apps/admin/src/app/(staff)/customers/[id]/invoices-tab.tsx`** (a server component: forms post to server actions, nothing else)

```tsx
import {
  BILLING_TIMING_LABELS,
  BILLING_TIMINGS,
  formatDateSA,
  formatZAR,
  fromDateString,
  type BillingTiming,
  type listCustomerInvoices,
} from "@ace/core";
import Link from "next/link";
import { secondaryButtonClass } from "@/components/form";
import { Notice, noticeFor } from "@/components/notice";
import { setBillingTimingAction } from "../../invoices/actions";
import { InvoiceStatusBadge } from "../../invoices/status-badge";

type Data = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof listCustomerInvoices>>;

const NOTICES = {
  "timing-saved": { text: "Billing timing saved.", ok: true },
  "timing-generic": { text: "That could not be saved. Refresh the page and try again.", ok: false },
};

const day = (value: string) => formatDateSA(fromDateString(value));
const linkButton =
  "inline-flex min-h-10 items-center rounded-full bg-forest px-5 py-2.5 font-display text-sm font-semibold text-cream hover:bg-forest-deep";

export function InvoicesTab({
  customerId,
  billingTiming,
  locked,
  data,
  notice,
}: {
  customerId: string;
  billingTiming: BillingTiming;
  /** Archived or anonymised: nothing can be changed or started from here. */
  locked: boolean;
  data: Data;
  notice: string | undefined;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <Notice notice={noticeFor(notice, NOTICES)} />

      <section className="space-y-3 rounded-2xl bg-white/70 p-4 ring-1 ring-charcoal/10" aria-labelledby="timing-heading">
        <h2 id="timing-heading" className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-forest">
          Billing timing
        </h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-charcoal/70">
          Pre: the run on the 20th bills next month, due at the end of this month. Post: it bills this month,
          due on the 25th.
        </p>
        {locked ? (
          <p className="text-sm">{BILLING_TIMING_LABELS[billingTiming]}</p>
        ) : (
          <form action={setBillingTimingAction} className="space-y-2">
            <input type="hidden" name="customerId" value={customerId} />
            <fieldset className="space-y-1">
              <legend className="sr-only">Billing timing</legend>
              {BILLING_TIMINGS.map((t) => (
                <label key={t} className="flex min-h-10 items-center gap-3 text-sm">
                  <input
                    type="radio"
                    name="billingTiming"
                    value={t}
                    defaultChecked={t === billingTiming}
                    className="h-4 w-4 accent-forest"
                  />
                  {BILLING_TIMING_LABELS[t]}
                </label>
              ))}
            </fieldset>
            <button type="submit" className={secondaryButtonClass}>
              Save billing timing
            </button>
          </form>
        )}
      </section>

      <section className="space-y-3" aria-labelledby="money-heading">
        <h2 id="money-heading" className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-forest">
          Money
        </h2>
        <p className="text-sm">
          Owed as the payer: <span className="font-semibold">{formatZAR(data.owedCents)}</span>
          {data.creditCents > 0 ? (
            <span className="block text-charcoal/70">
              Credit on account (paid, not yet allocated): {formatZAR(data.creditCents)}
            </span>
          ) : null}
        </p>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {!locked ? (
            <Link href={`/invoices/new?customer=${customerId}`} className={linkButton}>
              New invoice
            </Link>
          ) : null}
          <Link href={`/payments/new?payer=${customerId}`} className={secondaryButtonClass}>
            Record payment
          </Link>
          <Link href={`/statements?customer=${customerId}`} className={secondaryButtonClass}>
            Statement
          </Link>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="space-y-2" aria-labelledby="invoices-heading">
        <h2 id="invoices-heading" className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-forest">
          Invoices
        </h2>
        {data.items.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-charcoal/60">No invoices yet.</p>
        ) : (
          <ul className="divide-y divide-charcoal/10 rounded-2xl bg-white/70 ring-1 ring-charcoal/10">
            {data.items.map((i) => (
              <li key={i.id}>
                <Link
                  href={`/invoices/${i.id}`}
                  className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 px-4 py-3 hover:bg-cream/60"
                >
                  <span className="min-w-0 break-words">
                    <span className="font-semibold text-forest">{i.reference ?? "Draft"}</span>
                    <span className="block text-sm text-charcoal/60">
                      {[
                        formatZAR(i.totalCents),
                        i.dueDate ? `due ${day(i.dueDate)}` : null,
                        i.billTo.id !== customerId ? `billed to ${i.billTo.displayName}` : null,
                        i.customer.id !== customerId ? `for ${i.customer.displayName}` : null,
                      ]
                        .filter(Boolean)
                        .join(", ")}
                    </span>
                  </span>
                  <InvoiceStatusBadge status={i.displayStatus} />
                </Link>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        )}
      </section>

      <section className="space-y-2" aria-labelledby="payments-heading">
        <h2 id="payments-heading" className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-forest">
          Payments received
        </h2>
        {data.payments.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-charcoal/60">No payments recorded.</p>
        ) : (
          <ul className="divide-y divide-charcoal/10 rounded-2xl bg-white/70 ring-1 ring-charcoal/10">
            {data.payments.map((p) => (
              <li key={p.id}>
                <Link
                  href={`/payments/${p.id}`}
                  className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 px-4 py-3 hover:bg-cream/60"
                >
                  <span className="min-w-0 break-words">
                    <span className="font-semibold">{formatZAR(p.amountCents)}</span>
                    <span className="block text-sm text-charcoal/60">
                      {[day(p.receivedOn), p.reference, p.unallocatedCents > 0 ? `${formatZAR(p.unallocatedCents)} not allocated` : null]
                        .filter(Boolean)
                        .join(", ")}
                    </span>
                  </span>
                  {p.voided ? (
                    <span className="rounded-full bg-charcoal/10 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-charcoal/50">
                      Void
                    </span>
                  ) : null}
                </Link>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Replace `apps/admin/src/app/(staff)/invoices/actions.ts`** with:

```ts
"use server";

import {
  billingTimingSchema,
  checkInvoiceEmailable,
  createMonthlyDrafts,
  createOnceOffDraft,
  deleteInvoiceDraft,
  dismissPaymentReport,
  DomainError,
  draftInputFromForm,
  ForbiddenError,
  getDb,
  inTx,
  INVOICE_MESSAGES,
  issueAllDrafts,
  issueInvoice,
  MANAGEMENT,
  runMonthSchema,
  saveInvoiceDraft,
  setBillingTiming,
  voidInvoice,
  type RunResult,
} from "@ace/core";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { z } from "zod";
import { toActor } from "@/lib/actor";
import { requireStaff } from "@/lib/auth";
import { failure, formValues, type FormState } from "@/lib/form";
import { emailInvoicesLater } from "@/lib/invoice-email";

const idSchema = z.uuid();
const BAD_REQUEST = "Something went wrong. Refresh the page and try again.";

/**
 * For buttons that cannot show an error themselves: a refused role goes to /forbidden, a refusal the user can
 * fix becomes a fixed notice code (never typed text), anything unexpected is re-thrown.
 */
function refusal(error: unknown): void {
  if (error instanceof ForbiddenError) redirect("/forbidden");
  if (!(error instanceof DomainError)) throw error;
}

function codeFor(error: unknown): string {
  refusal(error);
  const message = error instanceof DomainError ? error.message : "";
  if (message === INVOICE_MESSAGES.quoteInvoiced) return "quote-invoiced";
  if (message === INVOICE_MESSAGES.archived) return "archived";
  if (message === INVOICE_MESSAGES.runMonth) return "run-month";
  return "cannot";
}

/** The Pre or Post switch on the customer's Invoices tab. */
export async function setBillingTimingAction(formData: FormData): Promise<void> {
  const staff = await requireStaff(MANAGEMENT);
  const actor = await toActor(staff);
  const id = idSchema.safeParse(formData.get("customerId"));
  if (!id.success) redirect("/customers");
  const timing = billingTimingSchema.safeParse(formData.get("billingTiming"));
  let code = "timing-saved";
  if (!timing.success) {
    code = "timing-generic";
  } else {
    try {
      await inTx((tx) => setBillingTiming(tx, actor, id.data, timing.data));
    } catch (error) {
      refusal(error);
      code = "timing-generic";
    }
  }
  redirect(`/customers/${id.data}?tab=invoices&notice=${code}`);
}

/** "Create this month's invoices": drafts only, each customer in its own transaction. */
export async function runMonthAction(formData: FormData): Promise<void> {
  const staff = await requireStaff(MANAGEMENT);
  const actor = await toActor(staff);
  const month = runMonthSchema.safeParse(formData.get("runMonth"));
  if (!month.success) redirect("/invoices?notice=run-month");
  let result: RunResult;
  try {
    result = await createMonthlyDrafts(getDb(), actor, month.data, { run: inTx });
  } catch (error) {
    redirect(`/invoices?notice=${codeFor(error)}`);
  }
  const failed = result.skipped.filter((s) => s.reason === "failed").length;
  redirect(
    `/invoices?notice=run&created=${result.created.length}&skipped=${result.skipped.length - failed}&failed=${failed}`,
  );
}

/** "Issue all drafts": each in its own transaction; the emails go after the response. */
export async function issueAllAction(formData: FormData): Promise<void> {
  const staff = await requireStaff(MANAGEMENT);
  const actor = await toActor(staff);
  if (formData.get("confirm") !== "yes") redirect("/invoices");
  let result: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof issueAllDrafts>>;
  try {
    result = await issueAllDrafts(getDb(), actor, { run: inTx });
  } catch (error) {
    redirect(`/invoices?notice=${codeFor(error)}`);
  }
  emailInvoicesLater(
    actor,
    result.issued.map((i) => i.id),
  );
  redirect(`/invoices?notice=issued-all&issued=${result.issued.length}&failed=${result.failed.length}`);
}

/** "New invoice" on a customer: an empty once-off draft, or one pre-filled from an accepted quote. */
export async function createOnceOffAction(formData: FormData): Promise<void> {
  const staff = await requireStaff(MANAGEMENT);
  const actor = await toActor(staff);
  const customer = idSchema.safeParse(formData.get("customerId"));
  if (!customer.success) redirect("/invoices/new");
  const quoteText = formData.get("quoteId");
  const quote = typeof quoteText === "string" && quoteText !== "" ? idSchema.safeParse(quoteText) : null;
  if (quote && !quote.success) redirect(`/invoices/new?customer=${customer.data}&notice=cannot`);
  let id: string;
  try {
    id = (
      await inTx((tx) =>
        createOnceOffDraft(tx, actor, { customerId: customer.data, fromQuoteId: quote?.data ?? null }),
      )
    ).id;
  } catch (error) {
    redirect(`/invoices/new?customer=${customer.data}&notice=${codeFor(error)}`);
  }
  redirect(`/invoices/${id}`);
}

export async function saveInvoiceDraftAction(_prev: FormState, formData: FormData): Promise<FormState> {
  const staff = await requireStaff(MANAGEMENT);
  const actor = await toActor(staff);
  const values = formValues(formData);
  const id = idSchema.safeParse(values.id);
  if (!id.success) return { error: BAD_REQUEST, nonce: Date.now() };
  const parsed = draftInputFromForm(values);
  if (!parsed.ok) {
    const { form, ...fieldErrors } = parsed.fieldErrors;
    return { error: form ?? "Check the boxes marked below.", fieldErrors, values, nonce: Date.now() };
  }
  try {
    await inTx((tx) => saveInvoiceDraft(tx, actor, id.data, parsed.input));
  } catch (error) {
    return failure(error, values);
  }
  redirect(`/invoices/${id.data}?notice=saved`);
}

/** Issue one draft. Every problem comes back as one line of the message. The email goes after the response. */
export async function issueInvoiceAction(_prev: FormState, formData: FormData): Promise<FormState> {
  const staff = await requireStaff(MANAGEMENT);
  const actor = await toActor(staff);
  const id = idSchema.safeParse(formData.get("id"));
  if (!id.success) return { error: BAD_REQUEST, nonce: Date.now() };
  try {
    await inTx((tx) => issueInvoice(tx, actor, id.data));
  } catch (error) {
    return failure(error);
  }
  emailInvoicesLater(actor, [id.data]);
  redirect(`/invoices/${id.data}?notice=issued`);
}

export async function deleteInvoiceDraftAction(formData: FormData): Promise<void> {
  const staff = await requireStaff(MANAGEMENT);
  const actor = await toActor(staff);
  const id = idSchema.safeParse(formData.get("id"));
  if (!id.success || formData.get("confirm") !== "yes") redirect("/invoices");
  try {
    await inTx((tx) => deleteInvoiceDraft(tx, actor, id.data));
  } catch (error) {
    redirect(`/invoices/${id.data}?notice=${codeFor(error)}`);
  }
  redirect("/invoices?notice=deleted");
}

export async function voidInvoiceAction(_prev: FormState, formData: FormData): Promise<FormState> {
  const staff = await requireStaff(MANAGEMENT);
  const actor = await toActor(staff);
  const values = formValues(formData);
  const id = idSchema.safeParse(values.id);
  if (!id.success) return { error: BAD_REQUEST, nonce: Date.now() };
  try {
    await inTx((tx) => voidInvoice(tx, actor, id.data, values.reason ?? ""));
  } catch (error) {
    return failure(error, values);
  }
  redirect(`/invoices/${id.data}?notice=voided`);
}

/** "Resend email" on an issued invoice. */
export async function resendInvoiceEmailAction(formData: FormData): Promise<void> {
  const staff = await requireStaff(MANAGEMENT);
  const actor = await toActor(staff);
  const id = idSchema.safeParse(formData.get("id"));
  if (!id.success) redirect("/invoices");
  try {
    await checkInvoiceEmailable(getDb(), actor, id.data);
  } catch (error) {
    redirect(`/invoices/${id.data}?notice=${codeFor(error)}`);
  }
  emailInvoicesLater(actor, [id.data]);
  redirect(`/invoices/${id.data}?notice=email-queued`);
}

/** "Dismiss" on a reported payment, from the invoice page (`back` = the invoice id) or the reports list. */
export async function dismissReportAction(formData: FormData): Promise<void> {
  const staff = await requireStaff(MANAGEMENT);
  const actor = await toActor(staff);
  const id = idSchema.safeParse(formData.get("id"));
  const backRaw = formData.get("back");
  const backInvoice = idSchema.safeParse(backRaw);
  const back = backInvoice.success ? `/invoices/${backInvoice.data}` : "/payments/reports";
  if (!id.success) redirect(back);
  let code = "dismissed";
  try {
    await inTx((tx) => dismissPaymentReport(tx, actor, id.data));
  } catch (error) {
    code = codeFor(error);
  }
  redirect(`${back}?notice=${code}`);
}
```

```tsx
import {
  currentMonthSA,
  DRAFT_WARNING_LABELS,
  formatCustomerNumber,
  formatDateSA,
  formatMonthLabel,
  formatZAR,
  fromDateString,
  getDb,
  INVOICE_FILTERS,
  invoiceSettingsStatus,
  listDraftsForReview,
  listInvoices,
} from "@ace/core";
import Link from "next/link";
import { inputClass } from "@/components/auth-card";
import { dangerButtonClass, secondaryButtonClass } from "@/components/form";
import { Notice, noticeFor, type NoticeText } from "@/components/notice";
import { toActor } from "@/lib/actor";
import { requireStaff } from "@/lib/auth";
import { first, nonNegativeInt, positiveInt } from "@/lib/search-params";
import { issueAllAction, runMonthAction } from "./actions";
import { InvoiceStatusBadge } from "./status-badge";

type Param = string | string[] | undefined;
type Search = {
  q?: Param;
  status?: Param;
  page?: Param;
  notice?: Param;
  created?: Param;
  skipped?: Param;
  failed?: Param;
  issued?: Param;
};

const FILTER_LABELS: Record<"ALL" | (typeof INVOICE_FILTERS)[number], string> = {
  ALL: "All",
  DRAFT: "Drafts",
  UNPAID: "Unpaid",
  OVERDUE: "Overdue",
  PAID: "Paid",
  VOID: "Void",
};

const FIXED: Record<string, NoticeText> = {
  deleted: { text: "Draft deleted.", ok: true },
  cannot: { text: "That could not be done. Refresh the page and try again.", ok: false },
  "run-month": { text: "Choose a month from a year ago up to next month.", ok: false },
};

const day = (value: string) => formatDateSA(fromDateString(value));
const plural = (n: number, word: string) => `${n} ${word}${n === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;

/** Counts are whole numbers from the address bar; nothing typed is ever shown. */
function pageNotice(sp: Search): NoticeText | null {
  const code = first(sp.notice);
  const n = (p: Param) => nonNegativeInt(p) ?? 0;
  if (code === "run") {
    const failed = n(sp.failed);
    return {
      text:
        `Created ${plural(n(sp.created), "draft")}. Skipped ${plural(n(sp.skipped), "customer")} (already invoiced, not started yet, paused, archived or still a lead).` +
        (failed > 0 ? ` ${plural(failed, "customer")} could not be drafted: press the button again to retry.` : ""),
      ok: failed === 0,
    };
  }
  if (code === "issued-all") {
    const failed = n(sp.failed);
    return {
      text:
        `Issued ${plural(n(sp.issued), "invoice")}. The emails go out in the background.` +
        (failed > 0 ? ` ${plural(failed, "draft")} could not be issued: open them to see why.` : ""),
      ok: failed === 0,
    };
  }
  return noticeFor(code, FIXED);
}

export default async function InvoicesPage({ searchParams }: { searchParams: Promise<Search> }) {
  const staff = await requireStaff(["OWNER", "MANAGER"]); // every page starts with this
  const sp = await searchParams;
  const actor = await toActor(staff);
  const db = getDb();
  const q = (first(sp.q) ?? "").trim().slice(0, 100);
  const status = INVOICE_FILTERS.find((f) => f === first(sp.status));
  const page = positiveInt(sp.page, 10_000) ?? 1;
  const [settings, drafts, result] = await Promise.all([
    invoiceSettingsStatus(db, actor),
    listDraftsForReview(db, actor),
    listInvoices(db, actor, { q, status, page }),
  ]);
  const pages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(result.total / result.pageSize));
  const link = (over: { status?: string | undefined; page?: number }) => {
    const params = new URLSearchParams();
    if (q) params.set("q", q);
    const s = "status" in over ? over.status : status;
    if (s) params.set("status", s);
    if (over.page && over.page > 1) params.set("page", String(over.page));
    const text = params.toString();
    return text ? `/invoices?${text}` : "/invoices";
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-8">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <h1 className="font-display text-2xl font-semibold text-forest">Invoices</h1>
        <Link
          href="/invoices/new"
          className="inline-flex min-h-10 items-center rounded-full bg-forest px-5 py-2.5 font-display text-sm font-semibold text-cream hover:bg-forest-deep"
        >
          New once-off invoice
        </Link>
      </div>
      <Notice notice={pageNotice(sp)} />
      {!settings.bankReady ? (
        <p role="alert" className="rounded-lg bg-ochre/10 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-charcoal ring-1 ring-ochre/30">
          ACE&apos;s bank details are not entered yet, so invoices cannot be issued.{" "}
          {staff.role === "OWNER" ? (
            <Link href="/settings/invoices" className="font-semibold text-forest underline">
              Enter them in Invoice settings
            </Link>
          ) : (
            "Ask the owner to enter them in Invoice settings."
          )}
        </p>
      ) : null}

      <section aria-labelledby="run-heading" className="space-y-3 rounded-2xl bg-white/70 p-4 ring-1 ring-charcoal/10">
        <h2 id="run-heading" className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-forest">
          Monthly run
        </h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-charcoal/70">
          Normally done on the 20th. Pre clients get next month&apos;s invoice, due at the end of this month.
          Post clients get this month&apos;s invoice, due on the 25th. Only drafts are made: nothing is sent
          until you issue them. Running it again for the same month makes no duplicates.
        </p>
        <form action={runMonthAction} className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-2">
          <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-charcoal">
            Month of the run
            <input
              type="month"
              name="runMonth"
              required
              defaultValue={currentMonthSA()}
              className={`${inputClass} min-h-10`}
            />
          </label>
          <button
            type="submit"
            className="inline-flex min-h-10 items-center rounded-full bg-forest px-5 py-2.5 font-display text-sm font-semibold text-cream hover:bg-forest-deep"
          >
            Create this month&apos;s invoices
          </button>
        </form>
      </section>

      <section aria-labelledby="drafts-heading" className="space-y-3">
        <h2 id="drafts-heading" className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-forest">
          Drafts to review ({drafts.length})
        </h2>
        {drafts.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-charcoal/60">No drafts waiting.</p>
        ) : (
          <>
            <ul className="divide-y divide-charcoal/10 rounded-2xl bg-white/70 ring-1 ring-charcoal/10">
              {drafts.map((d) => (
                <li key={d.id}>
                  <Link href={`/invoices/${d.id}`} className="block px-4 py-3 hover:bg-cream/60">
                    <span className="flex flex-wrap items-baseline justify-between gap-2">
                      <span className="min-w-0 break-words font-semibold text-forest">
                        {d.customer.displayName}{" "}
                        <span className="text-xs font-normal text-charcoal/50">
                          {formatCustomerNumber(d.customer.number)}
                        </span>
                      </span>
                      <span className="font-semibold">{formatZAR(d.totalCents)}</span>
                    </span>
                    <span className="block text-sm text-charcoal/60">
                      {[
                        d.serviceMonth ? `Monthly, ${formatMonthLabel(d.serviceMonth)}` : "Once-off",
                        d.dueDate ? `due ${day(d.dueDate)}` : null,
                        d.billTo.id !== d.customer.id ? `billed to ${d.billTo.displayName}` : null,
                        `${d.lineCount} ${d.lineCount === 1 ? "line" : "lines"}`,
                      ]
                        .filter(Boolean)
                        .join(", ")}
                    </span>
                    {d.warnings.length > 0 ? (
                      <span className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap gap-1">
                        {d.warnings.map((w) => (
                          <span key={w} className="rounded-full bg-ochre/15 px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-ochre">
                            {DRAFT_WARNING_LABELS[w]}
                          </span>
                        ))}
                      </span>
                    ) : null}
                  </Link>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
            {settings.bankReady ? (
              <details className="rounded-2xl bg-white/70 p-4 ring-1 ring-charcoal/10">
                <summary className="min-h-10 cursor-pointer py-2 font-display text-base font-semibold text-forest">
                  Issue all {plural(drafts.length, "draft")}
                </summary>
                <form action={issueAllAction} className="mt-3 space-y-3">
                  <input type="hidden" name="confirm" value="yes" />
                  <p className="text-sm text-charcoal/70">
                    Every draft above gets its invoice number and is emailed to the people who receive
                    invoices. Drafts with a problem are left as drafts.
                  </p>
                  <button type="submit" className={dangerButtonClass}>
                    Yes, issue and email them all
                  </button>
                </form>
              </details>
            ) : null}
          </>
        )}
      </section>

      <section aria-labelledby="all-heading" className="space-y-4">
        <h2 id="all-heading" className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-forest">
          All invoices
        </h2>
        <form method="get" className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {status ? <input type="hidden" name="status" value={status} /> : null}
          <input
            name="q"
            defaultValue={q}
            placeholder="Search invoice number or customer name"
            aria-label="Search invoices"
            className={`${inputClass} mt-0 flex-1 basis-64`}
          />
          <button type="submit" className={secondaryButtonClass}>
            Search
          </button>
        </form>
        <nav className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-sm font-semibold" aria-label="Status filter">
          {(["ALL", ...INVOICE_FILTERS] as const).map((f) => {
            const active = f === "ALL" ? !status : f === status;
            return (
              <Link
                key={f}
                href={link({ status: f === "ALL" ? undefined : f })}
                aria-current={active ? "page" : undefined}
                className={`inline-flex min-h-10 items-center rounded-full px-4 ${active ? "bg-forest text-cream" : "bg-white/70 text-forest ring-1 ring-charcoal/10 hover:bg-cream"}`}
              >
                {FILTER_LABELS[f]}
              </Link>
            );
          })}
        </nav>
        {result.items.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-center text-charcoal/60">
            {q || status ? "No invoices match that search or filter." : "No invoices yet."}
          </p>
        ) : (
          <ul className="divide-y divide-charcoal/10 rounded-2xl bg-white/70 ring-1 ring-charcoal/10">
            {result.items.map((i) => (
              <li key={i.id}>
                <Link
                  href={`/invoices/${i.id}`}
                  className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 px-4 py-3 hover:bg-cream/60"
                >
                  <span className="min-w-0 break-words">
                    <span className="font-semibold text-forest">{i.reference ?? "Draft"}</span>
                    <span className="ml-2 text-sm">{i.customer.displayName}</span>
                    <span className="block text-sm text-charcoal/60">
                      {[
                        formatZAR(i.totalCents),
                        i.status === "ISSUED" && i.balanceCents > 0 ? `${formatZAR(i.balanceCents)} owed` : null,
                        i.dueDate ? `due ${day(i.dueDate)}` : null,
                        i.billTo.id !== i.customer.id ? `billed to ${i.billTo.displayName}` : null,
                        i.emailStatus === "FAILED" ? "email not sent" : null,
                      ]
                        .filter(Boolean)
                        .join(", ")}
                    </span>
                  </span>
                  <InvoiceStatusBadge status={i.displayStatus} />
                </Link>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        )}
        {pages > 1 ? (
          <nav className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm" aria-label="Pages">
            {page > 1 ? (
              <Link href={link({ page: page - 1 })} className="inline-flex min-h-10 items-center px-2 text-forest hover:underline">
                Previous
              </Link>
            ) : (
              <span />
            )}
            <span className="text-charcoal/60">
              Page {page} of {pages} ({result.total} invoices)
            </span>
            {page < pages ? (
              <Link href={link({ page: page + 1 })} className="inline-flex min-h-10 items-center px-2 text-forest hover:underline">
                Next
              </Link>
            ) : (
              <span />
            )}
          </nav>
        ) : null}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `apps/admin/src/app/(staff)/invoices/new/page.tsx`**

```tsx
import {
  formatCustomerNumber,
  formatZAR,
  getCustomer,
  getDb,
  listAcceptedOnceOffQuotes,
  listCustomers,
} from "@ace/core";
import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import { z } from "zod";
import { inputClass } from "@/components/auth-card";
import { secondaryButtonClass } from "@/components/form";
import { Notice, noticeFor } from "@/components/notice";
import { toActor } from "@/lib/actor";
import { requireStaff } from "@/lib/auth";
import { first } from "@/lib/search-params";
import { createOnceOffAction } from "../actions";

type Param = string | string[] | undefined;

const NOTICES = {
  "quote-invoiced": { text: "That quote has already been invoiced.", ok: false },
  archived: { text: "Restore this customer before invoicing.", ok: false },
  cannot: { text: "That could not be done. Refresh the page and try again.", ok: false },
};

export default async function NewInvoicePage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<{ customer?: Param; q?: Param; notice?: Param }>;
}) {
  const staff = await requireStaff(["OWNER", "MANAGER"]); // every page starts with this
  const sp = await searchParams;
  const actor = await toActor(staff);
  const db = getDb();
  const picked = z.uuid().safeParse(first(sp.customer));

  if (!picked.success) {
    const q = (first(sp.q) ?? "").trim().slice(0, 100);
    const found = q ? await listCustomers(db, actor, { q, pageSize: 20 }) : null;
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-2xl space-y-4">
        <Link href="/invoices" className="inline-flex min-h-10 items-center text-sm text-forest hover:underline">
          ← Invoices
        </Link>
        <h1 className="font-display text-2xl font-semibold text-forest">New once-off invoice</h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-charcoal/70">Find the customer the work was for.</p>
        <form method="get" role="search" className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <input
            name="q"
            defaultValue={q}
            maxLength={100}
            placeholder="Customer name, number or email"
            aria-label="Find a customer"
            className={`${inputClass} mt-0 flex-1 basis-64`}
          />
          <button type="submit" className={secondaryButtonClass}>
            Search
          </button>
        </form>
        {found ? (
          found.items.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-sm text-charcoal/60">No customer matches that search.</p>
          ) : (
            <ul className="divide-y divide-charcoal/10 rounded-2xl bg-white/70 ring-1 ring-charcoal/10">
              {found.items.map((c) => (
                <li key={c.id}>
                  <Link
                    href={`/invoices/new?customer=${c.id}`}
                    className="flex min-h-10 flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 px-4 py-3 hover:bg-cream/60"
                  >
                    <span className="min-w-0 break-words font-semibold text-forest">{c.displayName}</span>
                    <span className="text-xs text-charcoal/50">{formatCustomerNumber(c.number)}</span>
                  </Link>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          )
        ) : null}
      </div>
    );
  }

  const customer = await getCustomer(db, actor, picked.data);
  if (!customer) notFound();
  const locked = customer.status === "ARCHIVED" || Boolean(customer.anonymisedAt);
  const quotes = locked ? [] : await listAcceptedOnceOffQuotes(db, actor, customer.id);
  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-2xl space-y-4">
      <Link
        href={`/customers/${customer.id}?tab=invoices`}
        className="inline-flex min-h-10 items-center text-sm text-forest hover:underline"
      >
        ← {customer.displayName}
      </Link>
      <h1 className="font-display text-2xl font-semibold text-forest">New invoice</h1>
      <Notice notice={noticeFor(first(sp.notice), NOTICES)} />
      {locked ? (
        <p className="text-sm text-charcoal/70">This customer is archived or anonymised and cannot be invoiced.</p>
      ) : (
        <>
          <section className="space-y-2">
            <h2 className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-forest">From an accepted quote</h2>
            {quotes.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="text-sm text-charcoal/60">No accepted quote with once-off work.</p>
            ) : (
              <ul className="space-y-2">
                {quotes.map((q) => (
                  <li key={q.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-white/70 p-4 ring-1 ring-charcoal/10">
                    <span className="min-w-0 break-words">
                      <span className="font-semibold text-forest">{q.reference}</span>
                      <span className="block text-sm text-charcoal/60">
                        {q.propertyLabel}, once-off work {formatZAR(q.onceOffCents)}
                      </span>
                    </span>
                    {q.alreadyInvoiced ? (
                      <span className="text-sm text-charcoal/60">Already invoiced</span>
                    ) : (
                      <form action={createOnceOffAction}>
                        <input type="hidden" name="customerId" value={customer.id} />
                        <input type="hidden" name="quoteId" value={q.id} />
                        <button type="submit" className={secondaryButtonClass}>
                          Invoice this quote
                        </button>
                      </form>
                    )}
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            )}
          </section>
          <section className="space-y-2">
            <h2 className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-forest">Or type the lines yourself</h2>
            <form action={createOnceOffAction}>
              <input type="hidden" name="customerId" value={customer.id} />
              <button type="submit" className={secondaryButtonClass}>
                Start an empty invoice
              </button>
            </form>
          </section>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `apps/admin/src/app/(staff)/invoices/[id]/draft-form.tsx`**

```tsx
"use client";

import { INVOICE_LINE_KIND_LABELS, MAX_INVOICE_LINES, NEW_LINE_KINDS } from "@ace/core/client";
import type { InvoiceLineKindValue } from "@ace/core";
import { useActionState } from "react";
import { buttonClass, inputClass } from "@/components/auth-card";
import { FormMessage } from "@/components/form";
import { saveInvoiceDraftAction } from "../actions";

export type DraftLine = { id: string; kind: InvoiceLineKindValue; description: string; amount: string };

const EMPTY_ROWS = 3;

function FieldError({ message }: { message: string | undefined }) {
  return message ? (
    <span role="alert" className="mt-1 block text-xs font-normal text-red-700">
      {message}
    </span>
  ) : null;
}

/** The whole draft in one form: due date, every line, three empty rows for new lines. */
export function DraftForm({
  id,
  version,
  monthly,
  dueDate,
  lines,
}: {
  id: string;
  version: number;
  monthly: boolean;
  dueDate: string;
  lines: DraftLine[];
}) {
  const [state, action, pending] = useActionState(saveInvoiceDraftAction, undefined);
  const typed = state?.values;
  const e = state?.fieldErrors ?? {};
  const val = (name: string, fallback: string) => (typed ? (typed[name] ?? "") : fallback);
  const empty = Math.max(0, Math.min(EMPTY_ROWS, MAX_INVOICE_LINES - lines.length));
  const rows = [
    ...lines.map((l) => ({ ...l, existing: true })),
    ...Array.from({ length: empty }, () => ({
      id: "",
      kind: "SERVICE" as InvoiceLineKindValue,
      description: "",
      amount: "",
      existing: false,
    })),
  ];
  return (
    <form action={action} className="space-y-4">
      <input type="hidden" name="id" value={id} />
      <input type="hidden" name="version" value={version} />
      <div key={state?.nonce ?? "initial"} className="space-y-4">
        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-charcoal">
          Due date
          <input
            type="date"
            name="dueDate"
            required={monthly}
            defaultValue={val("dueDate", dueDate)}
            aria-invalid={e.dueDate ? true : undefined}
            className={`${inputClass} min-h-10`}
          />
          <span className="mt-1 block text-xs font-normal text-charcoal/60">
            {monthly
              ? "Set by the run from the customer's billing timing. Change it if needed."
              : "Leave empty to use the default from Invoice settings, counted from the day it is issued."}
          </span>
          <FieldError message={e.dueDate} />
        </label>
        <ol className="space-y-3">
          {rows.map((r, i) => {
            const p = `line-${i}-`;
            return (
              <li key={i} className="space-y-2 rounded-2xl bg-white/70 p-3 ring-1 ring-charcoal/10">
                {r.existing ? (
                  <>
                    <input type="hidden" name={`${p}id`} value={r.id} />
                    <input type="hidden" name={`${p}kind`} value={r.kind} />
                    <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-charcoal/60">
                      Line {i + 1}: {INVOICE_LINE_KIND_LABELS[r.kind]}
                    </p>
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-charcoal">
                    New line
                    <select
                      name={`${p}kind`}
                      defaultValue={val(`${p}kind`, "SERVICE")}
                      className={`${inputClass} min-h-10`}
                    >
                      {NEW_LINE_KINDS.map((k) => (
                        <option key={k} value={k}>
                          {INVOICE_LINE_KIND_LABELS[k]}
                        </option>
                      ))}
                    </select>
                    <FieldError message={e[`${p}kind`]} />
                  </label>
                )}
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-charcoal">
                  Description
                  <input
                    name={`${p}description`}
                    maxLength={300}
                    defaultValue={val(`${p}description`, r.description)}
                    aria-invalid={e[`${p}description`] ? true : undefined}
                    className={`${inputClass} min-h-10`}
                  />
                  <FieldError message={e[`${p}description`]} />
                </label>
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-charcoal">
                  Amount (rand)
                  <input
                    name={`${p}amount`}
                    inputMode="decimal"
                    defaultValue={val(`${p}amount`, r.amount)}
                    aria-invalid={e[`${p}amount`] ? true : undefined}
                    className={`${inputClass} min-h-10`}
                  />
                  {!r.existing ? (
                    <span className="mt-1 block text-xs font-normal text-charcoal/60">
                      Only an adjustment can be negative, for example -100.
                    </span>
                  ) : null}
                  <FieldError message={e[`${p}amount`]} />
                </label>
                {r.existing ? (
                  <label className="flex min-h-10 items-center gap-3 text-sm text-charcoal">
                    <input
                      type="checkbox"
                      name={`${p}remove`}
                      defaultChecked={val(`${p}remove`, "") === "on"}
                      className="h-5 w-5 rounded border-charcoal/30"
                    />
                    Remove this line
                  </label>
                ) : null}
              </li>
            );
          })}
        </ol>
        <p className="text-xs text-charcoal/60">
          {empty === 0
            ? "This invoice has the most lines allowed."
            : "Need more lines? Save, and three new empty lines appear."}
        </p>
      </div>
      <FormMessage error={state?.error} />
      <button type="submit" disabled={pending} className={buttonClass}>
        {pending ? "Saving..." : "Save draft"}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: `apps/admin/src/app/(staff)/invoices/[id]/invoice-forms.tsx`**

```tsx
"use client";

import { useActionState } from "react";
import { buttonClass } from "@/components/auth-card";
import { dangerButtonClass, FormMessage, TextAreaField } from "@/components/form";
import { issueInvoiceAction, voidInvoiceAction } from "../actions";

/** Issue one draft; each problem shows on its own line. */
export function IssueForm({ id, disabled }: { id: string; disabled: boolean }) {
  const [state, action, pending] = useActionState(issueInvoiceAction, undefined);
  return (
    <form action={action} className="space-y-3">
      <input type="hidden" name="id" value={id} />
      {state?.error ? (
        <p
          role="alert"
          className="whitespace-pre-line rounded-lg bg-red-50 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-red-800 ring-1 ring-red-200"
        >
          {state.error}
        </p>
      ) : null}
      <button type="submit" disabled={pending || disabled} className={buttonClass}>
        {pending ? "Issuing..." : "Issue and email"}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}

export function VoidForm({ id }: { id: string }) {
  const [state, action, pending] = useActionState(voidInvoiceAction, undefined);
  return (
    <form action={action} className="space-y-3">
      <input type="hidden" name="id" value={id} />
      <div key={state?.nonce ?? "initial"}>
        <TextAreaField
          label="Why is this invoice being voided?"
          name="reason"
          rows={2}
          required
          defaultValue={state?.values?.reason}
        />
      </div>
      <FormMessage error={state?.error} />
      <button type="submit" disabled={pending} className={dangerButtonClass}>
        {pending ? "Voiding..." : "Void this invoice"}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: `apps/admin/src/app/(staff)/invoices/[id]/page.tsx`**

```tsx
import {
  formatCentsForInput,
  formatCustomerNumber,
  formatDateSA,
  formatDateTimeSA,
  formatMonthLabel,
  formatZAR,
  fromDateString,
  getDb,
  getInvoice,
  INVOICE_ACTION_LABELS,
  invoiceSettingsStatus,
  listInvoiceActivity,
  loadInvoiceDocument,
  PAYMENT_REPORT_STATUS_LABELS,
} from "@ace/core";
import { InvoiceDocumentView } from "@ace/ui/invoice-document";
import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import { z } from "zod";
import { dangerButtonClass, secondaryButtonClass } from "@/components/form";
import { Notice, noticeFor } from "@/components/notice";
import { toActor } from "@/lib/actor";
import { requireStaff } from "@/lib/auth";
import { first } from "@/lib/search-params";
import { deleteInvoiceDraftAction, dismissReportAction, resendInvoiceEmailAction } from "../actions";
import { InvoiceStatusBadge } from "../status-badge";
import { DraftForm } from "./draft-form";
import { IssueForm, VoidForm } from "./invoice-forms";

const NOTICES = {
  saved: { text: "Draft saved.", ok: true },
  issued: { text: "Issued. The email goes out in the background: refresh in a moment to see the result.", ok: true },
  voided: { text: "Invoice voided.", ok: true },
  "email-queued": { text: "The email is being sent again. Refresh in a moment to see the result.", ok: true },
  dismissed: { text: "Report dismissed.", ok: true },
  cannot: {
    text: "That could not be done. The invoice may have changed: check it below and try again.",
    ok: false,
  },
};

const day = (value: Date | string) =>
  formatDateSA(typeof value === "string" ? fromDateString(value) : value);
/** 125050 -> "1250.50", -5050 -> "-50.50" (for an edit box). */
const amountForInput = (cents: number) =>
  cents < 0 ? `-${formatCentsForInput(-cents)}` : formatCentsForInput(cents);

function Section({ title, children }: { title: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <section className="space-y-3 rounded-2xl bg-white/70 p-4 ring-1 ring-charcoal/10">
      <h2 className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-forest">{title}</h2>
      {children}
    </section>
  );
}

export default async function InvoicePage({
  params,
  searchParams,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ id: string }>;
  searchParams: Promise<{ notice?: string | string[] }>;
}) {
  const staff = await requireStaff(["OWNER", "MANAGER"]); // every page starts with this
  const { id } = await params;
  if (!z.uuid().safeParse(id).success) notFound();
  const sp = await searchParams;
  const actor = await toActor(staff);
  const db = getDb();
  const inv = await getInvoice(db, actor, id);
  if (!inv) notFound();
  const [loaded, history, settings] = await Promise.all([
    loadInvoiceDocument(db, actor, id),
    listInvoiceActivity(db, actor, id),
    invoiceSettingsStatus(db, actor),
  ]);
  const anonymised = Boolean(inv.customer.anonymisedAt);
  const editable = inv.status === "DRAFT" && !anonymised;
  const waiting = inv.reports.filter((r) => r.status === "WAITING");
  const title = inv.reference ?? (inv.kind === "MONTHLY" ? "Draft monthly invoice" : "Draft invoice");

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl space-y-6">
      <Link href="/invoices" className="inline-flex min-h-10 items-center text-sm text-forest hover:underline">
        ← Invoices
      </Link>
      <div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-3">
          <h1 className="font-display text-2xl font-semibold text-forest">{title}</h1>
          <InvoiceStatusBadge status={inv.displayStatus} />
        </div>
        <p className="mt-1 break-words text-sm text-charcoal/70">
          For{" "}
          <Link href={`/customers/${inv.customer.id}?tab=invoices`} className="font-semibold text-forest underline">
            {inv.customer.displayName}
          </Link>{" "}
          {formatCustomerNumber(inv.customer.number)}
          {inv.billTo.id !== inv.customer.id ? `, billed to ${inv.billTo.displayName}` : ""}
          {inv.serviceMonthString ? `, service month ${formatMonthLabel(inv.serviceMonthString)}` : ""}
          {inv.sourceQuoteReference && inv.sourceQuoteId ? (
            <>
              , from quote{" "}
              <Link href={`/quotes/${inv.sourceQuoteId}`} className="text-forest underline">
                {inv.sourceQuoteReference}
              </Link>
            </>
          ) : null}
        </p>
      </div>
      <Notice notice={noticeFor(first(sp.notice), NOTICES)} />

      {editable ? (
        <>
          <Section title="Edit the draft">
            <DraftForm
              key={inv.draftVersion}
              id={inv.id}
              version={inv.draftVersion}
              monthly={inv.kind === "MONTHLY"}
              dueDate={inv.dueDateString ?? ""}
              lines={inv.lines.map((l) => ({
                id: l.id,
                kind: l.kind,
                description: l.description,
                amount: amountForInput(l.amountCents),
              }))}
            />
          </Section>
          <Section title="Issue">
            <p className="text-sm text-charcoal/70">
              Issuing gives the invoice its number, fixes the amounts, the address and the bank details, and
              emails it with the PDF to {inv.billTo.displayName}&apos;s contacts who receive invoices.
            </p>
            {!settings.bankReady ? (
              <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-red-800">
                ACE&apos;s bank details are not entered yet, so this cannot be issued.
              </p>
            ) : null}
            <IssueForm id={inv.id} disabled={!settings.bankReady} />
          </Section>
        </>
      ) : null}

      {inv.status === "DRAFT" ? (
        <details className="rounded-2xl bg-white/70 p-4 ring-1 ring-charcoal/10">
          <summary className="min-h-10 cursor-pointer py-2 font-display text-base font-semibold text-forest">
            Delete this draft
          </summary>
          <form action={deleteInvoiceDraftAction} className="mt-3 space-y-3">
            <input type="hidden" name="id" value={inv.id} />
            <input type="hidden" name="confirm" value="yes" />
            <p className="text-sm text-charcoal/70">
              {anonymised
                ? "This customer has been anonymised, so this draft can no longer be edited or issued."
                : "The draft is removed. A monthly draft can be made again by running the month again."}
            </p>
            <button type="submit" className={dangerButtonClass}>
              Yes, delete the draft
            </button>
          </form>
        </details>
      ) : null}

      {inv.status !== "DRAFT" ? (
        <Section title="Payment and email">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            <a href={`/invoices/${inv.id}/pdf`} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className={secondaryButtonClass}>
              Download PDF<span className="sr-only"> (opens in a new tab)</span>
            </a>
            {inv.status === "ISSUED" && inv.balanceCents > 0 ? (
              <Link href={`/payments/new?payer=${inv.billTo.id}&invoice=${inv.id}`} className={secondaryButtonClass}>
                Record payment
              </Link>
            ) : null}
          </div>
          <p className="text-sm">
            Total {formatZAR(inv.totalCents)}, paid {formatZAR(inv.allocatedCents)}
            {inv.status === "ISSUED" ? `, still owed ${formatZAR(Math.max(0, inv.balanceCents))}` : ""}
            {inv.dueDateString ? `, due ${day(inv.dueDateString)}` : ""}.
          </p>
          <p className="text-sm text-charcoal/70">
            {inv.emailStatus === "SENT" && inv.emailedAt
              ? `Emailed ${formatDateTimeSA(inv.emailedAt)}.`
              : inv.emailStatus === "FAILED"
                ? "The email could not be sent (nobody receives invoices, or the send failed). Check the contacts, then resend."
                : "Not emailed yet."}
          </p>
          {inv.status === "ISSUED" ? (
            <form action={resendInvoiceEmailAction}>
              <input type="hidden" name="id" value={inv.id} />
              <button type="submit" className={secondaryButtonClass}>
                Resend email
              </button>
            </form>
          ) : null}
          {inv.allocations.length > 0 ? (
            <ul className="divide-y divide-charcoal/10 text-sm">
              {inv.allocations.map((a) => (
                <li key={a.id} className="flex flex-wrap justify-between gap-2 py-2">
                  <Link href={`/payments/${a.payment.id}`} className="text-forest underline">
                    Payment of {day(a.payment.receivedOn)}
                  </Link>
                  <span>
                    {formatZAR(a.amountCents)}
                    {a.payment.voidedAt ? " (payment voided)" : ""}
                  </span>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          ) : null}
          {inv.status === "VOID" && inv.voidedAt ? (
            <p className="text-sm text-charcoal/70">
              Voided {formatDateTimeSA(inv.voidedAt)}
              {inv.voidReason ? `: ${inv.voidReason}` : ""}
            </p>
          ) : null}
          {inv.status === "ISSUED" && inv.allocatedCents === 0 ? (
            <details>
              <summary className="min-h-10 cursor-pointer py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-red-700">
                Void this invoice
              </summary>
              <div className="mt-2 space-y-2">
                <p className="text-sm text-charcoal/70">
                  A voided invoice is kept with its number but owes nothing. To correct it, void it and make a
                  new one.
                </p>
                <VoidForm id={inv.id} />
              </div>
            </details>
          ) : null}
        </Section>
      ) : null}

      {waiting.length > 0 ? (
        <Section title="Reported payments">
          <ul className="space-y-3 text-sm">
            {waiting.map((r) => (
              <li key={r.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
                <span className="min-w-0 break-words">
                  Paid on {day(r.paidOn)}
                  {r.reference ? `, reference ${r.reference}` : ""} ({PAYMENT_REPORT_STATUS_LABELS[r.status]})
                </span>
                <span className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                  <Link href={`/payments/new?report=${r.id}`} className={secondaryButtonClass}>
                    Confirm
                  </Link>
                  <form action={dismissReportAction}>
                    <input type="hidden" name="id" value={r.id} />
                    <input type="hidden" name="back" value={inv.id} />
                    <button type="submit" className={secondaryButtonClass}>
                      Dismiss
                    </button>
                  </form>
                </span>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </Section>
      ) : null}

      {loaded ? <InvoiceDocumentView doc={loaded.doc} /> : null}

      <Section title="History">
        {history.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-charcoal/60">Nothing yet.</p>
        ) : (
          <ul className="space-y-1 text-sm">
            {history.map((h) => (
              <li key={h.id}>
                {formatDateTimeSA(h.at)}: {INVOICE_ACTION_LABELS[h.action] ?? h.action}, {h.actorName}
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        )}
      </Section>
    </div>
  );
}
```

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/admin/src/app/(staff)/payments/actions.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (`@ace/core`): `recordPayment`, `allocatePayment`, `unallocatePayment`, `voidPayment`, `paymentInputFromForm`, `allocationFormSchema`, `INVOICE_MESSAGES`, `getPayment`, `listPayments`, `openInvoicesForPayer`, `listPaymentReports`, `getPaymentReport`, `getCustomer`, `listCustomers`, `loadStatementDocument`, `defaultStatementPeriod`, `statementPeriodProblem`, `PAYMENT_REPORT_STATUS_LABELS`, `formatCustomerNumber`, `formatZAR`, `formatCentsForInput`, `formatDateSA`, `fromDateString`, `todaySA`, `DomainError`, `ForbiddenError`, `inTx`, `getDb`, `MANAGEMENT`, type `OpenInvoice`; (`@ace/core/client` in the client form): `formatCentsForInput`, `formatZAR`, `parseRandToCents`, `suggestAllocations`; (`@ace/ui/invoice-document`): `StatementDocumentView`; Task 14/15: `Notice`, `noticeFor`, `dismissReportAction` (from `../../invoices/actions`); staff app: `requireStaff`, `toActor`, `failure`, `formValues`, `FormState`, `first`, `positiveInt`, `inputClass`, `buttonClass`, `secondaryButtonClass`, `dangerButtonClass`, `FormMessage`.
- Produces: `recordPaymentAction(prev, formData): Promise<FormState>`, `allocateAction(formData)`, `unallocateAction(formData)`, `voidPaymentAction(formData)` (`payments/actions.ts`); `PaymentForm({ payerId, reportId, open, initial, today })` with types `OpenRow` and `PaymentInitial` (client); `CustomerSearchForm({ basePath, param, q, items })` (`components/customer-search.tsx`, a server component that builds its result links from the plain strings `basePath` and `param`, so no function is passed between components).

Every page starts with `requireStaff(["OWNER", "MANAGER"])`; every action with `requireStaff(MANAGEMENT)`.

import { formatCustomerNumber } from "@ace/core";
import Link from "next/link";
import { inputClass } from "@/components/auth-card";
import { secondaryButtonClass } from "@/components/form";

/** Search for a customer; each result links to `<basePath>?<param>=<customer id>`. */
export function CustomerSearchForm({
  basePath,
  param,
  q,
  items,
}: {
  basePath: string;
  param: string;
  q: string;
  items: { id: string; number: number; displayName: string }[] | null;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      <form method="get" role="search" className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        <input
          name="q"
          defaultValue={q}
          maxLength={100}
          placeholder="Customer name, number or email"
          aria-label="Find a customer"
          className={`${inputClass} mt-0 flex-1 basis-64`}
        />
        <button type="submit" className={secondaryButtonClass}>
          Search
        </button>
      </form>
      {items ? (
        items.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-charcoal/60">No customer matches that search.</p>
        ) : (
          <ul className="divide-y divide-charcoal/10 rounded-2xl bg-white/70 ring-1 ring-charcoal/10">
            {items.map((c) => (
              <li key={c.id}>
                <Link
                  href={`${basePath}?${param}=${c.id}`}
                  className="flex min-h-10 flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 px-4 py-3 hover:bg-cream/60"
                >
                  <span className="min-w-0 break-words font-semibold text-forest">{c.displayName}</span>
                  <span className="text-xs text-charcoal/50">{formatCustomerNumber(c.number)}</span>
                </Link>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        )
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
