- [ ] **Step 2: `apps/admin/src/app/(staff)/payments/actions.ts`**

```ts
"use server";

import {
  allocatePayment,
  allocationFormSchema,
  DomainError,
  ForbiddenError,
  inTx,
  INVOICE_MESSAGES,
  MANAGEMENT,
  paymentInputFromForm,
  recordPayment,
  todaySA,
  unallocatePayment,
  voidPayment,
} from "@ace/core";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { z } from "zod";
import { toActor } from "@/lib/actor";
import { requireStaff } from "@/lib/auth";
import { failure, formValues, type FormState } from "@/lib/form";

const idSchema = z.uuid();

/** A refusal as a fixed notice code; a refused role goes to /forbidden; anything unexpected is re-thrown. */
function codeFor(error: unknown): string {
  if (error instanceof ForbiddenError) redirect("/forbidden");
  if (!(error instanceof DomainError)) throw error;
  if (error.message === INVOICE_MESSAGES.overBalance) return "over-balance";
  if (error.message === INVOICE_MESSAGES.overPayment) return "over-payment";
  if (error.message === INVOICE_MESSAGES.paymentVoided) return "voided-already";
  return "cannot";
}

export async function recordPaymentAction(_prev: FormState, formData: FormData): Promise<FormState> {
  const staff = await requireStaff(MANAGEMENT);
  const actor = await toActor(staff);
  const values = formValues(formData);
  const parsed = paymentInputFromForm(values, todaySA());
  if (!parsed.ok) {
    const { form, ...fieldErrors } = parsed.fieldErrors;
    return { error: form ?? "Check the boxes marked below.", fieldErrors, values, nonce: Date.now() };
  }
  let id: string;
  try {
    id = (await inTx((tx) => recordPayment(tx, actor, parsed.input))).id;
  } catch (error) {
    return failure(error, values);
  }
  redirect(`/payments/${id}?notice=recorded`);
}

export async function allocateAction(formData: FormData): Promise<void> {
  const staff = await requireStaff(MANAGEMENT);
  const actor = await toActor(staff);
  const paymentId = idSchema.safeParse(formData.get("paymentId"));
  if (!paymentId.success) redirect("/payments");
  const parsed = allocationFormSchema.safeParse({
    paymentId: formData.get("paymentId"),
    invoiceId: formData.get("invoiceId"),
    amount: formData.get("amount") ?? "",
  });
  if (!parsed.success) redirect(`/payments/${paymentId.data}?notice=amount`);
  let code = "allocated";
  try {
    await inTx((tx) => allocatePayment(tx, actor, parsed.data));
  } catch (error) {
    code = codeFor(error);
  }
  redirect(`/payments/${paymentId.data}?notice=${code}`);
}

export async function unallocateAction(formData: FormData): Promise<void> {
  const staff = await requireStaff(MANAGEMENT);
  const actor = await toActor(staff);
  const paymentId = idSchema.safeParse(formData.get("paymentId"));
  const allocationId = idSchema.safeParse(formData.get("allocationId"));
  if (!paymentId.success) redirect("/payments");
  if (!allocationId.success) redirect(`/payments/${paymentId.data}?notice=cannot`);
  let code = "unallocated";
  try {
    await inTx((tx) => unallocatePayment(tx, actor, allocationId.data));
  } catch (error) {
    code = codeFor(error);
  }
  redirect(`/payments/${paymentId.data}?notice=${code}`);
}

export async function voidPaymentAction(formData: FormData): Promise<void> {
  const staff = await requireStaff(MANAGEMENT);
  const actor = await toActor(staff);
  const paymentId = idSchema.safeParse(formData.get("paymentId"));
  if (!paymentId.success) redirect("/payments");
  const reason = String(formData.get("reason") ?? "").trim();
  if (reason === "") redirect(`/payments/${paymentId.data}?notice=void-reason`);
  let code = "voided";
  try {
    await inTx((tx) => voidPayment(tx, actor, paymentId.data, reason));
  } catch (error) {
    code = codeFor(error);
  }
  redirect(`/payments/${paymentId.data}?notice=${code}`);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `apps/admin/src/app/(staff)/payments/page.tsx`**

```tsx
import { formatDateSA, formatZAR, fromDateString, getDb, listPaymentReports, listPayments } from "@ace/core";
import Link from "next/link";
import { secondaryButtonClass } from "@/components/form";
import { toActor } from "@/lib/actor";
import { requireStaff } from "@/lib/auth";
import { positiveInt } from "@/lib/search-params";

const day = (value: string) => formatDateSA(fromDateString(value));

export default async function PaymentsPage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<{ page?: string | string[] }>;
}) {
  const staff = await requireStaff(["OWNER", "MANAGER"]); // every page starts with this
  const sp = await searchParams;
  const actor = await toActor(staff);
  const db = getDb();
  const page = positiveInt(sp.page, 10_000) ?? 1;
  const [result, waiting] = await Promise.all([listPayments(db, actor, { page }), listPaymentReports(db, actor)]);
  const pages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(result.total / result.pageSize));
  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <h1 className="font-display text-2xl font-semibold text-forest">Payments</h1>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <Link href="/payments/reports" className={secondaryButtonClass}>
            Reported payments ({waiting.length} waiting)
          </Link>
          <Link
            href="/payments/new"
            className="inline-flex min-h-10 items-center rounded-full bg-forest px-5 py-2.5 font-display text-sm font-semibold text-cream hover:bg-forest-deep"
          >
            Record a payment
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>
      {result.items.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="text-center text-charcoal/60">No payments recorded yet.</p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="divide-y divide-charcoal/10 rounded-2xl bg-white/70 ring-1 ring-charcoal/10">
          {result.items.map((p) => (
            <li key={p.id}>
              <Link href={`/payments/${p.id}`} className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 px-4 py-3 hover:bg-cream/60">
                <span className="min-w-0 break-words">
                  <span className="font-semibold text-forest">{formatZAR(p.amountCents)}</span>
                  <span className="ml-2 text-sm">{p.payer.displayName}</span>
                  <span className="block text-sm text-charcoal/60">
                    {[day(p.receivedOn), p.reference, p.unallocatedCents > 0 ? `${formatZAR(p.unallocatedCents)} not allocated` : null]
                      .filter(Boolean)
                      .join(", ")}
                  </span>
                </span>
                {p.voided ? (
                  <span className="rounded-full bg-charcoal/10 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-charcoal/50">Void</span>
                ) : null}
              </Link>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
      {pages > 1 ? (
        <nav className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm" aria-label="Pages">
          {page > 1 ? (
            <Link href={`/payments?page=${page - 1}`} className="inline-flex min-h-10 items-center px-2 text-forest hover:underline">
              Previous
            </Link>
          ) : (
            <span />
          )}
          <span className="text-charcoal/60">
            Page {page} of {pages}
          </span>
          {page < pages ? (
            <Link href={`/payments?page=${page + 1}`} className="inline-flex min-h-10 items-center px-2 text-forest hover:underline">
              Next
            </Link>
          ) : (
            <span />
          )}
        </nav>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `apps/admin/src/app/(staff)/payments/new/payment-form.tsx`**

```tsx
"use client";

import { formatCentsForInput, formatZAR, parseRandToCents, suggestAllocations } from "@ace/core/client";
import { useActionState, useState } from "react";
import { buttonClass, inputClass } from "@/components/auth-card";
import { FormMessage, secondaryButtonClass } from "@/components/form";
import { recordPaymentAction } from "../actions";

export type OpenRow = {
  id: string;
  reference: string;
  forName: string | null;
  dueDate: string;
  dueLabel: string;
  balanceCents: number;
};

export type PaymentInitial = {
  receivedOn: string;
  amount: string;
  reference: string;
  allocations: Record<string, string>;
};

function FieldError({ message }: { message: string | undefined }) {
  return message ? (
    <span role="alert" className="mt-1 block text-xs font-normal text-red-700">
      {message}
    </span>
  ) : null;
}

export function PaymentForm({
  payerId,
  reportId,
  open,
  initial,
  today,
}: {
  payerId: string;
  reportId: string | null;
  open: OpenRow[];
  initial: PaymentInitial;
  today: string;
}) {
  const [state, action, pending] = useActionState(recordPaymentAction, undefined);
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(initial.amount);
  const [alloc, setAlloc] = useState<Record<string, string>>(initial.allocations);
  const e = state?.fieldErrors ?? {};
  const typed = state?.values;
  const amountCents = parseRandToCents(amount) ?? 0;
  const allocatedCents = Object.values(alloc).reduce((t, v) => t + (parseRandToCents(v) ?? 0), 0);
  const left = amountCents - allocatedCents;

  const fillOldest = () => {
    const next: Record<string, string> = {};
    for (const a of suggestAllocations(
      amountCents,
      open.map((o) => ({ id: o.id, balanceCents: o.balanceCents, dueDate: o.dueDate, reference: o.reference })),
    )) {
      next[a.invoiceId] = formatCentsForInput(a.amountCents);
    }
    setAlloc(next);
  };

  return (
    <form action={action} className="space-y-4">
      <input type="hidden" name="payerCustomerId" value={payerId} />
      <input type="hidden" name="reportId" value={reportId ?? ""} />
      <div key={state?.nonce ?? "initial"} className="space-y-4">
        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-charcoal">
          Date the money arrived
          <input
            type="date"
            name="receivedOn"
            required
            max={today}
            defaultValue={typed?.receivedOn ?? initial.receivedOn}
            aria-invalid={e.receivedOn ? true : undefined}
            className={`${inputClass} min-h-10`}
          />
          <FieldError message={e.receivedOn} />
        </label>
        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-charcoal">
          Bank reference (optional)
          <input
            name="reference"
            maxLength={100}
            defaultValue={typed?.reference ?? initial.reference}
            className={`${inputClass} min-h-10`}
          />
          <FieldError message={e.reference} />
        </label>
        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-charcoal">
          Note (optional)
          <textarea name="note" rows={2} maxLength={500} defaultValue={typed?.note ?? ""} className={inputClass} />
          <FieldError message={e.note} />
        </label>
      </div>
      <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-charcoal">
        Amount received (rand)
        <input
          name="amount"
          inputMode="decimal"
          required
          value={amount}
          onChange={(ev) => setAmount(ev.target.value)}
          aria-invalid={e.amount ? true : undefined}
          className={`${inputClass} min-h-10`}
        />
        <FieldError message={e.amount} />
      </label>
      <fieldset className="space-y-3 rounded-2xl bg-white/70 p-4 ring-1 ring-charcoal/10">
        <legend className="px-1 font-display text-base font-semibold text-forest">Allocate to invoices</legend>
        {open.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-charcoal/60">Nothing is owed: the whole payment becomes credit on account.</p>
        ) : (
          <>
            <button type="button" onClick={fillOldest} className={secondaryButtonClass}>
              Fill oldest first
            </button>
            {open.map((o) => (
              <label key={o.id} className="block text-sm font-medium text-charcoal">
                {o.reference}
                {o.forName ? `, ${o.forName}` : ""}: {formatZAR(o.balanceCents)} owed, due {o.dueLabel}
                <input
                  name={`alloc-${o.id}`}
                  inputMode="decimal"
                  value={alloc[o.id] ?? ""}
                  onChange={(ev) => setAlloc({ ...alloc, [o.id]: ev.target.value })}
                  aria-invalid={e[`alloc-${o.id}`] ? true : undefined}
                  className={`${inputClass} min-h-10`}
                />
                <FieldError message={e[`alloc-${o.id}`]} />
              </label>
            ))}
          </>
        )}
        <p className="text-sm text-charcoal/70">
          Allocated {formatZAR(allocatedCents)} of {formatZAR(amountCents)}.
          {left > 0 ? ` ${formatZAR(left)} stays as credit on account.` : ""}
          {left < 0 ? " That is more than the payment." : ""}
        </p>
      </fieldset>
      <FormMessage error={state?.error} />
      <button type="submit" disabled={pending} className={buttonClass}>
        {pending ? "Recording..." : "Record payment"}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: `apps/admin/src/app/(staff)/payments/new/page.tsx`**

```tsx
import {
  formatCentsForInput,
  formatDateSA,
  fromDateString,
  getCustomer,
  getDb,
  getPaymentReport,
  listCustomers,
  openInvoicesForPayer,
  todaySA,
} from "@ace/core";
import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import { z } from "zod";
import { CustomerSearchForm } from "@/components/customer-search";
import { toActor } from "@/lib/actor";
import { requireStaff } from "@/lib/auth";
import { first } from "@/lib/search-params";
import { PaymentForm } from "./payment-form";

type Param = string | string[] | undefined;

export default async function NewPaymentPage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<{ payer?: Param; report?: Param; invoice?: Param; q?: Param }>;
}) {
  const staff = await requireStaff(["OWNER", "MANAGER"]); // every page starts with this
  const sp = await searchParams;
  const actor = await toActor(staff);
  const db = getDb();
  const today = todaySA();
  const uuid = z.uuid();

  let payerId: string | null = null;
  let reportId: string | null = null;
  let prefillInvoice: string | null = null;
  const initial = { receivedOn: today, amount: "", reference: "", allocations: {} as Record<string, string> };

  const reportParam = uuid.safeParse(first(sp.report));
  if (reportParam.success) {
    const report = await getPaymentReport(db, actor, reportParam.data);
    if (!report || report.status !== "WAITING") {
      return (
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-2xl space-y-4">
          <h1 className="font-display text-2xl font-semibold text-forest">Record a payment</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-charcoal/70">This report has already been dealt with, or it could not be found.</p>
          <Link href="/payments/reports" className="text-forest underline">
            Back to reported payments
          </Link>
        </div>
      );
    }
    payerId = report.billToCustomerId;
    reportId = report.id;
    prefillInvoice = report.invoiceId;
    initial.receivedOn = report.paidOn <= today ? report.paidOn : today;
    initial.reference = report.reference ?? "";
  } else {
    const payerParam = uuid.safeParse(first(sp.payer));
    if (payerParam.success) payerId = payerParam.data;
    const invoiceParam = uuid.safeParse(first(sp.invoice));
    if (invoiceParam.success) prefillInvoice = invoiceParam.data;
  }

  if (!payerId) {
    const q = (first(sp.q) ?? "").trim().slice(0, 100);
    const found = q ? await listCustomers(db, actor, { q, pageSize: 20 }) : null;
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-2xl space-y-4">
        <Link href="/payments" className="inline-flex min-h-10 items-center text-sm text-forest hover:underline">
          ← Payments
        </Link>
        <h1 className="font-display text-2xl font-semibold text-forest">Record a payment</h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-charcoal/70">
          Find who paid. For a complex whose bills go to its managing agency, that is the agency.
        </p>
        <CustomerSearchForm basePath="/payments/new" param="payer" q={q} items={found ? found.items : null} />
      </div>
    );
  }

  const payer = await getCustomer(db, actor, payerId);
  if (!payer) notFound();
  if (payer.anonymisedAt) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-2xl space-y-4">
        <h1 className="font-display text-2xl font-semibold text-forest">Record a payment</h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-charcoal/70">This customer has been anonymised: no payment can be recorded.</p>
      </div>
    );
  }
  const open = await openInvoicesForPayer(db, actor, payerId);
  const target = open.find((o) => o.id === prefillInvoice);
  if (target) {
    initial.allocations = { [target.id]: formatCentsForInput(target.balanceCents) };
    initial.amount = formatCentsForInput(target.balanceCents);
  }
  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-2xl space-y-4">
      <Link
        href={`/customers/${payer.id}?tab=invoices`}
        className="inline-flex min-h-10 items-center text-sm text-forest hover:underline"
      >
        ← {payer.displayName}
      </Link>
      <h1 className="font-display text-2xl font-semibold text-forest">Record a payment</h1>
      <p className="text-sm text-charcoal/70">
        From {payer.displayName}.{reportId ? " The client reported this payment: check it is on the bank statement first." : ""}
      </p>
      <PaymentForm
        payerId={payer.id}
        reportId={reportId}
        today={today}
        initial={initial}
        open={open.map((o) => ({
          id: o.id,
          reference: o.reference,
          forName: o.forName,
          dueDate: o.dueDate,
          dueLabel: o.dueDate ? formatDateSA(fromDateString(o.dueDate)) : "",
          balanceCents: o.balanceCents,
        }))}
      />
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import {
  formatCustomerNumber,
  formatDateSA,
  formatDateTimeSA,
  formatZAR,
  fromDateString,
  getDb,
  getPayment,
  openInvoicesForPayer,
} from "@ace/core";
import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import { z } from "zod";
import { inputClass } from "@/components/auth-card";
import { dangerButtonClass, secondaryButtonClass } from "@/components/form";
import { Notice, noticeFor } from "@/components/notice";
import { toActor } from "@/lib/actor";
import { requireStaff } from "@/lib/auth";
import { first } from "@/lib/search-params";
import { allocateAction, unallocateAction, voidPaymentAction } from "../actions";

const NOTICES = {
  recorded: { text: "Payment recorded.", ok: true },
  allocated: { text: "Allocated.", ok: true },
  unallocated: { text: "Allocation removed. The money is credit on account again.", ok: true },
  voided: { text: "Payment voided. Its allocations no longer count.", ok: true },
  amount: { text: "Enter an amount above zero.", ok: false },
  "over-balance": { text: "That is more than the invoice still owes.", ok: false },
  "over-payment": { text: "That is more than is left of the payment.", ok: false },
  "voided-already": { text: "This payment has been voided.", ok: false },
  "void-reason": { text: "Say why the payment is being voided.", ok: false },
  cannot: { text: "That could not be done. Refresh the page and try again.", ok: false },
};

const day = (value: string) => formatDateSA(fromDateString(value));

export default async function PaymentPage({
  params,
  searchParams,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ id: string }>;
  searchParams: Promise<{ notice?: string | string[] }>;
}) {
  const staff = await requireStaff(["OWNER", "MANAGER"]); // every page starts with this
  const { id } = await params;
  if (!z.uuid().safeParse(id).success) notFound();
  const sp = await searchParams;
  const actor = await toActor(staff);
  const db = getDb();
  const p = await getPayment(db, actor, id);
  if (!p) notFound();
  const live = !p.voidedAt;
  const open = live && p.unallocatedCents > 0 ? await openInvoicesForPayer(db, actor, p.payer.id) : [];

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-2xl space-y-6">
      <Link href="/payments" className="inline-flex min-h-10 items-center text-sm text-forest hover:underline">
        ← Payments
      </Link>
      <div>
        <h1 className="font-display text-2xl font-semibold text-forest">Payment of {formatZAR(p.amountCents)}</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 break-words text-sm text-charcoal/70">
          From{" "}
          <Link href={`/customers/${p.payer.id}?tab=invoices`} className="font-semibold text-forest underline">
            {p.payer.displayName}
          </Link>{" "}
          {formatCustomerNumber(p.payer.number)}, received {day(p.receivedOn)}
          {p.reference ? `, reference ${p.reference}` : ""}
        </p>
        {p.note ? <p className="mt-1 break-words text-sm text-charcoal/70">Note: {p.note}</p> : null}
        {p.voidedAt ? (
          <p className="mt-2 rounded-lg bg-charcoal/10 px-3 py-2 text-sm">
            Voided {formatDateTimeSA(p.voidedAt)}
            {p.voidReason ? `: ${p.voidReason}` : ""}
          </p>
        ) : null}
      </div>
      <Notice notice={noticeFor(first(sp.notice), NOTICES)} />

      <section className="space-y-3 rounded-2xl bg-white/70 p-4 ring-1 ring-charcoal/10">
        <h2 className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-forest">Allocated to</h2>
        {p.allocations.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-charcoal/60">Not allocated to any invoice.</p>
        ) : (
          <ul className="divide-y divide-charcoal/10 text-sm">
            {p.allocations.map((a) => (
              <li key={a.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 py-2">
                <Link href={`/invoices/${a.invoiceId}`} className="min-w-0 break-words text-forest underline">
                  {a.reference ?? "Invoice"}
                  {a.forName ? `, ${a.forName}` : ""}
                </Link>
                <span className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  {formatZAR(a.amountCents)}
                  {live ? (
                    <form action={unallocateAction}>
                      <input type="hidden" name="paymentId" value={p.id} />
                      <input type="hidden" name="allocationId" value={a.id} />
                      <button type="submit" className="min-h-10 px-2 text-sm font-semibold text-red-700 underline">
                        Remove
                      </button>
                    </form>
                  ) : null}
                </span>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        )}
        <p className="text-sm">
          Not allocated: <span className="font-semibold">{formatZAR(p.unallocatedCents)}</span>
        </p>
      </section>

      {open.length > 0 ? (
        <section className="space-y-3 rounded-2xl bg-white/70 p-4 ring-1 ring-charcoal/10">
          <h2 className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-forest">Allocate the rest</h2>
          <form action={allocateAction} className="space-y-3">
            <input type="hidden" name="paymentId" value={p.id} />
            <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-charcoal">
              Invoice
              <select name="invoiceId" required className={`${inputClass} min-h-10`}>
                {open.map((o) => (
                  <option key={o.id} value={o.id}>
                    {o.reference}
                    {o.forName ? `, ${o.forName}` : ""}: {formatZAR(o.balanceCents)} owed
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </label>
            <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-charcoal">
              Amount (rand)
              <input name="amount" inputMode="decimal" required className={`${inputClass} min-h-10`} />
            </label>
            <button type="submit" className={secondaryButtonClass}>
              Allocate
            </button>
          </form>
        </section>
      ) : null}

      {live ? (
        <details className="rounded-2xl bg-white/70 p-4 ring-1 ring-charcoal/10">
          <summary className="min-h-10 cursor-pointer py-2 font-display text-base font-semibold text-red-700">
            Void this payment
          </summary>
          <form action={voidPaymentAction} className="mt-3 space-y-3">
            <input type="hidden" name="paymentId" value={p.id} />
            <p className="text-sm text-charcoal/70">
              For example a bounced EFT or a payment recorded by mistake. The invoices it paid owe the money
              again. This cannot be undone.
            </p>
            <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-charcoal">
              Why?
              <textarea name="reason" rows={2} maxLength={300} required className={inputClass} />
            </label>
            <button type="submit" className={dangerButtonClass}>
              Void the payment
            </button>
          </form>
        </details>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

import {
  formatDateSA,
  formatDateTimeSA,
  formatZAR,
  fromDateString,
  getDb,
  listPaymentReports,
  PAYMENT_REPORT_STATUS_LABELS,
} from "@ace/core";
import Link from "next/link";
import { secondaryButtonClass } from "@/components/form";
import { Notice, noticeFor } from "@/components/notice";
import { toActor } from "@/lib/actor";
import { requireStaff } from "@/lib/auth";
import { first } from "@/lib/search-params";
import { dismissReportAction } from "../../invoices/actions";

const NOTICES = {
  dismissed: { text: "Report dismissed.", ok: true },
  cannot: { text: "That could not be done. The report may already have been dealt with.", ok: false },
};

export default async function ReportedPaymentsPage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<{ show?: string | string[]; notice?: string | string[] }>;
}) {
  const staff = await requireStaff(["OWNER", "MANAGER"]); // every page starts with this
  const sp = await searchParams;
  const actor = await toActor(staff);
  const all = first(sp.show) === "all";
  const reports = await listPaymentReports(getDb(), actor, { status: all ? "ALL" : "WAITING" });
  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl space-y-6">
      <Link href="/payments" className="inline-flex min-h-10 items-center text-sm text-forest hover:underline">
        ← Payments
      </Link>
      <h1 className="font-display text-2xl font-semibold text-forest">Reported payments</h1>
      <p className="text-sm text-charcoal/70">
        Clients will be able to tell ACE &quot;I have paid&quot; from the client portal. Check each one against the
        bank statement, then confirm it (this records the payment) or dismiss it.
      </p>
      <Notice notice={noticeFor(first(sp.notice), NOTICES)} />
      <Link href={all ? "/payments/reports" : "/payments/reports?show=all"} className="inline-flex min-h-10 items-center text-sm text-forest underline">
        {all ? "Show waiting reports only" : "Show all reports"}
      </Link>
      {reports.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="text-charcoal/60">{all ? "No reports yet." : "No reports are waiting."}</p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="space-y-3">
          {reports.map((r) => (
            <li key={r.id} className="space-y-2 rounded-2xl bg-white/70 p-4 ring-1 ring-charcoal/10">
              <p className="break-words text-sm">
                <Link href={`/invoices/${r.invoice.id}`} className="font-semibold text-forest underline">
                  {r.invoice.reference ?? "Invoice"}
                </Link>{" "}
                ({formatZAR(r.invoice.totalCents ?? 0)}) for {r.invoice.customer.displayName}
                {r.invoice.billTo.id !== r.invoice.customer.id ? `, billed to ${r.invoice.billTo.displayName}` : ""}
              </p>
              <p className="break-words text-sm text-charcoal/70">
                Paid on {formatDateSA(fromDateString(r.paidOn))}
                {r.reference ? `, reference ${r.reference}` : ""}
                {r.reportedBy ? `, reported by ${r.reportedBy}` : ""}, {formatDateTimeSA(r.createdAt)}.{" "}
                {PAYMENT_REPORT_STATUS_LABELS[r.status]}
              </p>
              {r.status === "WAITING" ? (
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                  <Link href={`/payments/new?report=${r.id}`} className={secondaryButtonClass}>
                    Confirm
                  </Link>
                  <form action={dismissReportAction}>
                    <input type="hidden" name="id" value={r.id} />
                    <input type="hidden" name="back" value="reports" />
                    <button type="submit" className={secondaryButtonClass}>
                      Dismiss
                    </button>
                  </form>
                </div>
              ) : null}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

```tsx
import {
  defaultStatementPeriod,
  getDb,
  listCustomers,
  loadStatementDocument,
  statementPeriodProblem,
  todaySA,
} from "@ace/core";
import { StatementDocumentView } from "@ace/ui/invoice-document";
import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import { z } from "zod";
import { inputClass } from "@/components/auth-card";
import { CustomerSearchForm } from "@/components/customer-search";
import { secondaryButtonClass } from "@/components/form";
import { toActor } from "@/lib/actor";
import { requireStaff } from "@/lib/auth";
import { first } from "@/lib/search-params";

type Param = string | string[] | undefined;

export default async function StatementsPage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<{ customer?: Param; q?: Param; from?: Param; to?: Param }>;
}) {
  const staff = await requireStaff(["OWNER", "MANAGER"]); // every page starts with this
  const sp = await searchParams;
  const actor = await toActor(staff);
  const db = getDb();
  const picked = z.uuid().safeParse(first(sp.customer));

  if (!picked.success) {
    const q = (first(sp.q) ?? "").trim().slice(0, 100);
    const found = q ? await listCustomers(db, actor, { q, pageSize: 20 }) : null;
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-2xl space-y-4">
        <h1 className="font-display text-2xl font-semibold text-forest">Statements</h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-charcoal/70">
          A statement is for the customer who pays. For an agency it lists every complex billed to it.
        </p>
        <CustomerSearchForm basePath="/statements" param="customer" q={q} items={found ? found.items : null} />
      </div>
    );
  }

  const fallback = defaultStatementPeriod(todaySA());
  const from = first(sp.from) ?? fallback.from;
  const to = first(sp.to) ?? fallback.to;
  const problem = statementPeriodProblem(from, to);
  const loaded = problem ? null : await loadStatementDocument(db, actor, picked.data, { from, to });
  if (!problem && !loaded) notFound();

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl space-y-6">
      <Link href="/statements" className="inline-flex min-h-10 items-center text-sm text-forest hover:underline">
        ← Statements
      </Link>
      <form method="get" className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-2">
        <input type="hidden" name="customer" value={picked.data} />
        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-charcoal">
          From
          <input type="date" name="from" defaultValue={from} required className={`${inputClass} min-h-10`} />
        </label>
        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-charcoal">
          To
          <input type="date" name="to" defaultValue={to} required className={`${inputClass} min-h-10`} />
        </label>
        <button type="submit" className={secondaryButtonClass}>
          Show
        </button>
      </form>
      {problem ? (
        <p role="alert" className="rounded-lg bg-red-50 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-red-800 ring-1 ring-red-200">
          {problem}
        </p>
      ) : null}
      {loaded ? (
        <>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            <a
              href={`/statements/${picked.data}/pdf?from=${from}&to=${to}`}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              className={secondaryButtonClass}
            >
              Download PDF<span className="sr-only"> (opens in a new tab)</span>
            </a>
            <Link href={`/customers/${picked.data}?tab=invoices`} className={secondaryButtonClass}>
              Open the customer
            </Link>
          </div>
          <StatementDocumentView doc={loaded.doc} />
        </>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

(`from` and `to` in the PDF link are only ever valid dates here: `statementPeriodProblem` returned null for them.)

### Task 17: Documentation and the spec status line

**Files:**

- Modify: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-27-invoices-design.md` (status line only)

- [ ] **Step 1: `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`**
- In the roadmap table, change row 5's "Delivers" cell to `5a built (staff): Pre or Post billing, monthly run, EFT invoices with PDF and email, payments, statements; 5b (portal) next` and re-align the table (the "Delivers" column widens; keep every row's pipes lined up, prettier does this).
- Add a new section after the Module 4 section ("Client portal, sign-in and managing agencies (Module 4)", including its two sub-sections) and before "Working agreement":

```markdown
## Invoices, payments and statements (Module 5a, staff side)

- **Where the code lives.** `packages/core/src/invoices/`: small pure files with their own tests (`dates.ts`
  for run month, service month, due dates and part months; `money.ts` for totals, balances, statuses and
  allocation suggestions; `reference.ts`; `statement-math.ts`; `schemas.ts`), the shared helpers (`store.ts`:
  locks, allocated sums, audit lines) and the staff functions (`settings.ts`, `drafts.ts`, `run.ts`,
  `issue.ts`, `payments.ts`, `reports.ts`, `statements.ts`, `document.ts`, `emails.ts`, `activity.ts`), each
  taking `(store, actor, ...)` with the role check first. Invoice settings are owner only; everything else is
  owner and manager; crew never see invoices. The screens are `/invoices`, `/payments`, `/payments/reports`,
  `/statements`, `/settings/invoices` and the Invoices tab on the customer page.
- **Pre or Post.** Each customer has a Billing timing switch (default Pre). The run in month M bills Pre
  clients for month M+1 (due the last day of M) and Post clients for month M (due the 25th of M).
- **The run.** "Create this month's invoices" makes one DRAFT per active customer with a current monthly
  agreement, each customer in its own transaction. Lines come from the accepted quote (priced monthly lines
  and chosen monthly options, labelled with the property); a first month that starts after the 1st is charged
  by days; each overdue issued invoice without a fee yet gets a suggested late payment fee line (amount in
  settings, 0 switches it off). Running it twice makes no duplicates (a partial unique index guards it). It is
  one function, so a later automatic switch can call it.
- **Issue.** Gives the number `INV-<year>-<0001>` from an atomic yearly counter, freezes the recipient (the
  bill-to customer: the agency when the complex's bills go to it), ACE's bank and business details, the total
  and a fingerprint. The email (one per address of the payer's contacts who receive invoices, every PDF of
  that issue attached) goes after the response with `after()`; a failure never undoes the issue, is noted on
  the invoice and in its history, and Resend email sends it again. Only Draft, Issued and Void are stored;
  Unpaid, Part paid, Paid and Overdue are worked out from the balance and the due date (South African time).
- **Payments.** EFT only. Staff record the date, amount, bank reference and note, and allocate to the payer's
  issued invoices (oldest first by default); money not allocated is credit on account. A voided payment keeps
  its allocations as history but they stop counting. An invoice with live allocations cannot be voided.
- **Lock order** everywhere: customers (smaller id first), then invoices (by id), then the payment, then the
  payment report; every write re-reads and re-checks after the locks.
- **Reported payments.** The `payment_reports` table exists; stage 5b's portal "I have paid" writes it. Staff
  confirm (this records the payment in the same step) or dismiss.
- **Statements** are worked out on demand for the paying customer and a period (opening balance, invoices,
  payments, closing balance, credit, what is still owed, and a per-complex breakdown for an agency), on
  screen and as a PDF. Allocations have no date, so balances and credit are shown as they stand now.
- **Privacy.** Anonymising a customer keeps invoice numbers, dates, amounts and allocations, clears the frozen
  recipient, void reasons, payment references and notes and report references, replaces line wording with a
  fixed word, and deletes its drafts. The export includes its invoices, payments and reports (without ACE's
  bank snapshot). Audit lines hold ids, counts and fixed words only. The portal's database login has no
  access to any invoice table yet (stage 5b adds exactly what the portal needs).
- **Database tests** run one file at a time (`packages/core/scripts/test-db.mjs`, `--no-file-parallelism`):
  files that issue quote or invoice numbers hold the counter row lock for a whole rolled-back test, and in
  parallel they timed out. `ACE_DB_TEST_WORKERS=2` runs two files at once for measuring.

### Known limitations and follow-ups

- Annual escalation is manual: revise the accepted quote or edit the drafts.
- Only void-and-reissue corrects an issued invoice; there are no credit notes.
- The run does not notice a client who paused mid-month; the owner reviews the drafts.
- A Pre client whose service started before the first service month is not charged for that earlier part
  month automatically: the "First month" warning on the draft says to check it.
- Statements are on demand only and are not emailed; credit on account is shown but never refunded or
  applied automatically.
- There is no fully automatic run on the 20th yet (the run is one function, ready for a switch).

### Before real customer data goes in

- [ ] The owner enters ACE's bank details (bank, account holder, account number, branch code, account type)
      in Invoice settings before the first issue. Check the once-off due days (14) and the late fee (R100).
- [ ] The Module 4 email checklist applies: Resend domain, `RESEND_API_KEY` and `EMAIL_FROM` in the hosting
      settings of the staff app (it sends the invoices).
- [ ] Send one real invoice email with its PDF to an inbox you control and check it arrives (not in spam)
      and the PDF opens.
```

- [ ] **Database test timing** (Task 3, Step 3) measured before and after and reported to the owner.
- [ ] **Full checks:** `npm run db:generate`, `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint`, `npm run test`, then `npm run test:db` (dev database, rolled back; note the time). Every test passes, including the unchanged `quotes/portal-role.db.test.ts` frozen catalogue. A counter-row timeout in a file that issues numbers is the known flake class: re-run that file alone (`cd packages/core && ACE_DB_TESTS=1 npx vitest run <file>` with the dev settings loaded, or `npm run test:db` again) and report both results. Then `npm run build` with no dev server running.
- [ ] **Search for em dashes** in every file this branch added or changed that holds screen, email or PDF text: `git diff --name-only main... -- apps packages | xargs grep -n "—"` must print nothing (apart from the test assertions that check for its absence) from the new invoice files (older files that already had one are out of scope).
- [ ] **Real browser** (`npm run dev:admin` in Git Bash; made-up data only, names like "ZZ E2E Agency", "ZZ E2E Complex A/B", "ZZ E2E Post Client", emails `@example.com`; no `RESEND_API_KEY`, so emails print to the admin dev server console):
  1. As owner: `/settings/invoices`, enter made-up bank details (for example "ZZ Test Bank", account 1234567890, branch 250655), save. A manager account (if one exists) gets the forbidden page there; crew get the forbidden page on `/invoices`, `/payments`, `/statements` and the PDF routes.
  2. Make "ZZ E2E Post Client" with an accepted monthly quote (start date this month, one optional extra chosen); on its Invoices tab switch Billing timing to Post.
  3. Make "ZZ E2E Agency" and two complexes linked to it (Managed by, Bills go to the agency), each with an accepted monthly quote; one complex's start date after the 1st of next month's service month so its line is a part month.
  4. `/invoices`: run the current month. The notice counts the drafts; the drafts list shows the Post client (this month, due the 25th) and both complexes (next month, due the last day of this month, "Billed to the managing agency", "First month").
  5. Open a draft, change an amount, add an adjustment of -50, save (notice "Draft saved"), delete a line, save again. Try an amount of "abc": the message sits next to the box and nothing typed is lost.
  6. Issue the Post client's draft: it gets `INV-<year>-<n>`, the console shows the email with an "Attachments: INV-....pdf" line, the invoice page says "Emailed ..." after a refresh, the PDF opens (bank details, payment reference = the customer number, "not VAT registered", no em dashes).
  7. "Issue all drafts" for the two complexes: one email to the agency's address with both PDFs attached.
  8. Run the month again: no new drafts ("already invoiced").
  9. Record one payment from the agency covering both complex invoices ("Fill oldest first"), with some money left over: both invoices show Paid, the payment shows credit. Remove one allocation, allocate it again.
  10. Void an unpaid invoice with a reason (an invoice with a payment allocated is refused with the plain message). Void a payment: its invoices owe again.
  11. `/statements` for the agency, this month: invoices, the payment, credit, closing balance and the per-complex breakdown; open the PDF. The complex's own statement is empty.
  12. `/payments/reports` shows "No reports are waiting".
  13. Every page above at 375px: no sideways scroll, buttons at least 40px high.
  14. Overdue and late fee: if there is no overdue invoice to try it with, rely on the database test (`run.db.test.ts`); optionally create one with a past due date through a throwaway script against the dev database only with the owner's agreement.
- [ ] **No real `ace_portal` login check in 5a**: the portal role's rights did not change (the frozen catalogue test proves it). That check belongs to 5b.
- [ ] **Clean up** the made-up data created in the browser: void the made-up invoices and payments, archive the "ZZ E2E ..." customers (or anonymise them with the owner's agreement), and clear the made-up bank details in Invoice settings if the owner wants them empty again.
- [ ] Final whole-branch review, then tell the owner in plain words what was built, what was decided on their behalf (the "Decisions taken in this plan" list), and the go-live checklist (enter the bank details first).

## Self-review (done while writing this plan)

- **Spec coverage.** Pre or Post switch (Task 5, 14); owner-only settings with bank details, due days and late fee (Tasks 1, 5, 14); the 20th run with Pre and Post dates, review before sending, idempotent single function, skipped reasons, agency bill-to, pro-rata, optional lines and late-fee suggestions (Task 7); draft review, edit with the version guard, skip (delete) (Task 6, 15); issue with number, frozen recipient, address, bank details and total, contentHash, audit, emails after commit with try/catch and fixed phrases, issue-all per transaction (Tasks 8, 13, 15); once-off from an accepted quote or free lines with the default due date (Tasks 6, 8, 15); void rules and late fees freed by a void (Tasks 7, 8); payments, allocations with limits, credit on account, void payment, lock order (Task 9); reported payments table, screen, confirm and dismiss (Tasks 1, 9, 15, 16); statements per bill-to with per-complex breakdown and PDF (Tasks 4, 10, 11, 16); anonymise and export (Task 12); privacy coverage and DB protections including the partial unique index (Task 1); email attachments with limits and fixed subjects (Task 13); `@ace/ui` documents and PDFs (Task 11); every staff page and action starts with `requireStaff`, crew never (Tasks 14 to 16); docs and go-live (Task 17). Stage 5b items (portal pages, "I have paid" button, `ace_portal` grants) are deliberately absent.
- **Placeholders.** None: every step has the code or the exact edit and the exact command.
- **Names and types.** Checked across tasks: `InvoiceStore`, `INVOICE_MESSAGES`, `InvoiceDraftConflictError`, `InvoiceIssueError`, `lockCustomersInOrder`, `lockInvoiceRows`, `lockPaymentRow`, `lockReportRow`, `allocatedByInvoice`, `allocatedByPayment`, `billToIdFor`, `isMonthlyDuplicate`, `logInvoiceEvent`, `logPaymentEvent` (Task 5); `readInvoiceSettings`, `buildInvoiceSnapshot`, `bankDetailsReady` (Task 5, used in 7, 8, 11, 13); `draftInputFromForm`, `paymentInputFromForm`, `allocationFormSchema`, `invoiceSettingsFormSchema`, `invoiceSnapshotSchema`, `reasonSchema`, `runMonthSchema`, `billingTimingSchema` (Task 5, used in 8, 9, 14 to 16); `createMonthlyDrafts(store, actor, runMonth, { run, only?, today? })` (Task 7, used in 15); `issueAllDrafts(store, actor, { run, only? })` (Task 8, used in 15); `emailIssuedInvoices` and `checkInvoiceEmailable` (Task 13, used in 14, 15); `loadInvoiceDocument`, `loadStatementDocument` (Task 11, used in 13 via the app, 14 to 16); the admin `emailInvoicesLater(actor, ids)` (Task 14, used in 15). `TxRunner` is the existing type from `portal/access.ts`; `inTx` satisfies it.
- **Barrels.** Role-less internals (`readInvoiceSettings`, `buildInvoiceSnapshot`, `allocateInvoiceNumber`, the lock and sum helpers, `scrubInvoicesForAnonymise`, `invoiceExportFor`, `MONTHLY_AGREEMENT`, `ONCE_OFF_ACCEPTED`) are not exported from `invoices/index.ts`.
